# 3D Gaussian Splatting 论文列表

## November 2025

### [1] GFix: Perceptually Enhanced Gaussian Splatting Video Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2025-11-10  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Siyue Teng, Ge Gao, Duolikun Danier et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06953v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enhances 3D scene reconstruction through explicit representation and fast rendering, demonstrating potential benefits for various low-level vision tasks, including video compression. However, existing 3DGS-based video codecs generally exhibit more noticeable visual artifacts and relatively low compression ratios. In this paper, we specifically target the perceptual enhancement of 3DGS-based video compression, based on the assumption that artifacts from 3DGS rendering and quantization resemble noisy latents sampled during diffusion training. Building on this premise, we propose a content-adaptive framework, GFix, comprising a streamlined, single-step diffusion model that serves as an off-the-shelf neural enhancer. Moreover, to increase compression efficiency, We propose a modulated LoRA scheme that freezes the low-rank decompositions and modulates the intermediate hidden states, thereby achieving efficient adaptation of the diffusion backbone with highly compressible updates. Experimental results show that GFix delivers strong perceptual quality enhancement, outperforming GSVC with up to 72.1% BD-rate savings in LPIPS and 21.4% in FID.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射（3DGS）通过显式表示和快速渲染增强了3D场景重建，展示了对各种低层视觉任务（包括视频压缩）的潜在优势。然而，现有基于3DGS的视频编解码器通常表现出更明显的视觉伪影和相对较低的压缩比。本文特别针对基于3DGS的视频压缩的感知增强，基于3DGS渲染和量化产生的伪影类似于扩散训练中采样的噪声潜源。基于这一前提，我们提出了一个内容自适应框架GFix，包含一个简化的单步扩散模型，作为现成的神经增强器。此外，为提高压缩效率，我们提出了一种调制LoRA方案，冻结低秩分解并调制中间隐藏态，从而实现高度可压缩更新的扩散主干的高效适应。实验结果显示，GFix在感知质量方面表现强劲，在LPIPS中节省高达72.1%的BD率，FID节省21.4%，优于GSVC。  

### [2] FastGS: Training 3D Gaussian Splatting in 100 Seconds  
- **⏳发布**：2025-11-06  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shiwei Ren, Tianci Wen, Yongchun Fang, Biao Lu  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://fastgs.github.io/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04283v1)   · [Project](https://fastgs.github.io/)  
- **📝摘要**：The dominant 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) acceleration methods fail to properly regulate the number of Gaussians during training, causing redundant computational time overhead. In this paper, we propose FastGS, a novel, simple, and general acceleration framework that fully considers the importance of each Gaussian based on multi-view consistency, efficiently solving the trade-off between training time and rendering quality. We innovatively design a densification and pruning strategy based on multi-view consistency, dispensing with the budgeting mechanism. Extensive experiments on Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks & Temples, and Deep Blending datasets demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in training speed, achieving a 3.32$\times$ training acceleration and comparable rendering quality compared with DashGaussian on the Mip-NeRF 360 dataset and a 15.45$\times$ acceleration compared with vanilla 3DGS on the Deep Blending dataset. We demonstrate that FastGS exhibits strong generality, delivering 2-7$\times$ training acceleration across various tasks, including dynamic scene reconstruction, surface reconstruction, sparse-view reconstruction, large-scale reconstruction, and simultaneous localization and mapping. The project page is available at https://fastgs.github.io/  
- **📝翻译**: 主流的三维高斯喷射（3DGS）加速度方法在训练过程中无法有效调节高斯分布量，导致计算时间冗余。本文提出了FastGS，一种新颖、简单且通用的加速框架，充分考虑每个高斯分布在多视角一致性基础上的重要性，高效解决训练时间与渲染质量之间的权衡。我们创新地设计了基于多视图一致性的密度化和修剪策略，摒弃了预算机制。在 Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks & Temples 和 Deep Blending 数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在训练速度上显著优于最先进方法，与 Mip-NeRF 360 数据集上的 DashGaussian 相比，训练加速达到 3.32 美元/时间美元，渲染质量相当;在深度混合数据集上，与原版 3DGS 相比，加速为 15.45 美元\时间美元。我们证明了FastGS表现出强烈的泛化性，在包括动态场景重建、表面重建、稀疏视野重建、大规模重建以及同时定位与映射等多个任务中，实现了2-7美元×每小时的训练加速。项目页面可在 https://fastgs.github.io/  


## October 2025

### [1] Gen-LangSplat: Generalized Language Gaussian Splatting with Pre-Trained Feature Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-27  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Pranav Saxena  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22930v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Modeling open-vocabulary language fields in 3D is essential for intuitive human-AI interaction and querying within physical environments. State-of-the-art approaches, such as LangSplat, leverage 3D Gaussian Splatting to efficiently construct these language fields, encoding features distilled from high-dimensional models like CLIP. However, this efficiency is currently offset by the requirement to train a scene-specific language autoencoder for feature compression, introducing a costly, per-scene optimization bottleneck that hinders deployment scalability. In this work, we introduce Gen-LangSplat, that eliminates this requirement by replacing the scene-wise autoencoder with a generalized autoencoder, pre-trained extensively on the large-scale ScanNet dataset. This architectural shift enables the use of a fixed, compact latent space for language features across any new scene without any scene-specific training. By removing this dependency, our entire language field construction process achieves a efficiency boost while delivering querying performance comparable to, or exceeding, the original LangSplat method. To validate our design choice, we perform a thorough ablation study empirically determining the optimal latent embedding dimension and quantifying representational fidelity using Mean Squared Error and cosine similarity between the original and reprojected 512-dimensional CLIP embeddings. Our results demonstrate that generalized embeddings can efficiently and accurately support open-vocabulary querying in novel 3D scenes, paving the way for scalable, real-time interactive 3D AI applications.  
- **📝翻译**: 在三维中建模开放词汇语言字段对于在物理环境中实现直观的人机交互和查询至关重要。最先进的方法，如LangSplat，利用三维高斯Splatting高效构建这些语言场，编码源自CLIP等高维模型的特征。然而，目前这一效率被需要训练场景特定语言的特征压缩自编码器所抵消，这带来了成本高昂的每场景优化瓶颈，阻碍了部署的可扩展性。在本研究中，我们引入了Gen-LangSplat，通过用广义自编码器替换场景层次的自编码器，并广泛预先在大规模ScanNet数据集上训练，从而消除了这一需求。这种架构转变使得在任何新场景中，无需特定场景培训即可使用固定、紧凑的潜在空间来表达语言特征。通过消除这种依赖，我们整个语言字段构建过程在查询性能上达到或与原始 LangSplat 方法相当甚至超越的同时，实现了效率的提升。为验证设计选择，我们进行了全面的消融研究，实证确定最佳潜在嵌入维度，并利用均方误差和原始与重投影的512维CLIP嵌入之间的余弦相似度量化表示真实度。我们的结果表明，泛化嵌入能够高效且准确地支持新颖三维场景中的开放词汇查询，为可扩展的实时交互式3D AI应用铺平了道路。  

### [2] Region-Adaptive Learned Hierarchical Encoding for 3D Gaussian Splatting Data  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-26  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shashank N. Sridhara, Birendra Kathariya, Fangjun Pu et al.  
- **📝说明**：10 Pages, 5 Figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22812v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We introduce Region-Adaptive Learned Hierarchical Encoding (RALHE) for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) data. While 3DGS has recently become popular for novel view synthesis, the size of trained models limits its deployment in bandwidth-constrained applications such as volumetric media streaming. To address this, we propose a learned hierarchical latent representation that builds upon the principles of "overfitted" learned image compression (e.g., Cool-Chic and C3) to efficiently encode 3DGS attributes. Unlike images, 3DGS data have irregular spatial distributions of Gaussians (geometry) and consist of multiple attributes (signals) defined on the irregular geometry. Our codec is designed to account for these differences between images and 3DGS. Specifically, we leverage the octree structure of the voxelized 3DGS geometry to obtain a hierarchical multi-resolution representation. Our approach overfits latents to each Gaussian attribute under a global rate constraint. These latents are decoded independently through a lightweight decoder network. To estimate the bitrate during training, we employ an autoregressive probability model that leverages octree-derived contexts from the 3D point structure. The multi-resolution latents, decoder, and autoregressive entropy coding networks are jointly optimized for each Gaussian attribute. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed RALHE compression framework achieves a rendering PSNR gain of up to 2dB at low bitrates (less than 1 MB) compared to the baseline 3DGS compression methods.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们引入了区域自适应学习分层编码（RALHE）技术，用于3D高斯溅射（3DGS）数据。虽然3DGS近年来在新颖的视图合成领域变得流行，但训练模型的规模限制了其在带宽受限的应用中，如体积媒体流的应用。为此，我们提出了一种学习式层级潜在表示，基于“过拟合”学习图像压缩（如Cool-Chic和C3）的原则，高效编码3DGS属性。与图像不同，3DGS数据具有高斯分布（几何）的不规则空间分布，并且由定义在不规则几何上的多个属性（信号）组成。我们的编解码器旨在考虑图像与3DGS之间的这些差异。具体来说，我们利用体素化3DGS几何的八叉树结构，获得分层多分辨率表示。我们的方法在全局速率约束下对每个高斯属性的潜在变量进行过拟合。这些潜伏通过轻量级解码器网络独立解码。为了估计训练中的比特率，我们采用了利用三维点结构中八叉树上下文的自回归概率模型。多分辨率潜伏、解码器和自回归熵编码网络均针对每个高斯属性共同优化。实验表明，所提出的RALHE压缩框架在低比特率（小于1 MB）下，相比基础3DGS压缩方法，实现了高达2dB的渲染PSNR增益。  

### [3] MoE-GS: Mixture of Experts for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-22  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：In-Hwan Jin, Hyeongju Mun, Joonsoo Kim, Kugjin Yun, Kyeongbo Kong  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19210v1)   · [Project](https://anonymous.4open.science/w/MoE-GS-68BA/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Recent advances in dynamic scene reconstruction have significantly benefited from 3D Gaussian Splatting, yet existing methods show inconsistent performance across diverse scenes, indicating no single approach effectively handles all dynamic challenges. To overcome these limitations, we propose Mixture of Experts for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting (MoE-GS), a unified framework integrating multiple specialized experts via a novel Volume-aware Pixel Router. Our router adaptively blends expert outputs by projecting volumetric Gaussian-level weights into pixel space through differentiable weight splatting, ensuring spatially and temporally coherent results. Although MoE-GS improves rendering quality, the increased model capacity and reduced FPS are inherent to the MoE architecture. To mitigate this, we explore two complementary directions: (1) single-pass multi-expert rendering and gate-aware Gaussian pruning, which improve efficiency within the MoE framework, and (2) a distillation strategy that transfers MoE performance to individual experts, enabling lightweight deployment without architectural changes. To the best of our knowledge, MoE-GS is the first approach incorporating Mixture-of-Experts techniques into dynamic Gaussian splatting. Extensive experiments on the N3V and Technicolor datasets demonstrate that MoE-GS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with improved efficiency. Video demonstrations are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/w/MoE-GS-68BA/.  
- **📝翻译**: 动态场景重建的最新进展显著受益于三维高斯喷溅，但现有方法在不同场景中表现不一致，表明没有单一方法能有效解决所有动态挑战。为克服这些限制，我们提出了动态高斯喷溅专家混合（MoE-GS），这是一个通过新型体积感知像素路由器整合多位专业专家的统一框架。我们的路由器通过可微分权重喷射，将体积高斯级权重投影到像素空间中，实现专家输出的自适应混合，确保空间和时间上的连贯结果。尽管MoE-GS提升了渲染质量，但模型容量的提升和帧率的降低是MoE架构固有的问题。为缓解这一问题，我们探讨了两种互补方向：（1）单次多专家渲染和门感知高斯剪枝，提升MoE框架内的效率;（2）将MoE性能转移给个别专家的提纯策略，实现轻量级部署而无需架构变更。据我们所知，MoE-GS是首个将专家混合技术融入动态高斯喷溅的方法。在N3V和Technicolor数据集上的大量实验表明，MoE-GS持续优于最先进方法，且效率有所提升。视频演示可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/w/MoE-GS-68BA/ 观看。  

### [4] HGC-Avatar: Hierarchical Gaussian Compression for Streamable Dynamic 3D Avatars  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-18  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haocheng Tang, Ruoke Yan, Xinhui Yin et al.  
- **📝说明**：ACM International Conference on Multimedia 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16463v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled fast, photorealistic rendering of dynamic 3D scenes, showing strong potential in immersive communication. However, in digital human encoding and transmission, the compression methods based on general 3DGS representations are limited by the lack of human priors, resulting in suboptimal bitrate efficiency and reconstruction quality at the decoder side, which hinders their application in streamable 3D avatar systems. We propose HGC-Avatar, a novel Hierarchical Gaussian Compression framework designed for efficient transmission and high-quality rendering of dynamic avatars. Our method disentangles the Gaussian representation into a structural layer, which maps poses to Gaussians via a StyleUNet-based generator, and a motion layer, which leverages the SMPL-X model to represent temporal pose variations compactly and semantically. This hierarchical design supports layer-wise compression, progressive decoding, and controllable rendering from diverse pose inputs such as video sequences or text. Since people are most concerned with facial realism, we incorporate a facial attention mechanism during StyleUNet training to preserve identity and expression details under low-bitrate constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that HGC-Avatar provides a streamable solution for rapid 3D avatar rendering, while significantly outperforming prior methods in both visual quality and compression efficiency.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯溅射（3DGS）的最新进展使动态3D场景能够快速、写实地渲染，展现出沉浸式交流的强大潜力。然而，在数字人工编码和传输中，基于通用3DGS表示的压缩方法受限于缺乏人工先验，导致解码器端的比特率效率和重建质量不理想，阻碍了其在可流式3D虚拟形象系统的应用。我们提出了HGC-Avatar，一种创新的分层高斯压缩框架，旨在高效传输和高质量渲染动态化身。我们的方法将高斯表示解开为结构层，通过基于StyleUNet的生成器将姿态映射为高斯图，以及运动层，利用SMPL-X模型紧凑且语义地表示时间姿态变化。这种分层设计支持分层压缩、渐进解码以及从视频序列或文本等多样姿态输入中可控渲染。由于人们最关心面部真实感，我们在StyleUNet培训中引入了面部注意力机制，以在低比特率约束下保持身份和表情细节。实验结果表明，HGC-Avatar为快速3D头像渲染提供了可流式解决方案，同时在视觉质量和压缩效率方面远超以往方法。  

### [5] Leveraging Learned Image Prior for 3D Gaussian Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-16  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Seungjoo Shin, Jaesik Park, Sunghyun Cho  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to ICCV 2025 Workshop on ECLR  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14705v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Compression techniques for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have recently achieved considerable success in minimizing storage overhead for 3D Gaussians while preserving high rendering quality. Despite the impressive storage reduction, the lack of learned priors restricts further advances in the rate-distortion trade-off for 3DGS compression tasks. To address this, we introduce a novel 3DGS compression framework that leverages the powerful representational capacity of learned image priors to recover compression-induced quality degradation. Built upon initially compressed Gaussians, our restoration network effectively models the compression artifacts in the image space between degraded and original Gaussians. To enhance the rate-distortion performance, we provide coarse rendering residuals into the restoration network as side information. By leveraging the supervision of restored images, the compressed Gaussians are refined, resulting in a highly compact representation with enhanced rendering performance. Our framework is designed to be compatible with existing Gaussian compression methods, making it broadly applicable across different baselines. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our framework, demonstrating superior rate-distortion performance and outperforming the rendering quality of state-of-the-art 3DGS compression methods while requiring substantially less storage.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅压缩技术（3DGS）近年来在最小化3D高斯分布存储开销的同时保持高渲染质量方面取得了显著成功。尽管存储量大幅减少，但缺乏学习先验限制了3DGS压缩任务在速率-失真权衡上的进一步进展。为此，我们引入了一种新颖的3DGS压缩框架，利用学习到的图像先验的强大表征能力，恢复压缩引起的质量下降。基于最初压缩的高斯，我们的恢复网络有效模拟了退化高斯与原始高斯图像空间中的压缩伪影。为了提升速率-失真性能，我们向恢复网络提供了粗略渲染残差作为辅助信息。通过对还原图像的监督，压缩高斯得以优化，从而获得高度紧凑且渲染性能增强的表示。我们的框架设计兼容现有的高斯压缩方法，使其在不同基线间广泛适用。大量实验验证了我们框架的有效性，展示了更优越的速率失真性能，并优于最先进的3DGS压缩方法的渲染质量，同时所需存储空间大幅减少。  

### [6] PAGS: Priority-Adaptive Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Driving Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-14  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Ying A, Wenzhang Sun, Chang Zeng et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12282v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Reconstructing dynamic 3D urban scenes is crucial for autonomous driving, yet current methods face a stark trade-off between fidelity and computational cost. This inefficiency stems from their semantically agnostic design, which allocates resources uniformly, treating static backgrounds and safety-critical objects with equal importance. To address this, we introduce Priority-Adaptive Gaussian Splatting (PAGS), a framework that injects task-aware semantic priorities directly into the 3D reconstruction and rendering pipeline. PAGS introduces two core contributions: (1) Semantically-Guided Pruning and Regularization strategy, which employs a hybrid importance metric to aggressively simplify non-critical scene elements while preserving fine-grained details on objects vital for navigation. (2) Priority-Driven Rendering pipeline, which employs a priority-based depth pre-pass to aggressively cull occluded primitives and accelerate the final shading computations. Extensive experiments on the Waymo and KITTI datasets demonstrate that PAGS achieves exceptional reconstruction quality, particularly on safety-critical objects, while significantly reducing training time and boosting rendering speeds to over 350 FPS.  
- **📝翻译**: 重建动态3D城市场景对于自动驾驶至关重要，但现有方法在真实度和计算成本之间面临着巨大的权衡。这种低效源于其语义中立的设计，资源分配统一，静态背景和安全关键对象同等重视。为此，我们引入了优先自适应高斯散画（PAGS），这是一个将任务感知语义优先级直接注入三维重建和渲染流水线的框架。PAGS引入了两项核心贡献：（1）语义引导剪枝与正则化策略，采用混合重要度量，积极简化非关键场景元素，同时保留对导航至关重要物体的细致细节。（2） 优先级驱动渲染流水线，采用基于优先级的深度预通，积极剔除被遮挡的图元，加速最终着色计算。对Waymo和KITTI数据集的广泛实验表明，PAGS在安全关键物体上实现了卓越的重建质量，同时显著缩短了训练时间，并将渲染速度提升至350 FPS以上。  

### [7] UniGS: Unified Geometry-Aware Gaussian Splatting for Multimodal Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-14（更新：2025-11-13）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yusen Xie, Zhenmin Huang, Jianhao Jiao, Dimitrios Kanoulas, Jun Ma  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12174v2)    
- **📝摘要**：In this paper, we propose UniGS, a unified map representation and differentiable framework for high-fidelity multimodal 3D reconstruction based on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our framework integrates a CUDA-accelerated rasterization pipeline capable of rendering photo-realistic RGB images, geometrically accurate depth maps, consistent surface normals, and semantic logits simultaneously. We redesign the rasterization to render depth via differentiable ray-ellipsoid intersection rather than using Gaussian centers, enabling effective optimization of rotation and scale attribute through analytic depth gradients. Furthermore, we derive the analytic gradient formulation for surface normal rendering, ensuring geometric consistency among reconstructed 3D scenes. To improve computational and storage efficiency, we introduce a learnable attribute that enables differentiable pruning of Gaussians with minimal contribution during training. Quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy across all modalities, validating the efficacy of our geometry-aware paradigm. Source code and multimodal viewer will be available on GitHub.  
- **📝翻译**: 本文提出了UniGS，这是一种基于3D高斯喷涂的高精度多模态3D重建的统一地图表示和可微框架。我们的框架集成了CUDA加速的光栅化流水线，能够同时渲染逼真的RGB图像、几何精确的深度图、一致的表面法线和语义对数。我们重新设计光栅化，通过可微射线-椭球交集渲染深度，而非使用高斯中心，从而通过解析深度梯度有效优化旋转和尺度属性。此外，我们还推导出了用于表面法线渲染的解析梯度表述，确保重建后的三维场景之间的几何一致性。为提升计算和存储效率，我们引入了可学习属性，使高斯分布在训练过程中贡献最小，实现可微剪枝。定量和定性实验展示了所有模态中最先进的重建精度，验证了我们几何感知范式的有效性。源代码和多模态查看器将发布在GitHub上。  

### [8] Towards Efficient 3D Gaussian Human Avatar Compression: A Prior-Guided Framework  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-12  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shanzhi Yin, Bolin Chen, Xinju Wu et al.  
- **📝说明**：10 pages, 4 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10492v1)    
- **📝摘要**：This paper proposes an efficient 3D avatar coding framework that leverages compact human priors and canonical-to-target transformation to enable high-quality 3D human avatar video compression at ultra-low bit rates. The framework begins by training a canonical Gaussian avatar using articulated splatting in a network-free manner, which serves as the foundation for avatar appearance modeling. Simultaneously, a human-prior template is employed to capture temporal body movements through compact parametric representations. This decomposition of appearance and temporal evolution minimizes redundancy, enabling efficient compression: the canonical avatar is shared across the sequence, requiring compression only once, while the temporal parameters, consisting of just 94 parameters per frame, are transmitted with minimal bit-rate. For each frame, the target human avatar is generated by deforming canonical avatar via Linear Blend Skinning transformation, facilitating temporal coherent video reconstruction and novel view synthesis. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms conventional 2D/3D codecs and existing learnable dynamic 3D Gaussian splatting compression method in terms of rate-distortion performance on mainstream multi-view human video datasets, paving the way for seamless immersive multimedia experiences in meta-verse applications.  
- **📝翻译**: 本文提出了一种高效的3D头像编码框架，利用紧凑的人类先验和规范到目标的转换，实现高质量的3D头像视频压缩，且以超低比特率实现。该框架首先通过无网络的方式使用有节拍的喷溅训练一个典型的高斯化身，这成为化身外观建模的基础。同时，采用人先验模板通过紧凑的参数化表示捕捉时间上的身体运动。这种外观分解和时间演变最大限度地减少了冗余，从而实现高效压缩：规范的头像在序列中共享，只需压缩一次，而每帧仅有94个参数的时间参数以最小的比特率传输。对于每一帧，目标人类头像通过线性混合换皮变形的典型头像生成，促进时间连贯的视频重建和新颖视图的合成。实验结果表明，该方法在主流多视角人类视频数据集的速率失真表现上，显著优于传统2D/3D编解码器和现有可学习的3D高斯喷涂压缩方法，为元宇宙应用中无缝沉浸式多媒体体验铺平了道路。  

### [9] Opacity-Gradient Driven Density Control for Compact and Efficient Few-Shot 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Abdelrhman Elrawy, Emad A. Mohammed  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10257v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) struggles in few-shot scenarios, where its standard adaptive density control (ADC) can lead to overfitting and bloated reconstructions. While state-of-the-art methods like FSGS improve quality, they often do so by significantly increasing the primitive count. This paper presents a framework that revises the core 3DGS optimization to prioritize efficiency. We replace the standard positional gradient heuristic with a novel densification trigger that uses the opacity gradient as a lightweight proxy for rendering error. We find this aggressive densification is only effective when paired with a more conservative pruning schedule, which prevents destructive optimization cycles. Combined with a standard depth-correlation loss for geometric guidance, our framework demonstrates a fundamental improvement in efficiency. On the 3-view LLFF dataset, our model is over 40% more compact (32k vs. 57k primitives) than FSGS, and on the Mip-NeRF 360 dataset, it achieves a reduction of approximately 70%. This dramatic gain in compactness is achieved with a modest trade-off in reconstruction metrics, establishing a new state-of-the-art on the quality-vs-efficiency Pareto frontier for few-shot view synthesis.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射（3DGS）在少数拍摄场景中表现不佳，其标准的自适应密度控制（ADC）可能导致过度拟合和重建臃肿。虽然像FSGS这样的先进方法提升了质量，但它们往往通过显著增加原始数量来实现。本文提出了一个框架，修订了核心3DGS优化，以优先考虑效率。我们用一种新颖的密度化触发方式替代了标准的位置梯度启发式，该触发器将不透明度梯度作为渲染误差的轻量级代理。我们发现，这种积极的致密化只有在配合更保守的修剪计划时才有效，从而防止破坏性的优化循环。结合标准的深度相关损失，我们的框架展示了效率的根本性提升。在三视图LLFF数据集中，我们的模型比FSGS紧凑超过40%（32k对57k原语），而在Mip-NeRF 360数据集中，其缩减约为70%。这种紧凑度的显著提升是通过在重建指标上做出适度权衡实现的，为少张图合成在质量与效率的帕累托边界建立了新的尖端技术。  

### [10] P-4DGS: Predictive 4D Gaussian Splatting with 90$\times$ Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Henan Wang, Hanxin Zhu, Xinliang Gong et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10030v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has garnered significant attention due to its superior scene representation fidelity and real-time rendering performance, especially for dynamic 3D scene reconstruction (\textit{i.e.}, 4D reconstruction). However, despite achieving promising results, most existing algorithms overlook the substantial temporal and spatial redundancies inherent in dynamic scenes, leading to prohibitive memory consumption. To address this, we propose P-4DGS, a novel dynamic 3DGS representation for compact 4D scene modeling. Inspired by intra- and inter-frame prediction techniques commonly used in video compression, we first design a 3D anchor point-based spatial-temporal prediction module to fully exploit the spatial-temporal correlations across different 3D Gaussian primitives. Subsequently, we employ an adaptive quantization strategy combined with context-based entropy coding to further reduce the size of the 3D anchor points, thereby achieving enhanced compression efficiency. To evaluate the rate-distortion performance of our proposed P-4DGS in comparison with other dynamic 3DGS representations, we conduct extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and the fastest rendering speed, with a remarkably low storage footprint (around \textbf{1MB} on average), achieving up to \textbf{40$\times$} and \textbf{90$\times$} compression on synthetic and real-world scenes, respectively.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射（3DGS）因其卓越的场景表现精度和实时渲染性能而备受关注，尤其是在动态3D场景重建（\textit{即}，4D重建方面）。然而，尽管取得了有希望的结果，大多数现有算法却忽视了动态场景中固有的大量时间和空间冗余，导致内存消耗过高。为此，我们提出了P-4DGS，一种用于紧凑4D场景建模的新型动态3DGS表示。受视频压缩中常用的帧内和帧间预测技术启发，我们首先设计了一个基于3D锚点的时空预测模块，充分利用不同3D高斯原语间的时空相关性。随后，我们采用自适应量化策略结合基于上下文的熵编码，进一步缩小三维锚点大小，从而实现更高的压缩效率。为了评估我们提出的P-4DGS与其他动态3DGS表示的速率失真性能，我们在合成和现实世界数据集上进行了大量实验。实验结果表明，我们的方法实现了最先进的重建质量和最快的渲染速度，存储占用极低（平均约为 \textbf{1MB}），在合成场景和现实场景中分别实现高达 \textbf{40$\times$} 和 \textbf{90$\times$}。  

### [11] CLoD-GS: Continuous Level-of-Detail via 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhigang Cheng, Mingchao Sun, Yu Liu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09997v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Level of Detail (LoD) is a fundamental technique in real-time computer graphics for managing the rendering costs of complex scenes while preserving visual fidelity. Traditionally, LoD is implemented using discrete levels (DLoD), where multiple, distinct versions of a model are swapped out at different distances. This long-standing paradigm, however, suffers from two major drawbacks: it requires significant storage for multiple model copies and causes jarring visual ``popping" artifacts during transitions, degrading the user experience. We argue that the explicit, primitive-based nature of the emerging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) technique enables a more ideal paradigm: Continuous LoD (CLoD). A CLoD approach facilitates smooth, seamless quality scaling within a single, unified model, thereby circumventing the core problems of DLOD. To this end, we introduce CLoD-GS, a framework that integrates a continuous LoD mechanism directly into a 3DGS representation. Our method introduces a learnable, distance-dependent decay parameter for each Gaussian primitive, which dynamically adjusts its opacity based on viewpoint proximity. This allows for the progressive and smooth filtering of less significant primitives, effectively creating a continuous spectrum of detail within one model. To train this model to be robust across all distances, we introduce a virtual distance scaling mechanism and a novel coarse-to-fine training strategy with rendered point count regularization. Our approach not only eliminates the storage overhead and visual artifacts of discrete methods but also reduces the primitive count and memory footprint of the final model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CLoD-GS achieves smooth, quality-scalable rendering from a single model, delivering high-fidelity results across a wide range of performance targets.  
- **📝翻译**: 细节水平（Level of Detail，简称LoD）是实时计算机图形学中一种基本技术，用于管理复杂场景的渲染成本，同时保持视觉真实度。传统上，LoD通过离散层级（DLoD）实现，即在不同距离处替换多个不同版本的模型。然而，这种长期存在的模式存在两个主要缺点：需要大量存储多个模型副本，并且在切换时会产生刺耳的视觉“爆裂”伪影，降低用户体验。我们认为，新兴的三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）技术的显式、基于原始的特性，使得更理想的范式成为可能：连续LoD（CLoD）。CLoD方法促进了单一统一模型内平滑无缝的质量扩展，从而绕过了DLOD的核心问题。为此，我们引入了CLoD-GS框架，该框架将连续LoD机制直接集成到3DGS表示中。我们的方法为每个高斯原语引入了一个可学习的、随距离变化的衰减参数，该参数根据视点的接近度动态调整其不透明度。这使得对次要基元进行渐进且平滑的过滤，在一个模型内有效地创造出连续的细节谱。为了训练该模型在所有距离上都鲁棒，我们引入了虚拟距离尺度机制和一种带有渲染点数正则化的全新粗细训练策略。我们的方法不仅消除了离散方法的存储开销和视觉伪影，还减少了最终模型的原始数量和内存占用。大量实验表明，CLoD-GS能够从单一模型实现平滑、质量可扩展的渲染，在广泛的性能目标上实现高保真效果。  

### [12] ReSplat: Learning Recurrent Gaussian Splats  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-09  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haofei Xu, Daniel Barath, Andreas Geiger, Marc Pollefeys  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://haofeixu.github.io/resplat/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08575v1)   · [Project](https://haofeixu.github.io/resplat/.)  
- **📝摘要**：While feed-forward Gaussian splatting models provide computational efficiency and effectively handle sparse input settings, their performance is fundamentally limited by the reliance on a single forward pass during inference. We propose ReSplat, a feed-forward recurrent Gaussian splatting model that iteratively refines 3D Gaussians without explicitly computing gradients. Our key insight is that the Gaussian splatting rendering error serves as a rich feedback signal, guiding the recurrent network to learn effective Gaussian updates. This feedback signal naturally adapts to unseen data distributions at test time, enabling robust generalization. To initialize the recurrent process, we introduce a compact reconstruction model that operates in a $16 \times$ subsampled space, producing $16 \times$ fewer Gaussians than previous per-pixel Gaussian models. This substantially reduces computational overhead and allows for efficient Gaussian updates. Extensive experiments across varying of input views (2, 8, 16), resolutions ($256 \times 256$ to $540 \times 960$), and datasets (DL3DV and RealEstate10K) demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly reducing the number of Gaussians and improving the rendering speed. Our project page is at https://haofeixu.github.io/resplat/.  
- **📝翻译**: 虽然前馈高斯喷溅模型提供了计算效率并有效处理稀疏输入设置，但其性能在推断过程中依赖单次前向传递而根本受限。我们提出了ReSplat，一种前馈循环高斯喷涂模型，通过迭代细化三维高斯分布而不显式计算梯度。我们的关键见解是，高斯溅射渲染错误作为丰富的反馈信号，引导循环网络学习有效的高斯更新。该反馈信号在测试时自然适应未见数据分布，实现稳健的泛化。为初始化递归过程，我们引入了一个紧凑的重建模型，其在16美元\乘值的子采样空间中运行，产生的高斯数比以往的每像素高斯模型少16/倍数。这大大降低了计算开销，并实现了高效的高斯更新。通过涵盖不同输入视图（2、8、16）、分辨率（$256 \ 乘以 $256$ 至 $540 \ 乘以 960 美元）以及数据集（DL3DV 和 RealEstate10K）进行的广泛实验，表明我们的方法在显著减少高斯分布数量和提升渲染速度的同时，实现了最先进的性能。我们的项目页面在 https://haofeixu.github.io/resplat/。  

### [13] ARTDECO: Towards Efficient and High-Fidelity On-the-Fly 3D Reconstruction with Structured Scene Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-09  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Guanghao Li, Kerui Ren, Linning Xu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08551v1)   · [Project](https://city-super.github.io/artdeco/.)  
- **📝摘要**：On-the-fly 3D reconstruction from monocular image sequences is a long-standing challenge in computer vision, critical for applications such as real-to-sim, AR/VR, and robotics. Existing methods face a major tradeoff: per-scene optimization yields high fidelity but is computationally expensive, whereas feed-forward foundation models enable real-time inference but struggle with accuracy and robustness. In this work, we propose ARTDECO, a unified framework that combines the efficiency of feed-forward models with the reliability of SLAM-based pipelines. ARTDECO uses 3D foundation models for pose estimation and point prediction, coupled with a Gaussian decoder that transforms multi-scale features into structured 3D Gaussians. To sustain both fidelity and efficiency at scale, we design a hierarchical Gaussian representation with a LoD-aware rendering strategy, which improves rendering fidelity while reducing redundancy. Experiments on eight diverse indoor and outdoor benchmarks show that ARTDECO delivers interactive performance comparable to SLAM, robustness similar to feed-forward systems, and reconstruction quality close to per-scene optimization, providing a practical path toward on-the-fly digitization of real-world environments with both accurate geometry and high visual fidelity. Explore more demos on our project page: https://city-super.github.io/artdeco/.  
- **📝翻译**: 从单眼图像序列进行即时三维重建是计算机视觉领域的长期挑战，对于实景模拟、增强现实/虚拟现实和机器人技术等应用至关重要。现有方法面临一个重大权衡：每场景优化能实现高保真度，但计算成本高，而前馈基础模型虽然支持实时推理，但在准确性和鲁棒性方面存在困难。在本研究中，我们提出了ARTDECO，这是一个统一框架，结合了前馈模型的效率与基于SLAM的管道的可靠性。ARTDECO使用三维基础模型进行姿态估计和点预测，结合高斯解码器，将多尺度特征转换为结构化的三维高斯解码器。为了在大规模下兼顾忠实度和效率，我们设计了带有 LoD 感知渲染策略的层次高斯表示，既提升了渲染的准确度，也减少了冗余。在八个不同的室内和室外基准测试上的实验表明，ARTDECO提供了可与SLAM相当的交互性能，稳健性类似于前馈系统，重建质量接近每场景优化，为实现实时数字化提供了实用路径，同时具备准确几何形状和高视觉真实度。更多演示请访问我们的项目页面：https://city-super.github.io/artdeco/。  

### [14] PrismGS: Physically-Grounded Anti-Aliasing for High-Fidelity Large-Scale 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-09  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Houqiang Zhong, Zhenglong Wu, Sihua Fu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07830v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently enabled real-time photorealistic rendering in compact scenes, but scaling to large urban environments introduces severe aliasing artifacts and optimization instability, especially under high-resolution (e.g., 4K) rendering. These artifacts, manifesting as flickering textures and jagged edges, arise from the mismatch between Gaussian primitives and the multi-scale nature of urban geometry. While existing ``divide-and-conquer'' pipelines address scalability, they fail to resolve this fidelity gap. In this paper, we propose PrismGS, a physically-grounded regularization framework that improves the intrinsic rendering behavior of 3D Gaussians. PrismGS integrates two synergistic regularizers. The first is pyramidal multi-scale supervision, which enforces consistency by supervising the rendering against a pre-filtered image pyramid. This compels the model to learn an inherently anti-aliased representation that remains coherent across different viewing scales, directly mitigating flickering textures. This is complemented by an explicit size regularization that imposes a physically-grounded lower bound on the dimensions of the 3D Gaussians. This prevents the formation of degenerate, view-dependent primitives, leading to more stable and plausible geometric surfaces and reducing jagged edges. Our method is plug-and-play and compatible with existing pipelines. Extensive experiments on MatrixCity, Mill-19, and UrbanScene3D demonstrate that PrismGS achieves state-of-the-art performance, yielding significant PSNR gains around 1.5 dB against CityGaussian, while maintaining its superior quality and robustness under demanding 4K rendering.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯拼拍（3DGS）近年来使得紧凑场景中的实时照片级真实渲染成为可能，但大规模城市环境的缩放会带来严重的锯齿伪影和优化不稳定性，尤其是在高分辨率（如4K）渲染下。这些文物表现为闪烁的纹理和锯齿状边缘，源于高斯原始体与城市几何多尺度本质之间的不匹配。虽然现有的“分而治之”管道解决了可扩展性问题，但未能弥补这一保真度差距。本文提出了棱镜GS，一种物理基础正则化框架，旨在改善三维高斯的内在渲染行为。PrismGS集成了两个协同正则化器。第一种是金字塔多尺度监督，通过在预滤波图像金字塔上监督渲染来强制一致性。这迫使模型学习一种固有的抗锯齿表示，在不同观看尺度下保持连贯性，直接减少闪烁纹理。这辅以显式的尺寸正则化，对三维高斯的维数施加物理基底的下界。这防止了退化、依赖视图的原语的形成，从而实现更稳定和合理的几何曲面，并减少锯齿边缘。我们的方法是即插即用，并且兼容现有的管道。在MatrixCity、Mill-19和UrbanScene3D上的广泛实验表明，PrismGS实现了最先进的性能，在相较于CityGaussian的PSNR上显著提升了约1.5 dB，同时在高要求的4K渲染下依然保持了其卓越的质量和稳健性。  

### [15] RTGS: Real-Time 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM via Multi-Level Redundancy Reduction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-08（更新：2025-10-09）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Leshu Li, Jiayin Qin, Jie Peng et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by MICRO2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06644v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) systems can largely benefit from 3DGS's state-of-the-art rendering efficiency and accuracy, but have not yet been adopted in resource-constrained edge devices due to insufficient speed. Addressing this, we identify notable redundancies across the SLAM pipeline for acceleration. While conceptually straightforward, practical approaches are required to minimize the overhead associated with identifying and eliminating these redundancies. In response, we propose RTGS, an algorithm-hardware co-design framework that comprehensively reduces the redundancies for real-time 3DGS-SLAM on edge. To minimize the overhead, RTGS fully leverages the characteristics of the 3DGS-SLAM pipeline. On the algorithm side, we introduce (1) an adaptive Gaussian pruning step to remove the redundant Gaussians by reusing gradients computed during backpropagation; and (2) a dynamic downsampling technique that directly reuses the keyframe identification and alpha computing steps to eliminate redundant pixels. On the hardware side, we propose (1) a subtile-level streaming strategy and a pixel-level pairwise scheduling strategy that mitigates workload imbalance via a Workload Scheduling Unit (WSU) guided by previous iteration information; (2) a Rendering and Backpropagation (R&B) Buffer that accelerates the rendering backpropagation by reusing intermediate data computed during rendering; and (3) a Gradient Merging Unit (GMU) to reduce intensive memory accesses caused by atomic operations while enabling pipelined aggregation. Integrated into an edge GPU, RTGS achieves real-time performance (>= 30 FPS) on four datasets and three algorithms, with up to 82.5x energy efficiency over the baseline and negligible quality loss. Code is available at https://github.com/UMN-ZhaoLab/RTGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 基于3D高斯喷射（3DGS）的同时定位与映射（SLAM）系统在很大程度上受益于3DGS最先进的渲染效率和精度，但由于速度不足，尚未被资源限制的边缘设备采用。针对此，我们识别了SLAM流程中显著的冗余以加速。虽然概念简单，但需要采取实际方法来最大限度地减少识别和消除这些冗余的开销。为此，我们提出了RTGS，一种算法-硬件协同设计框架，全面减少了边缘实时3DGS-SLAM的冗余。为了最大限度地降低开销，RTGS充分利用了3DGS-SLAM流水线的特性。在算法方面，我们引入了（1）自适应高斯剪枝步骤，通过重用反向传播时计算的梯度来去除冗余的高斯分布;以及（2）一种动态降采样技术，直接重用关键帧识别和α计算步骤以消除冗余像素。在硬件方面，我们提出：（1） 一种子瓦层流策略和像素层级的成对调度策略，通过工作负载调度单元（WSU）根据前次迭代信息引导，缓解工作负载不平衡;（2）一个渲染与反向传播（R&B）缓冲区，通过重用渲染过程中计算的中间数据加速渲染反向传播;以及（3）梯度合并单元（GMU），用于减少原子作带来的密集内存访问，同时实现流水线聚合。RTGS集成在边缘GPU中，在四个数据集和三种算法上实现实时性能（>= 30 FPS），能效比基线提升高达82.5倍，且质量损失可忽略不计。代码可在 https://github.com/UMN-ZhaoLab/RTGS 获取。  

### [16] ArchitectHead: Continuous Level of Detail Control for 3D Gaussian Head Avatars  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-07  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Peizhi Yan, Rabab Ward, Qiang Tang, Shan Du  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05488v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has enabled photorealistic and real-time rendering of 3D head avatars. Existing 3DGS-based avatars typically rely on tens of thousands of 3D Gaussian points (Gaussians), with the number of Gaussians fixed after training. However, many practical applications require adjustable levels of detail (LOD) to balance rendering efficiency and visual quality. In this work, we propose "ArchitectHead", the first framework for creating 3D Gaussian head avatars that support continuous control over LOD. Our key idea is to parameterize the Gaussians in a 2D UV feature space and propose a UV feature field composed of multi-level learnable feature maps to encode their latent features. A lightweight neural network-based decoder then transforms these latent features into 3D Gaussian attributes for rendering. ArchitectHead controls the number of Gaussians by dynamically resampling feature maps from the UV feature field at the desired resolutions. This method enables efficient and continuous control of LOD without retraining. Experimental results show that ArchitectHead achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) quality in self and cross-identity reenactment tasks at the highest LOD, while maintaining near SOTA performance at lower LODs. At the lowest LOD, our method uses only 6.2\% of the Gaussians while the quality degrades moderately (L1 Loss +7.9\%, PSNR --0.97\%, SSIM --0.6\%, LPIPS Loss +24.1\%), and the rendering speed nearly doubles.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）实现了3D头像的写实和实时渲染。现有基于3DGS的虚拟形象通常依赖数万个3D高斯点（高斯点），训练后高斯点数固定。然而，许多实际应用需要可调节的细节级别（LOD），以平衡渲染效率和视觉质量。在本研究中，我们提出了“ArchitectHead”，这是首个支持持续控制LOD的3D高斯头像框架。我们的核心思想是在二维紫外特征空间中参数化高斯分布，并提出一个由多层可学习特征映射组成的紫外特征场，以编码其潜在特征。然后，基于轻量级神经网络的解码器将这些潜在特征转换为三维高斯属性进行渲染。ArchitectHead 通过动态重采样 UV 特征场的特征映射以所需分辨率控制高斯分布数。该方法实现了高效且持续的LOD控制，无需重新训练。实验结果显示，ArchitectHead 在最高 LOD 下实现了自我和跨身份重现任务的尖端（SOTA）质量，同时在较低 LOD 下保持接近 SOTA 的性能。在最低LOD下，我们的方法只使用6.2%的高斯分布，而画质适度下降（L1损耗+7.9%，PSNR -0.97\%，SSIM -0.6\%，LPIPS损耗+24.1\%），渲染速度几乎翻倍。  

### [17] Optimized Minimal 4D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-04  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Minseo Lee, Byeonghyeon Lee, Lucas Yunkyu Lee et al.  
- **📝说明**：17 pages, 8 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03857v1)   · [Project](https://minshirley.github.io/OMG4/.)  
- **📝摘要**：4D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a new paradigm for dynamic scene representation, enabling real-time rendering of scenes with complex motions. However, it faces a major challenge of storage overhead, as millions of Gaussians are required for high-fidelity reconstruction. While several studies have attempted to alleviate this memory burden, they still face limitations in compression ratio or visual quality. In this work, we present OMG4 (Optimized Minimal 4D Gaussian Splatting), a framework that constructs a compact set of salient Gaussians capable of faithfully representing 4D Gaussian models. Our method progressively prunes Gaussians in three stages: (1) Gaussian Sampling to identify primitives critical to reconstruction fidelity, (2) Gaussian Pruning to remove redundancies, and (3) Gaussian Merging to fuse primitives with similar characteristics. In addition, we integrate implicit appearance compression and generalize Sub-Vector Quantization (SVQ) to 4D representations, further reducing storage while preserving quality. Extensive experiments on standard benchmark datasets demonstrate that OMG4 significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods, reducing model sizes by over 60% while maintaining reconstruction quality. These results position OMG4 as a significant step forward in compact 4D scene representation, opening new possibilities for a wide range of applications. Our source code is available at https://minshirley.github.io/OMG4/.  
- **📝翻译**: 4D高斯溅射已成为动态场景表现的新范式，使得具有复杂运动场景的实时渲染成为可能。然而，它面临着存储开销的重大挑战，因为高保真重建需要数百万个高斯处理器。尽管有多项研究试图减轻这种记忆负担，但它们在压缩比或视觉质量方面仍面临局限。在本研究中，我们提出了OMG4（优化最小四维高斯喷涂），这是一种构建紧凑且显著高斯分布集合的框架，能够忠实表示四维高斯模型。我们的方法逐步将高斯样本剪枝分为三个阶段：（1）高斯采样以识别对重建保真度至关重要的原语，（2）高斯剪枝以去除冗余，（3）高斯合并以融合具有相似特性的原语。此外，我们集成了隐式外观压缩，并将子矢量化（SVQ）推广到四维表示，进一步减少存储空间同时保持质量。对标准基准数据集的大量实验表明，OMG4 的表现显著优于最新最先进方法，模型规模减少了 60% 以上，同时保持了重建质量。这些结果使OMG4成为紧凑型4D场景表示领域的重要进步，为广泛应用开辟了新可能。我们的源代码可在 https://minshirley.github.io/OMG4/ 获取。  

### [18] GS-Share: Enabling High-fidelity Map Sharing with Incremental Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-03  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xinran Zhang, Hanqi Zhu, Yifan Duan, Yanyong Zhang  
- **📝说明**：11 pages, 11 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02884v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Constructing and sharing 3D maps is essential for many applications, including autonomous driving and augmented reality. Recently, 3D Gaussian splatting has emerged as a promising approach for accurate 3D reconstruction. However, a practical map-sharing system that features high-fidelity, continuous updates, and network efficiency remains elusive. To address these challenges, we introduce GS-Share, a photorealistic map-sharing system with a compact representation. The core of GS-Share includes anchor-based global map construction, virtual-image-based map enhancement, and incremental map update. We evaluate GS-Share against state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating that our system achieves higher fidelity, particularly for extrapolated views, with improvements of 11%, 22%, and 74% in PSNR, LPIPS, and Depth L1, respectively. Furthermore, GS-Share is significantly more compact, reducing map transmission overhead by 36%.  
- **📝翻译**: 构建和共享3D地图对于许多应用至关重要，包括自动驾驶和增强现实。近年来，三维高斯喷溅作为一种有前景的三维重建方法出现。然而，具备高保真度、持续更新和网络效率的实用地图共享系统仍然难以实现。为应对这些挑战，我们推出了GS-Share，一种紧凑的写实地图共享系统。GS-Share的核心包括基于锚点的全球地图构建、基于虚拟图像的地图增强和增量地图更新。我们用最先进的方法评估GS-Share，证明系统在外推视图方面实现了更高的保真度，PSNR、LPIPS和深度L1分别提升了11%、22%和74%。此外，GS-Share体积更紧凑，地图传输开销降低了36%。  

### [19] From Tokens to Nodes: Semantic-Guided Motion Control for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-03  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jianing Chen, Zehao Li, Yujun Cai et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02732v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Dynamic 3D reconstruction from monocular videos remains difficult due to the ambiguity inferring 3D motion from limited views and computational demands of modeling temporally varying scenes. While recent sparse control methods alleviate computation by reducing millions of Gaussians to thousands of control points, they suffer from a critical limitation: they allocate points purely by geometry, leading to static redundancy and dynamic insufficiency. We propose a motion-adaptive framework that aligns control density with motion complexity. Leveraging semantic and motion priors from vision foundation models, we establish patch-token-node correspondences and apply motion-adaptive compression to concentrate control points in dynamic regions while suppressing redundancy in static backgrounds. Our approach achieves flexible representational density adaptation through iterative voxelization and motion tendency scoring, directly addressing the fundamental mismatch between control point allocation and motion complexity. To capture temporal evolution, we introduce spline-based trajectory parameterization initialized by 2D tracklets, replacing MLP-based deformation fields to achieve smoother motion representation and more stable optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction quality and efficiency over existing state-of-the-art methods.  
- **📝翻译**: 由于有限视角推断3D运动存在歧义和对时间变化场景的计算需求，单目视频的动态3D重建仍然困难。虽然最近的稀疏控制方法通过将数百万高斯分布量减少到数千个控制点来缓解计算，但它们存在一个关键局限：它们仅通过几何分配点，导致静态冗余和动态不足。我们提出了一个运动自适应框架，将控制密度与运动复杂性对齐。利用视觉基础模型中的语义和运动先验，我们建立了补丁-令牌-节点对应关系，并应用运动自适应压缩，集中控制点于动态区域，同时抑制静态背景中的冗余。我们的方法通过迭代体素化和运动趋势评分实现了灵活的表征密度适应，直接解决了控制点分配与运动复杂度之间的根本不匹配问题。为捕捉时间演化，我们引入了基于样条的轨迹参数化，初始化为二维轨迹，取代基于MLP的变形场，实现更平滑的运动表示和更稳定的优化。大量实验显示，重建质量和效率相较于现有最先进方法有显著提升。  

### [20] Instant4D: 4D Gaussian Splatting in Minutes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-10-01  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhanpeng Luo, Haoxi Ran, Li Lu  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by NeurIPS 25  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01119v1)   · [Project](https://instant4d.github.io/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Dynamic view synthesis has seen significant advances, yet reconstructing scenes from uncalibrated, casual video remains challenging due to slow optimization and complex parameter estimation. In this work, we present Instant4D, a monocular reconstruction system that leverages native 4D representation to efficiently process casual video sequences within minutes, without calibrated cameras or depth sensors. Our method begins with geometric recovery through deep visual SLAM, followed by grid pruning to optimize scene representation. Our design significantly reduces redundancy while maintaining geometric integrity, cutting model size to under 10% of its original footprint. To handle temporal dynamics efficiently, we introduce a streamlined 4D Gaussian representation, achieving a 30x speed-up and reducing training time to within two minutes, while maintaining competitive performance across several benchmarks. Our method reconstruct a single video within 10 minutes on the Dycheck dataset or for a typical 200-frame video. We further apply our model to in-the-wild videos, showcasing its generalizability. Our project website is published at https://instant4d.github.io/.  
- **📝翻译**: 动态视图合成取得了显著进展，但由于优化缓慢和参数估计复杂，从未校准的休闲视频中重建场景仍然具有挑战性。本研究介绍了Instant4D，一种单眼重建系统，利用原生4D表示技术，无需校准摄像机或深度传感器，即可在几分钟内高效处理普通视频序列。我们的方法从深度可视化SLAM进行几何恢复开始，随后进行网格剪枝以优化场景表现。我们的设计显著减少了冗余，同时保持几何完整性，将模型尺寸缩短至原始占地面积的10%以下。为了高效处理时间动态，我们引入了简化的4D高斯表示，实现了30倍的加速，并将训练时间缩短至两分钟以内，同时在多个基准测试中保持竞争力。我们的方法可以在Dycheck数据集或典型的200帧视频中，在10分钟内重建单个视频。我们还将模型应用于实际视频，展示了其普适性。我们的项目网站发布在 https://instant4d.github.io/。  


## September 2025

### [1] GEM: 3D Gaussian Splatting for Efficient and Accurate Cryo-EM Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-29（更新：2025-10-02）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Huaizhi Qu, Xiao Wang, Gengwei Zhang, Jie Peng, Tianlong Chen  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25075v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) has become a central tool for high-resolution structural biology, yet the massive scale of datasets (often exceeding 100k particle images) renders 3D reconstruction both computationally expensive and memory intensive. Traditional Fourier-space methods are efficient but lose fidelity due to repeated transforms, while recent real-space approaches based on neural radiance fields (NeRFs) improve accuracy but incur cubic memory and computation overhead. Therefore, we introduce GEM, a novel cryo-EM reconstruction framework built on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) that operates directly in real-space while maintaining high efficiency. Instead of modeling the entire density volume, GEM represents proteins with compact 3D Gaussians, each parameterized by only 11 values. To further improve the training efficiency, we designed a novel gradient computation to 3D Gaussians that contribute to each voxel. This design substantially reduced both memory footprint and training cost. On standard cryo-EM benchmarks, GEM achieves up to 48% faster training and 12% lower memory usage compared to state-of-the-art methods, while improving local resolution by as much as 38.8%. These results establish GEM as a practical and scalable paradigm for cryo-EM reconstruction, unifying speed, efficiency, and high-resolution accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/UNITES-Lab/GEM.  
- **📝翻译**: 低温电子显微镜（cryo-EM）已成为高分辨率结构生物学的核心工具，但庞大的数据集（通常超过10万张粒子图像）使得三维重建既计算量大又占用内存。传统的傅里叶空间方法效率高，但由于重复变换而失去保真度，而基于神经辐射场（NeRFs）的实空间新方法提高了精度，但会产生立方存储和计算开销。因此，我们介绍了GEM，一种基于三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）的新型低温电磁重建框架，能够直接在实空间工作，同时保持高效率。GEM不对整个密度体积建模，而是用紧凑的三维高斯分布表示蛋白质，每个高斯分布仅由11个参数参数化。为了进一步提升训练效率，我们设计了一种新的梯度计算方法，用于贡献每个体素的三维高斯量。这一设计大幅降低了内存占用和训练成本。在标准冷冻电磁基准测试中，GEM相比最先进方法，训练速度提升了48%，内存使用降低了12%，同时局部分辨率提升了多达38.8%。这些结果确立了GEM作为低温电磁重建的实用且可扩展范式，统一了速度、效率和高分辨率精度。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/UNITES-Lab/GEM 获取。  

### [2] ExGS: Extreme 3D Gaussian Compression with Diffusion Priors  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-29（更新：2025-10-07）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiaqi Chen, Xinhao Ji, Yuanyuan Gao et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24758v4)    
- **📝摘要**：Neural scene representations, such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), have enabled high-quality neural rendering; however, their large storage and transmission costs hinder deployment in resource-constrained environments. Existing compression methods either rely on costly optimization, which is slow and scene-specific, or adopt training-free pruning and quantization, which degrade rendering quality under high compression ratios. In contrast, recent data-driven approaches provide a promising direction to overcome this trade-off, enabling efficient compression while preserving high rendering quality. We introduce ExGS, a novel feed-forward framework that unifies Universal Gaussian Compression (UGC) with GaussPainter for Extreme 3DGS compression. UGC performs re-optimization-free pruning to aggressively reduce Gaussian primitives while retaining only essential information, whereas GaussPainter leverages powerful diffusion priors with mask-guided refinement to restore high-quality renderings from heavily pruned Gaussian scenes. Unlike conventional inpainting, GaussPainter not only fills in missing regions but also enhances visible pixels, yielding substantial improvements in degraded renderings. To ensure practicality, it adopts a lightweight VAE and a one-step diffusion design, enabling real-time restoration. Our framework can even achieve over 100X compression (reducing a typical 354.77 MB model to about 3.31 MB) while preserving fidelity and significantly improving image quality under challenging conditions. These results highlight the central role of diffusion priors in bridging the gap between extreme compression and high-quality neural rendering. Our code repository will be released at: https://github.com/chenttt2001/ExGS  
- **📝翻译**: 神经场景表示，如3D高斯溅射（3DGS），使得高质量的神经渲染成为可能;然而，其高昂的存储和传输成本阻碍了在资源有限环境中的部署。现有压缩方法要么依赖成本高昂且因场景特定而缓慢的优化，要么采用无训练的剪枝和量化，这在高压缩比下会降低渲染质量。相比之下，近期的数据驱动方法为克服这一权衡提供了有前景的方向，实现高效压缩同时保持高渲染质量。我们介绍ExGS，一种新颖的前馈框架，将通用高斯压缩（UGC）与极限3DGS压缩的高斯画器（GaussPainter）合并。UGC通过无重新优化剪枝，在保留关键信息的同时积极减少高斯原语，而GaussPainter则利用强大的扩散先验和掩膜引导的细化，从高度修剪的高斯场景中恢复高质量渲染。与传统的补绘不同，GaussPainter不仅填补缺失区域，还增强可见像素，显著改善了劣化渲染。为确保实用性，采用轻量化VAE和一级扩散设计，实现实时修复。我们的框架甚至能实现超过 100 倍压缩（将典型的 354.77 MB 模型压缩至约 3.31 MB），同时保持画质并显著提升在挑战性条件下的画质。这些结果凸显了扩散先验在弥合极端压缩与高质量神经渲染之间差距中的核心作用。我们的代码库将发布于：https://github.com/chenttt2001/ExGS  

### [3] Proxy-GS: Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting via Proxy Mesh  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-29（更新：2025-10-01）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuanyuan Gao, Yuning Gong, Yifei Liu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24421v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an efficient approach for achieving photorealistic rendering. Recent MLP-based variants further improve visual fidelity but introduce substantial decoding overhead during rendering. To alleviate computation cost, several pruning strategies and level-of-detail (LOD) techniques have been introduced, aiming to effectively reduce the number of Gaussian primitives in large-scale scenes. However, our analysis reveals that significant redundancy still remains due to the lack of occlusion awareness. In this work, we propose Proxy-GS, a novel pipeline that exploits a proxy to introduce Gaussian occlusion awareness from any view. At the core of our approach is a fast proxy system capable of producing precise occlusion depth maps at a resolution of 1000x1000 under 1ms. This proxy serves two roles: first, it guides the culling of anchors and Gaussians to accelerate rendering speed. Second, it guides the densification towards surfaces during training, avoiding inconsistencies in occluded regions, and improving the rendering quality. In heavily occluded scenarios, such as the MatrixCity Streets dataset, Proxy-GS not only equips MLP-based Gaussian splatting with stronger rendering capability but also achieves faster rendering speed. Specifically, it achieves more than 2.5x speedup over Octree-GS, and consistently delivers substantially higher rendering quality. Code will be public upon acceptance.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为实现照片级真实渲染的高效方法。最新的基于MLP的变体进一步提升了视觉真实度，但在渲染过程中带来了大量的解码开销。为降低计算成本，已引入多种修剪策略和细节层级（LOD）技术，旨在有效减少大规模场景中高斯原语的数量。然而，我们的分析显示，由于缺乏遮挡意识，仍存在显著的冗余。在本研究中，我们提出了Proxy-GS这一新颖的流水线，利用代理从任何视角引入高斯遮挡感知。我们的核心是一个快速代理系统，能够在1毫秒以内生成精确的遮挡深度图，分辨率为1000x1000。该代理有两个作用：首先，它引导剔除锚点和高斯分布，以加快渲染速度。其次，它在训练过程中引导表面密度，避免遮挡区域的不一致，并提升渲染质量。在高度遮挡的场景中，如MatrixCity Streets数据集，Proxy-GS不仅为基于MLP的高斯喷涂赋予更强的渲染能力，还实现了更快的渲染速度。具体来说，它比Octree-GS快了2.5倍以上，并且持续呈现出显著更高的渲染质量。代码一旦被接受，将对外公开。  

### [4] OMeGa: Joint Optimization of Explicit Meshes and Gaussian Splats for Robust Scene-Level Surface Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-29  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuhang Cao, Haojun Yan, Danya Yao  
- **📝说明**：12 pages, 9 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24308v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Neural rendering with Gaussian splatting has advanced novel view synthesis, and most methods reconstruct surfaces via post-hoc mesh extraction. However, existing methods suffer from two limitations: (i) inaccurate geometry in texture-less indoor regions, and (ii) the decoupling of mesh extraction from optimization, thereby missing the opportunity to leverage mesh geometry to guide splat optimization. In this paper, we present OMeGa, an end-to-end framework that jointly optimizes an explicit triangle mesh and 2D Gaussian splats via a flexible binding strategy, where spatial attributes of Gaussian Splats are expressed in the mesh frame and texture attributes are retained on splats. To further improve reconstruction accuracy, we integrate mesh constraints and monocular normal supervision into the optimization, thereby regularizing geometry learning. In addition, we propose a heuristic, iterative mesh-refinement strategy that splits high-error faces and prunes unreliable ones to further improve the detail and accuracy of the reconstructed mesh. OMeGa achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging indoor reconstruction benchmarks, reducing Chamfer-$L_1$ by 47.3\% over the 2DGS baseline while maintaining competitive novel-view rendering quality. The experimental results demonstrate that OMeGa effectively addresses prior limitations in indoor texture-less reconstruction.  
- **📝翻译**: 高斯散拍的神经渲染带来了先进的视图合成技术，大多数方法通过后置网格提取重建曲面。然而，现有方法存在两个局限性：（i）无纹理室内区域的几何不准确，（ii）网格提取与优化脱钩，从而错失利用网格几何来指导splat优化的机会。本文介绍了OMeGa，一个端到端框架，通过灵活绑定策略联合优化显式三角形网格和二维高斯飞溅，其中高斯飞溅的空间属性表示在网格框架中，纹理属性保留在飞溅上。为进一步提升重建精度，我们将网格约束和单眼法线监督整合进优化，从而规范几何学习。此外，我们提出了一种启发式迭代网格细化策略，将高误差面拆分并修剪不可靠的面，以进一步提升重建网格的细节和准确性。OMeGa在具有挑战性的室内重建基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，比2DGS基线降低了47.3%的倒角$L_1美元成本，同时保持了竞争性的新颖视图渲染质量。实验结果表明，OMeGa有效解决了室内无纹理重建的先前局限性。  

### [5] Vision-Language Alignment from Compressed Image Representations using 2D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-26  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yasmine Omri, Connor Ding, Tsachy Weissman, Thierry Tambe  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22615v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Modern vision language pipelines are driven by RGB vision encoders trained on massive image text corpora. While these pipelines have enabled impressive zero shot capabilities and strong transfer across tasks, they still inherit two structural inefficiencies from the pixel domain: (i) transmitting dense RGB images from edge devices to the cloud is energy intensive and costly, and (ii) patch based tokenization explodes sequence length, stressing attention budgets and context limits. We explore 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) as an alternative visual substrate for alignment: a compact, spatially adaptive representation that parameterizes images by a set of colored anisotropic Gaussians. We develop a scalable 2DGS pipeline with structured initialization, luminance aware pruning, and batched CUDA kernels, achieving over 90x faster fitting and about 97% GPU utilization compared to prior implementations. We further adapt contrastive language image pretraining (CLIP) to 2DGS by reusing a frozen RGB-based transformer backbone with a lightweight splat aware input stem and a perceiver resampler, training only about 7% of the total parameters. On large DataComp subsets, GS encoders yield meaningful zero shot ImageNet-1K performance while compressing inputs 3 to 20x relative to pixels. While accuracy currently trails RGB encoders, our results establish 2DGS as a viable multimodal substrate, pinpoint architectural bottlenecks, and open a path toward representations that are both semantically powerful and transmission efficient for edge cloud learning.  
- **📝翻译**: 现代视觉语言流水线由训练在庞大图像文本语料库上的RGB视觉编码器驱动。虽然这些流水线实现了令人印象深刻的零拍摄能力和任务间的强力传输，但它们仍继承了像素领域的两个结构性低效：（i）从边缘设备传输密集的RGB图像到云端耗能且成本高昂;（ii）基于补丁的令牌化会爆炸式延长序列长度，紧张注意力预算和上下文限制。我们探索二维高斯喷溅（2DGS）作为对齐的替代视觉基底：一种紧凑、空间适应的表示，通过一组彩色各向异性高斯参数化图像。我们开发了可扩展的2DGS流水线，支持结构化初始化、亮度感知修剪和批处理CUDA内核，比以往实现实现了90倍以上的拟合速度，GPU利用率约97%。我们进一步将对比语言图像预训练（CLIP）适配为2DGS，通过重用冻结的基于RGB的变压器骨干，配备轻量级溅射感知输入主干和感知者重采样器，仅训练约7%的总参数。在大型DataComp子集上，GS编码器在相对于像素的输入压缩3倍到20倍的同时，能实现有意义的零相ImageNet-1K性能。虽然目前精度落后于RGB编码器，但我们的结果确立了2DGS作为一种可行的多模态基底，精准定位架构瓶颈，并为边缘云学习开辟了既语义强大又高效传输的表达路径。  

### [6] Gaussian splatting holography  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-25  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shuhe Zhang, Liangcai Cao  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20774v1)    
- **📝摘要**：In-line holography offers high space-bandwidth product imaging with a simplified lens-free optical system. However, in-line holographic reconstruction is troubled by twin images arising from the Hermitian symmetry of complex fields. Twin images disrupt the reconstruction in solving the ill-posed phase retrieval problem. The known parameters are less than the unknown parameters, causing phase ambiguities. State-of-the-art deep-learning or non-learning methods face challenges in balancing data fidelity with twin-image disturbance. We propose the Gaussian splatting holography (GSH) for twin-image-suppressed holographic reconstruction. GSH uses Gaussian splatting for optical field representation and compresses the number of unknown parameters by a maximum of 15 folds, transforming the original ill-posed phase retrieval into a well-posed one with reduced phase ambiguities. Additionally, the Gaussian splatting tends to form sharp patterns rather than those with noisy twin-image backgrounds as each Gaussian has a spatially slow-varying profile. Experiments show that GSH achieves constraint-free recovery for in-line holography with accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art constraint-based methods, with an average peak signal-to-noise ratio equal to 26 dB, and structure similarity equal to 0.8. Combined with total variation, GSH can be further improved, obtaining a peak signal-to-noise ratio of 31 dB, and a high compression ability of up to 15 folds.  
- **📝翻译**: 在线全息技术提供高空间带宽的产品成像，采用简化的无透镜光学系统。然而，线内全息重建受到由复场厄米对称性产生的双重图像的困扰。双重图像在解决错位相位恢复问题时会干扰重建过程。已知参数小于未知参数，导致相位模糊。最先进的深度学习或非学习方法在平衡数据忠实度与双图像干扰方面面临挑战。我们提出了高斯喷溅全息术（GSH）用于双重图像抑制全息重建。GSH使用高斯散射进行光学场表示，并将未知参数数量最多压缩15倍，将原本错误态的相位恢复转变为位位模糊度较小的良态。此外，高斯散射倾向于形成锐利的图案，而非噪声较大的双像背景，因为每个高斯分布的空间变化曲线都较慢。实验表明，GSH实现了无约束的在线全息恢复，精度可与最先进的基于约束的方法相当，平均峰值信噪比为26 dB，结构相似度为0.8。结合整体变异，GSH可以进一步提升，达到31 dB的峰值信噪比，并具备高达15倍的高度压缩能力。  

### [7] Neural-MMGS: Multi-modal Neural Gaussian Splats for Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-22  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Sitian Shen, Georgi Pramatarov, Yifu Tao, Daniele De Martini  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17762v1)    
- **📝摘要**：This paper proposes Neural-MMGS, a novel neural 3DGS framework for multimodal large-scale scene reconstruction that fuses multiple sensing modalities in a per-gaussian compact, learnable embedding. While recent works focusing on large-scale scene reconstruction have incorporated LiDAR data to provide more accurate geometric constraints, we argue that LiDAR's rich physical properties remain underexplored. Similarly, semantic information has been used for object retrieval, but could provide valuable high-level context for scene reconstruction. Traditional approaches append these properties to Gaussians as separate parameters, increasing memory usage and limiting information exchange across modalities. Instead, our approach fuses all modalities -- image, LiDAR, and semantics -- into a compact, learnable embedding that implicitly encodes optical, physical, and semantic features in each Gaussian. We then train lightweight neural decoders to map these embeddings to Gaussian parameters, enabling the reconstruction of each sensing modality with lower memory overhead and improved scalability. We evaluate Neural-MMGS on the Oxford Spires and KITTI-360 datasets. On Oxford Spires, we achieve higher-quality reconstructions, while on KITTI-360, our method reaches competitive results with less storage consumption compared with current approaches in LiDAR-based novel-view synthesis.  
- **📝翻译**: 本文提出了Neural-MMGS，一种用于多模态大规模场景重建的新型神经3DGS框架，融合多种感测模态，形成一个每高斯紧凑且可学习的嵌入。尽管近期专注于大规模场景重建的研究已利用激光雷达数据提供更精确的几何约束，但我们认为激光雷达丰富的物理特性仍未被充分探索。同样，语义信息也被用于对象检索，但也可能为场景重建提供有价值的高层次背景。传统方法将这些属性作为独立参数附加到高斯量，增加内存使用并限制不同模态间的信息交换。相反，我们的方法将所有模态——图像、激光雷达和语义——融合成一个紧凑且可学习的嵌入，隐式地编码每个高斯图中的光学、物理和语义特征。随后，我们训练轻量级神经解码器将这些嵌入映射到高斯参数，从而实现每种感测模态的重建，同时降低内存开销并提升可扩展性。我们在Oxford Spires和KITTI-360数据集上评估Neural-MMGS。在Oxford Spires上，我们实现了更高质量的重建，而在KITTI-360上，我们的方法以更低的存储消耗达到了竞争效果，相较于基于激光雷达的新颖视图合成方法。  

### [8] Improving 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression by Scene-Adaptive Lattice Vector Quantization  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-16  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Hao Xu, Xiaolin Wu, Xi Zhang  
- **📝说明**：Code available at https://github.com/hxu160/SALVQ  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13482v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is rapidly gaining popularity for its photorealistic rendering quality and real-time performance, but it generates massive amounts of data. Hence compressing 3DGS data is necessary for the cost effectiveness of 3DGS models. Recently, several anchor-based neural compression methods have been proposed, achieving good 3DGS compression performance. However, they all rely on uniform scalar quantization (USQ) due to its simplicity. A tantalizing question is whether more sophisticated quantizers can improve the current 3DGS compression methods with very little extra overhead and minimal change to the system. The answer is yes by replacing USQ with lattice vector quantization (LVQ). To better capture scene-specific characteristics, we optimize the lattice basis for each scene, improving LVQ's adaptability and R-D efficiency. This scene-adaptive LVQ (SALVQ) strikes a balance between the R-D efficiency of vector quantization and the low complexity of USQ. SALVQ can be seamlessly integrated into existing 3DGS compression architectures, enhancing their R-D performance with minimal modifications and computational overhead. Moreover, by scaling the lattice basis vectors, SALVQ can dynamically adjust lattice density, enabling a single model to accommodate multiple bit rate targets. This flexibility eliminates the need to train separate models for different compression levels, significantly reducing training time and memory consumption.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）因其逼真的渲染质量和实时性能迅速流行，但它会产生大量数据。因此，压缩3DGS数据对于3DGS模型的成本效益是必要的。近年来，提出了多种基于锚点的神经压缩方法，实现了良好的3DGS压缩性能。然而，由于纯量量子化的简单性，它们都依赖于均匀标量化（USQ）。一个引人入胜的问题是，更复杂的量化器是否能以极少额外开销和对系统最小改动的方式改进当前的3DGS压缩方法。答案是肯定的，通过用晶格矢量子化（LVQ）替换USQ。为更好地捕捉场景特性，我们优化每个场景的晶格基底，提升LVQ的适应性和R-D效率。这种场景自适应的LVQ（SALVQ）在向量量化的R-D效率和USQ的低复杂性之间取得了平衡。SALVQ可以无缝集成到现有的3DGS压缩架构中，以最小的修改和计算开销提升其研发性能。此外，通过缩放晶格基向量，SALVQ可以动态调整晶格密度，使单个模型能够支持多个比特率目标。这种灵活性消除了为不同压缩级别训练独立模型的需求，显著减少了训练时间和内存消耗。  

### [9] 3D Gaussian Modeling and Ray Marching of OpenVDB datasets for Scientific Visualization  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-14  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Isha Sharma, Dieter Schmalstieg  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11377v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussians are currently being heavily investigated for their scene modeling and compression abilities. In 3D volumes, their use is being explored for representing dense volumes as sparsely as possible. However, most of these methods begin with a memory inefficient data format. Specially in Scientific Visualization(SciVis), where most popular formats are dense-grid data structures that store every grid cell, irrespective of its contribution. OpenVDB library and data format were introduced for representing sparse volumetric data specifically for visual effects use cases such as clouds, fire, fluids etc. It avoids storing empty cells by masking them during storage. It presents an opportunity for use in SciVis, specifically as a modeling framework for conversion to 3D Gaussian particles for further compression and for a unified modeling approach for different scientific volume types. This compression head-start is non-trivial and this paper would like to present this with a rendering algorithm based on line integration implemented in OptiX8.1 for calculating 3D Gaussians contribution along a ray for optical-depth accumulation. For comparing the rendering results of our ray marching Gaussians renderer, we also implement a SciVis style primary-ray only NanoVDB HDDA based ray marcher for OpenVDB voxel grids. Finally, this paper also explores application of this Gaussian model to formats of volumes other than regular grids, such as AMR volumes and point clouds, using internal representation of OpenVDB grid class types for data hierarchy and subdivision structure.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯图目前正因其场景建模和压缩能力受到广泛研究。在三维体积中，正探索其用途以尽可能稀疏地表示密集体积。然而，大多数这些方法都以内存效率低下的数据格式为起点。特别是在科学可视化（SciVis）中，大多数流行的格式是密集网格数据结构，存储每个网格单元格，不论其贡献如何。OpenVDB库和数据格式被引入，专门用于表示稀疏体积数据，适用于云、火、流体等视觉特效场景。它通过在储存时掩盖空细胞，避免储存空细胞。它为科学可视化提供了使用机会，特别是作为一个建模框架，用于转换为三维高斯粒子以进一步压缩，并为不同科学体积类型提供统一建模方法。这种压缩领先优势并非简单，本文拟通过基于OptiX8.1实现的线积分算法，用于计算光线上的三维高斯分布，实现光学深度积累。为了比较我们的光线行进高斯渲染器的渲染结果，我们还实现了类似SciVis风格的仅主射线NanoVDB HDDA光线行进器，用于OpenVDB体素网格。最后，本文还探讨了该高斯模型在常规网格以外的体积格式上的应用，如AMR体积和点云，利用OpenVDB网格类类型的内部表示来实现数据层级结构和细分结构。  

### [10] ROSGS: Relightable Outdoor Scenes With Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-14  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Lianjun Liao, Chunhui Zhang, Tong Wu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11275v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Image data captured outdoors often exhibit unbounded scenes and unconstrained, varying lighting conditions, making it challenging to decompose them into geometry, reflectance, and illumination. Recent works have focused on achieving this decomposition using Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) or the 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) representation but remain hindered by two key limitations: the high computational overhead associated with neural networks of NeRF and the use of low-frequency lighting representations, which often result in inefficient rendering and suboptimal relighting accuracy. We propose ROSGS, a two-stage pipeline designed to efficiently reconstruct relightable outdoor scenes using the Gaussian Splatting representation. By leveraging monocular normal priors, ROSGS first reconstructs the scene's geometry with the compact 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) representation, providing an efficient and accurate geometric foundation. Building upon this reconstructed geometry, ROSGS then decomposes the scene's texture and lighting through a hybrid lighting model. This model effectively represents typical outdoor lighting by employing a spherical Gaussian function to capture the directional, high-frequency components of sunlight, while learning a radiance transfer function via Spherical Harmonic coefficients to model the remaining low-frequency skylight comprehensively. Both quantitative metrics and qualitative comparisons demonstrate that ROSGS achieves state-of-the-art performance in relighting outdoor scenes and highlight its ability to deliver superior relighting accuracy and rendering efficiency.  
- **📝翻译**: 户外拍摄的图像数据常呈现出无边界的场景和不受限制且变化的光照条件，这使得将其分解为几何形态、反射率和照明结构具有挑战性。近期研究重点通过神经辐射场（NeRF）或三维高斯溅射（3DGS）表示实现分解，但仍受两个关键限制：NeRF神经网络的高计算开销和低频照明表示，常导致渲染效率低和重照精度不佳。我们提出了ROSGS，这是一条两阶段流水线，旨在利用高斯喷溅表示高效重建可重新照明的户外场景。通过利用单眼法向先验，ROSGS首先用紧凑的二维高斯喷射（2DGS）表示重建场景几何，提供高效且准确的几何基础。基于重建后的几何，ROSGS通过混合光照模型分解场景的纹理和光照。该模型通过球面高斯函数捕捉阳光的高频方向分量，同时通过球谐系数学习辐射传递函数，全面模拟剩余的低频天窗，有效地表现了典型的户外照明。无论是定量指标还是定性比较都表明，ROSGS在户外场景重新照明方面达到了最先进的性能，并突出其卓越的再照明精度和渲染效率。  

### [11] SVR-GS: Spatially Variant Regularization for Probabilistic Masks in 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-14  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Ashkan Taghipour, Vahid Naghshin, Benjamin Southwell et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11116v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, high-quality novel view synthesis but typically relies on densification followed by pruning to optimize the number of Gaussians. Existing mask-based pruning, such as MaskGS, regularizes the global mean of the mask, which is misaligned with the local per-pixel (per-ray) reconstruction loss that determines image quality along individual camera rays. This paper introduces SVR-GS, a spatially variant regularizer that renders a per-pixel spatial mask from each Gaussian's effective contribution along the ray, thereby applying sparsity pressure where it matters: on low-importance Gaussians. We explore three spatial-mask aggregation strategies, implement them in CUDA, and conduct a gradient analysis to motivate our final design. Extensive experiments on Tanks\&Temples, Deep Blending, and Mip-NeRF360 datasets demonstrate that, on average across the three datasets, the proposed SVR-GS reduces the number of Gaussians by 1.79\(\times\) compared to MaskGS and 5.63\(\times\) compared to 3DGS, while incurring only 0.50 dB and 0.40 dB PSNR drops, respectively. These gains translate into significantly smaller, faster, and more memory-efficient models, making them well-suited for real-time applications such as robotics, AR/VR, and mobile perception.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）实现快速、高质量的新颖视图合成，但通常依赖于密度化后再修剪以优化高斯分布数。现有的基于遮罩的剪枝，如MaskGS，规范化了遮罩的全局平均值，但该均值与决定单个摄像机射线图像质量的局部每像素（每光线）重建损失不匹配。本文介绍了SVR-GS，一种空间变化的正则化器，通过每个高斯分布在射线上的有效贡献渲染每像素空间遮罩，从而在关键位置施加稀疏压力：低重要性高斯分布。我们探索三种空间掩膜聚合策略，在CUDA中实施，并进行梯度分析以激励最终设计。在Tanks\&Temples、Deep Blending和Mip-NeRF360数据集上的大量实验表明，在这三个数据集中，提出的SVR-GS平均比MaskGS减少1.79（\times\）高斯分布数，相比3DGS减少5.63/（\times\），同时PSNR分别仅下降0.50 dB和0.40 dB。这些优势转化为更小、更快且内存效率更高的模型，使其非常适合机器人、增强现实/虚拟现实和移动感知等实时应用。  

### [12] AD-GS: Alternating Densification for Sparse-Input 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-13（更新：2025-09-22）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Gurutva Patle, Nilay Girgaonkar, Nagabhushan Somraj, Rajiv Soundararajan  
- **📝说明**：SIGGRAPH Asia 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11003v2)   · [Project](https://gurutvapatle.github.io/publications/2025/ADGS.html)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown impressive results in real-time novel view synthesis. However, it often struggles under sparse-view settings, producing undesirable artifacts such as floaters, inaccurate geometry, and overfitting due to limited observations. We find that a key contributing factor is uncontrolled densification, where adding Gaussian primitives rapidly without guidance can harm geometry and cause artifacts. We propose AD-GS, a novel alternating densification framework that interleaves high and low densification phases. During high densification, the model densifies aggressively, followed by photometric loss based training to capture fine-grained scene details. Low densification then primarily involves aggressive opacity pruning of Gaussians followed by regularizing their geometry through pseudo-view consistency and edge-aware depth smoothness. This alternating approach helps reduce overfitting by carefully controlling model capacity growth while progressively refining the scene representation. Extensive experiments on challenging datasets demonstrate that AD-GS significantly improves rendering quality and geometric consistency compared to existing methods. The source code for our model can be found on our project page: https://gurutvapatle.github.io/publications/2025/ADGS.html .  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）在实时新颖视图合成方面取得了令人印象深刻的成果。然而，在稀疏视野设置下，它常常表现不佳，产生浮游物、不准确几何形状和因观测有限导致的过度拟合等不良伪影。我们发现一个关键的促成因素是不受控制的致密化，在没有指导的情况下快速添加高斯原语会损害几何结构并导致伪影。我们提出了AD-GS，一种新型交替致密化框架，交错高致密和低致密相。在高密度化过程中，模型会积极致密，随后进行基于光度损失的训练，以捕捉细粒度的场景细节。低密度化主要涉及对高斯分布进行激进的不透明度修剪，随后通过伪视图一致性和边缘感知深度平滑性来规范其几何体。这种交替方法通过精确控制模型容量增长，逐步优化场景表示，有助于减少过拟合。对复杂数据集的大量实验表明，与现有方法相比，AD-GS显著提升了渲染质量和几何一致性。我们模型的源代码可在我们的项目页面找到：https://gurutvapatle.github.io/publications/2025/ADGS.html 。  

### [13] MEGS$^{2}$: Memory-Efficient Gaussian Splatting via Spherical Gaussians and Unified Pruning  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-07（更新：2025-09-23）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiarui Chen, Yikeng Chen, Yingshuang Zou et al.  
- **📝说明**：20 pages, 8 figures. Project page at https://megs-2.github.io/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07021v2)   · [Project](https://megs-2.github.io/)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a dominant novel-view synthesis technique, but its high memory consumption severely limits its applicability on edge devices. A growing number of 3DGS compression methods have been proposed to make 3DGS more efficient, yet most only focus on storage compression and fail to address the critical bottleneck of rendering memory. To address this problem, we introduce MEGS$^{2}$, a novel memory-efficient framework that tackles this challenge by jointly optimizing two key factors: the total primitive number and the parameters per primitive, achieving unprecedented memory compression. Specifically, we replace the memory-intensive spherical harmonics with lightweight, arbitrarily oriented spherical Gaussian lobes as our color representations. More importantly, we propose a unified soft pruning framework that models primitive-number and lobe-number pruning as a single constrained optimization problem. Experiments show that MEGS$^{2}$ achieves a 50% static VRAM reduction and a 40% rendering VRAM reduction compared to existing methods, while maintaining comparable rendering quality. Project page: https://megs-2.github.io/  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种主流的新型视角合成技术，但其高内存占用严重限制了其在边缘设备上的适用性。越来越多的3DGS压缩方法被提出以提高3DGS效率，但大多数仅专注于存储压缩，未能解决渲染内存这一关键瓶颈。为解决这一问题，我们引入了MEGS$^{2}$，这是一种新型内存高效框架，通过联合优化两个关键因素——总原语数和每个原语参数——来应对这一挑战，实现了前所未有的内存压缩。具体来说，我们将需要大量内存的球面谐波替换为轻量级、任意方向的球面高斯瓣作为颜色表示。更重要的是，我们提出了一个统一的软剪枝框架，将原始数和叶数剪枝建模为一个单一的受限优化问题。实验显示，与现有方法相比，MEGS$^{2}$ 实现了 50% 的静态显存减少和 40% 的渲染显存降低，同时保持了相当的渲染质量。项目页面：https://megs-2.github.io/  

### [14] ContraGS: Codebook-Condensed and Trainable Gaussian Splatting for Fast, Memory-Efficient Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-03  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Sankeerth Durvasula, Sharanshangar Muhunthan, Zain Moustafa et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03775v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a state-of-art technique to model real-world scenes with high quality and real-time rendering. Typically, a higher quality representation can be achieved by using a large number of 3D Gaussians. However, using large 3D Gaussian counts significantly increases the GPU device memory for storing model parameters. A large model thus requires powerful GPUs with high memory capacities for training and has slower training/rendering latencies due to the inefficiencies of memory access and data movement. In this work, we introduce ContraGS, a method to enable training directly on compressed 3DGS representations without reducing the Gaussian Counts, and thus with a little loss in model quality. ContraGS leverages codebooks to compactly store a set of Gaussian parameter vectors throughout the training process, thereby significantly reducing memory consumption. While codebooks have been demonstrated to be highly effective at compressing fully trained 3DGS models, directly training using codebook representations is an unsolved challenge. ContraGS solves the problem of learning non-differentiable parameters in codebook-compressed representations by posing parameter estimation as a Bayesian inference problem. To this end, ContraGS provides a framework that effectively uses MCMC sampling to sample over a posterior distribution of these compressed representations. With ContraGS, we demonstrate that ContraGS significantly reduces the peak memory during training (on average 3.49X) and accelerated training and rendering (1.36X and 1.88X on average, respectively), while retraining close to state-of-art quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅技术（3DGS）是一种先进的技术，用于高质量且实时地建模真实世界场景。通常，使用大量三维高斯高斯可以实现更高质量的表示。然而，使用大量三维高斯计数显著增加了GPU设备存储模型参数的内存。因此，大型模型需要强大且内存容量大的GPU进行训练，且由于内存访问和数据流动的低效性，训练/渲染延迟较慢。在本研究中，我们介绍了ContraGS，这是一种能够直接在压缩后的3DGS表示上进行训练而不降低高斯计数的方法，从而略有模型质量损失。ContraGS利用码本在训练过程中紧凑地存储一组高斯参数向量，从而显著减少内存消耗。虽然码本已被证明在压缩完全训练好的3DGS模型方面非常有效，但直接使用码本表示进行训练仍是一个未解决的挑战。ContraGS通过将参数估计置于贝叶斯推断问题中，解决了在码本压缩表示中学习不可微参数的问题。为此，ContraGS提供了一个框架，有效利用MCMC抽样对这些压缩表示的后验分布进行采样。通过ContraGS，我们证明ContraGS在训练期间的峰值记忆显著降低（平均3.49倍）以及加速训练和渲染时的峰值记忆（平均为1.36倍和1.88倍），同时重新训练质量接近最先进水平。  

### [15] Efficient Geometry Compression and Communication for 3D Gaussian Splatting Point Clouds  
- **⏳发布**：2025-09-02  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Liang Xie, Yanting Li, Luyang Tang, Wei Gao  
- **📝说明**：8 pages,5 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02232v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Storage and transmission challenges in dynamic 3D scene representation based on the i3DV platform, With increasing scene complexity, the explosive growth of 3D Gaussian data volume causes excessive storage space occupancy. To address this issue, we propose adopting the AVS PCRM reference software for efficient compression of Gaussian point cloud geometry data. The strategy deeply integrates the advanced encoding capabilities of AVS PCRM into the i3DV platform, forming technical complementarity with the original rate-distortion optimization mechanism based on binary hash tables. On one hand, the hash table efficiently caches inter-frame Gaussian point transformation relationships, which allows for high-fidelity transmission within a 40 Mbps bandwidth constraint. On the other hand, AVS PCRM performs precise compression on geometry data. Experimental results demonstrate that the joint framework maintains the advantages of fast rendering and high-quality synthesis in 3D Gaussian technology while achieving significant 10\%-25\% bitrate savings on universal test sets. It provides a superior rate-distortion tradeoff solution for the storage, transmission, and interaction of 3D volumetric video.
