However, parametric curves inherently lack suitability for rendering-based multi-view optimization, necessitating a complementary representation that preserves their geometric properties while enabling differentiable rendering. We propose a novel bi-directional coupling mechanism between parametric curves and edge-oriented Gaussian components. This tight correspondence formulates a curve-aware Gaussian representation, \textbf{CurveGaussian}, that enables differentiable rendering of 3D curves, allowing direct optimization guided by multi-view evidence. Furthermore, we introduce a dynamically adaptive topology optimization framework during training to refine curve structures through linearization, merging, splitting, and pruning operations. Comprehensive evaluations on the ABC dataset and real-world benchmarks demonstrate our one-stage method's superiority over two-stage alternatives, particularly in producing cleaner and more robust reconstructions. Additionally, by directly optimizing parametric curves, our method significantly reduces the parameter count during training, achieving both higher efficiency and superior performance compared to existing approaches.  
- **📝翻译**: 本文提出了一个端到端的框架，用于直接从多视角边图重建三维参数曲线。与现有遵循顺序“边缘点云重建和参数曲线拟合”流程的两阶段方法不同，我们的单阶段方法直接从二维边缘映射优化三维参数曲线，消除了因分离阶段间固有优化差距而产生的误差累积。然而，参数曲线本质上不适合基于渲染的多视图优化，因此需要一种互补的表示方式，既保持其几何性质，又支持可微分渲染。我们提出了参数曲线与边向高斯分量之间的新颖双向耦合机制。这种紧密对应关系形成了一种曲线感知高斯表示\textbf{CurveGaussian}，使得三维曲线的可微化表示成为可能，并以多视角证据为指导进行直接优化。此外，我们在训练过程中引入了动态自适应拓扑优化框架，通过线性化、合并、拆分和剪枝作来优化曲线结构。对ABC数据集和实际基准的全面评估显示，我们的一阶段方法优于两阶段方法，尤其是在实现更清晰、更稳健的重建方面。此外，通过直接优化参数曲线，我们的方法显著减少了训练中的参数数量，实现了比现有方法更高的效率和更优的性能。  

### [4] 3DGS-IEval-15K: A Large-scale Image Quality Evaluation Database for 3D Gaussian-Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-17（更新：2025-08-09）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuke Xing, Jiarui Wang, Peizhi Niu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14642v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a promising approach for novel view synthesis, offering real-time rendering with high visual fidelity. However, its substantial storage requirements present significant challenges for practical applications. While recent state-of-the-art (SOTA) 3DGS methods increasingly incorporate dedicated compression modules, there is a lack of a comprehensive framework to evaluate their perceptual impact. Therefore we present 3DGS-IEval-15K, the first large-scale image quality assessment (IQA) dataset specifically designed for compressed 3DGS representations. Our dataset encompasses 15,200 images rendered from 10 real-world scenes through 6 representative 3DGS algorithms at 20 strategically selected viewpoints, with different compression levels leading to various distortion effects. Through controlled subjective experiments, we collect human perception data from 60 viewers. We validate dataset quality through scene diversity and MOS distribution analysis, and establish a comprehensive benchmark with 30 representative IQA metrics covering diverse types. As the largest-scale 3DGS quality assessment dataset to date, our work provides a foundation for developing 3DGS specialized IQA metrics, and offers essential data for investigating view-dependent quality distribution patterns unique to 3DGS. The database is publicly available at https://github.com/YukeXing/3DGS-IEval-15K.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种有前景的新视角合成方法，提供高视觉真实度的实时渲染。然而，其庞大的存储需求在实际应用中带来了重大挑战。尽管最新最先进的（SOTA）3DGS方法越来越多地集成了专用压缩模块，但目前尚缺乏一个全面的框架来评估其感知影响。因此，我们呈现3DGS-IEval-15K，这是首个专门为压缩3DGS表示设计的大规模图像质量评估（IQA）数据集。我们的数据集包含15,200张图像，来自10个真实场景，采用6个代表性的3DGS算法，分布在20个战略性选定的视点，不同的压缩级别导致各种畸变效果。通过受控的主观实验，我们收集了60名观众的人类感知数据。我们通过场景多样性和MOS分布分析验证数据集质量，并建立了涵盖多样类型、30个具代表性IQA指标的综合基准。作为迄今为止规模最大的3DGS质量评估数据集，我们的工作为开发3DGS专用的IQA指标奠定了基础，并为研究3DGS独有的视图依赖质量分布模式提供了关键数据。该数据库公开可访问，地址为 https://github.com/YukeXing/3DGS-IEval-15K。  

### [5] HRGS: Hierarchical Gaussian Splatting for Memory-Efficient High-Resolution 3D Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Changbai Li, Haodong Zhu, Hanlin Chen et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14229v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has made significant strides in real-time 3D scene reconstruction, but faces memory scalability issues in high-resolution scenarios. To address this, we propose Hierarchical Gaussian Splatting (HRGS), a memory-efficient framework with hierarchical block-level optimization. First, we generate a global, coarse Gaussian representation from low-resolution data. Then, we partition the scene into multiple blocks, refining each block with high-resolution data. The partitioning involves two steps: Gaussian partitioning, where irregular scenes are normalized into a bounded cubic space with a uniform grid for task distribution, and training data partitioning, where only relevant observations are retained for each block. By guiding block refinement with the coarse Gaussian prior, we ensure seamless Gaussian fusion across adjacent blocks. To reduce computational demands, we introduce Importance-Driven Gaussian Pruning (IDGP), which computes importance scores for each Gaussian and removes those with minimal contribution, speeding up convergence and reducing memory usage. Additionally, we incorporate normal priors from a pretrained model to enhance surface reconstruction quality. Our method enables high-quality, high-resolution 3D scene reconstruction even under memory constraints. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks show that HRGS achieves state-of-the-art performance in high-resolution novel view synthesis (NVS) and surface reconstruction tasks.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯溅射技术（3DGS）在实时3D场景重建方面取得了显著进展，但在高分辨率场景下仍面临内存可扩展性问题。为此，我们提出了分层高斯喷射（HRGS），这是一种具有分层块级优化的内存高效框架。首先，我们从低分辨率数据生成一个全局粗略的高斯表示。然后，我们将场景划分为多个块，每个块都用高分辨率数据进行细化。划分包括两个步骤：高斯划分，将不规则场景归一化为有界立方空间，任务分布采用均匀网格;以及训练数据划分，每个区块只保留相关观测值。通过用粗高斯先验引导块细化，我们确保相邻块间的高斯融合无缝。为了减少计算需求，我们引入了重要性驱动高斯剪枝（IDGP），该方法计算每个高斯的重要性分数，并去除贡献最小的部分，加快收敛速度并减少内存使用。此外，我们还结合预训练模型中的正常先验，以提升表面重建质量。我们的方法即使在内存限制下也能实现高质量、高分辨率的3D场景重建。对三个基准测试的广泛实验表明，HRGS在高分辨率新颖视图综合（NVS）和表面重建任务中实现了最先进的性能。  

### [6] Metropolis-Hastings Sampling for 3D Gaussian Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-15（更新：2025-10-24）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Hyunjin Kim, Haebeom Jung, Jaesik Park  
- **📝说明**：NeurIPS 2025. Project Page: https://hjhyunjinkim.github.io/MH-3DGS  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12945v2)    
- **📝摘要**：We propose an adaptive sampling framework for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) that leverages comprehensive multi-view photometric error signals within a unified Metropolis-Hastings approach. Vanilla 3DGS heavily relies on heuristic-based density-control mechanisms (e.g., cloning, splitting, and pruning), which can lead to redundant computations or premature removal of beneficial Gaussians. Our framework overcomes these limitations by reformulating densification and pruning as a probabilistic sampling process, dynamically inserting and relocating Gaussians based on aggregated multi-view errors and opacity scores. Guided by Bayesian acceptance tests derived from these error-based importance scores, our method substantially reduces reliance on heuristics, offers greater flexibility, and adaptively infers Gaussian distributions without requiring predefined scene complexity. Experiments on benchmark datasets, including Mip-NeRF360, Tanks and Temples and Deep Blending, show that our approach reduces the number of Gaussians needed, achieving faster convergence while matching or modestly surpassing the view-synthesis quality of state-of-the-art models.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了一个适用于3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）的自适应采样框架，利用统一的Metropolis-Hastings方法，利用全面的多视角光度误差信号。原版3DGS高度依赖基于启发式的密度控制机制（如克隆、分裂和修剪），这可能导致计算重复或有益高斯分布的提前移除。我们的框架通过将密度化和剪枝重新表述为一种概率抽样过程，基于聚合的多视图误差和不透明度评分动态插入和重定位高斯分布，克服了这些局限。在基于误差的重要性评分中得出的贝叶斯接受检验指导下，我们的方法大幅减少了对启发式的依赖，提供了更大的灵活性，并自适应地推断高斯分布，而无需预设场景复杂度。对基准数据集的实验，包括Mip-NeRF360、Tanks and Temples以及Deep Blending，表明我们的方法减少了所需的高斯分布量，实现了更快的收敛，同时匹配甚至略超最先进模型的视图-综合质量。  

### [7] Rasterizing Wireless Radiance Field via Deformable 2D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-15（更新：2025-06-18）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Mufan Liu, Cixiao Zhang, Qi Yang et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12787v2)   · [Project](https://evan-sudo.github.io/swiftwrf/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Modeling the wireless radiance field (WRF) is fundamental to modern communication systems, enabling key tasks such as localization, sensing, and channel estimation. Traditional approaches, which rely on empirical formulas or physical simulations, often suffer from limited accuracy or require strong scene priors. Recent neural radiance field (NeRF-based) methods improve reconstruction fidelity through differentiable volumetric rendering, but their reliance on computationally expensive multilayer perceptron (MLP) queries hinders real-time deployment. To overcome these challenges, we introduce Gaussian splatting (GS) to the wireless domain, leveraging its efficiency in modeling optical radiance fields to enable compact and accurate WRF reconstruction. Specifically, we propose SwiftWRF, a deformable 2D Gaussian splatting framework that synthesizes WRF spectra at arbitrary positions under single-sided transceiver mobility. SwiftWRF employs CUDA-accelerated rasterization to render spectra at over 100000 fps and uses a lightweight MLP to model the deformation of 2D Gaussians, effectively capturing mobility-induced WRF variations. In addition to novel spectrum synthesis, the efficacy of SwiftWRF is further underscored in its applications in angle-of-arrival (AoA) and received signal strength indicator (RSSI) prediction. Experiments conducted on both real-world and synthetic indoor scenes demonstrate that SwiftWRF can reconstruct WRF spectra up to 500x faster than existing state-of-the-art methods, while significantly enhancing its signal quality. The project page is https://evan-sudo.github.io/swiftwrf/.  
- **📝翻译**: 无线辐射场（WRF）建模是现代通信系统的基础，能够实现定位、传感和信道估计等关键任务。传统方法依赖经验公式或物理模拟，常常存在精度有限或需要强场景先验。最新的神经辐射场（基于NeRF）方法通过可微体积渲染提升重建准确性，但其对计算量高的多层感知器（MLP）查询的依赖阻碍了实时部署。为克服这些挑战，我们将高斯喷射（GS）引入无线领域，利用其在光学辐射场建模上的高效性，实现紧凑且精确的WRF重建。具体来说，我们提出了SwiftWRF，一种可变形的二维高斯散拍框架，在单侧收发机迁移率下合成任意位置的WRF光谱。SwiftWRF 采用 CUDA 加速光栅化技术，以超过 100000 fps 渲染光谱，并使用轻量级 MLP 模拟二维高斯变形，有效捕捉由移动性引起的 WRF 变化。除了新颖的频谱合成外，SwiftWRF的效能还在到达角（AoA）和接收信号强度指示器（RSSI）预测中的应用中得到了进一步强调。在真实世界和合成室内场景上的实验表明，SwiftWRF能够以高于现有先进方法高达500倍的速度重建WRF频谱，同时显著提升信号质量。项目页面是 https://evan-sudo.github.io/swiftwrf/。  

### [8] GraphGSOcc: Semantic-Geometric Graph Transformer with Dynamic-Static Decoupling for 3D Gaussian Splatting-based Occupancy Prediction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-13（更新：2025-07-02）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Ke Song, Yunhe Wu, Chunchit Siu, Huiyuan Xiong  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14825v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Addressing the task of 3D semantic occupancy prediction for autonomous driving, we tackle two key issues in existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods: (1) unified feature aggregation neglecting semantic correlations among similar categories and across regions, (2) boundary ambiguities caused by the lack of geometric constraints in MLP iterative optimization and (3) biased issues in dynamic-static object coupling optimization. We propose the GraphGSOcc model, a novel framework that combines semantic and geometric graph Transformer and decouples dynamic-static objects optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting-based Occupancy Prediction. We propose the Dual Gaussians Graph Attenntion, which dynamically constructs dual graph structures: a geometric graph adaptively calculating KNN search radii based on Gaussian poses, enabling large-scale Gaussians to aggregate features from broader neighborhoods while compact Gaussians focus on local geometric consistency; a semantic graph retaining top-M highly correlated nodes via cosine similarity to explicitly encode semantic relationships within and across instances. Coupled with the Multi-scale Graph Attention framework, fine-grained attention at lower layers optimizes boundary details, while coarsegrained attention at higher layers models object-level topology. On the other hand, we decouple dynamic and static objects by leveraging semantic probability distributions and design a Dynamic-Static Decoupled Gaussian Attention mechanism to optimize the prediction performance for both dynamic objects and static scenes. GraphGSOcc achieves state-ofthe-art performance on the SurroundOcc-nuScenes, Occ3D-nuScenes, OpenOcc and KITTI occupancy benchmarks. Experiments on the SurroundOcc dataset achieve an mIoU of 25.20%, reducing GPU memory to 6.8 GB, demonstrating a 1.97% mIoU improvement and 13.7% memory reduction compared to GaussianWorld.  
- **📝翻译**: 针对自动驾驶的三维语义占用预测任务，我们解决了现有三维高斯散拍（3DGS）方法中的两个关键问题：（1）统一特征聚合忽略相似类别间及跨区域的语义相关性，（2）由于MLP迭代优化缺乏几何约束导致的边界模糊性;（3）动态静态对象耦合优化中的偏颇问题。我们提出了GraphGSOcc模型，这是一个结合语义和几何图变换器，并对动态-静态对象进行解耦的3D高斯散落占用预测的新框架。我们提出了对偶高斯图趋向，它动态构造了对偶图结构：一个基于高斯姿势自适应计算KNN搜索半径的几何图，使大尺度高斯图能够聚合更广泛邻域的特征，而紧致高斯图则专注于局部几何一致性;一个保留top-M节点高度相关（通过余弦相似度）的语义图，以显式编码实例内及实例间的语义关系。结合多尺度图关注框架，低层的细粒度关注优化边界细节，而高层的粗细关注则建模对象级拓扑。另一方面，我们通过语义概率分布将动态对象与静态对象解耦，设计动态-静态解耦高斯注意力机制，以优化动态对象和静态场景的预测性能。GraphGSOcc在SurroundOcc-nuScenes、Occ3D-nuScenes、OpenOcc和KITTI占用基准测试上实现了最先进的性能。SurroundOcc 数据集的实验实现了 25.20% 的 mIoU，将 GPU 内存减少至 6.8 GB，显示出与 GaussianWorld 相比，mIoU 提升了 1.97%，内存减少了 13.7%。  

### [9] DynaSplat: Dynamic-Static Gaussian Splatting with Hierarchical Motion Decomposition for Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Junli Deng, Ping Shi, Qipei Li, Jinyang Guo  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09836v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Reconstructing intricate, ever-changing environments remains a central ambition in computer vision, yet existing solutions often crumble before the complexity of real-world dynamics. We present DynaSplat, an approach that extends Gaussian Splatting to dynamic scenes by integrating dynamic-static separation and hierarchical motion modeling. First, we classify scene elements as static or dynamic through a novel fusion of deformation offset statistics and 2D motion flow consistency, refining our spatial representation to focus precisely where motion matters. We then introduce a hierarchical motion modeling strategy that captures both coarse global transformations and fine-grained local movements, enabling accurate handling of intricate, non-rigid motions. Finally, we integrate physically-based opacity estimation to ensure visually coherent reconstructions, even under challenging occlusions and perspective shifts. Extensive experiments on challenging datasets reveal that DynaSplat not only surpasses state-of-the-art alternatives in accuracy and realism but also provides a more intuitive, compact, and efficient route to dynamic scene reconstruction.  
- **📝翻译**: 重建错综复杂且不断变化的环境仍是计算机视觉的核心目标，但现有解决方案常常在现实世界动态的复杂性面前崩溃。我们介绍了DynaSplat，这是一种通过整合动态静态分离和分层运动建模，将高斯喷射扩展到动态场景的方法。首先，我们通过新颖融合变形偏移统计和二维运动流一致性，将场景元素分为静态或动态，精炼空间表征以精准聚焦运动重要部位。随后，我们引入了一种分层运动建模策略，既捕捉粗略全局变换，也捕捉细粒度局部运动，从而实现对复杂非刚性运动的准确处理。最后，我们整合了基于物理的不透明度估计，确保即使在具有挑战性遮挡和视角变化的情况下，也能实现视觉上的连贯重建。对复杂数据集的大量实验表明，DynaSplat 不仅在准确性和真实性上超越了最先进的替代方案，还提供了更直观、更紧凑且高效的动态场景重建路径。  

### [10] Self-Supervised Multi-Part Articulated Objects Modeling via Deformable Gaussian Splatting and Progressive Primitive Segmentation  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haowen Wang, Xiaoping Yuan, Zhao Jin et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09663v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Articulated objects are ubiquitous in everyday life, and accurate 3D representations of their geometry and motion are critical for numerous applications. However, in the absence of human annotation, existing approaches still struggle to build a unified representation for objects that contain multiple movable parts. We introduce DeGSS, a unified framework that encodes articulated objects as deformable 3D Gaussian fields, embedding geometry, appearance, and motion in one compact representation. Each interaction state is modeled as a smooth deformation of a shared field, and the resulting deformation trajectories guide a progressive coarse-to-fine part segmentation that identifies distinct rigid components, all in an unsupervised manner. The refined field provides a spatially continuous, fully decoupled description of every part, supporting part-level reconstruction and precise modeling of their kinematic relationships. To evaluate generalization and realism, we enlarge the synthetic PartNet-Mobility benchmark and release RS-Art, a real-to-sim dataset that pairs RGB captures with accurately reverse-engineered 3D models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing methods in both accuracy and stability.  
- **📝翻译**: 关节物体在日常生活中无处不在，其几何和运动的精确三维表示对于许多应用至关重要。然而，由于缺乏人工注释，现有方法仍在努力构建包含多个可移动部件的对象的统一表示。我们介绍DeGSS，这是一个统一框架，将关节物体编码为可变形的三维高斯场，将几何、外观和运动嵌入于一个紧凑的表示中。每个相互作用态都被建模为共享场的平滑变形，由此产生的变形轨迹引导一个逐步的粗细零件分割，识别不同的刚性分量，且全部以无监督的方式进行。精细场提供了空间连续、完全解耦的每个部分描述，支持部分层级重建和对其运动学关系的精确建模。为了评估泛化性和真实性，我们扩大了合成的PartNet-Mobility基准测试，并发布了RS-Art，这是一个将RGB捕获与精确逆向工程的3D模型结合起来的实测到模拟数据集。大量实验表明，我们的方法在准确性和稳定性方面均优于现有方法。  

### [11] Gaussian Herding across Pens: An Optimal Transport Perspective on Global Gaussian Reduction for 3DGS  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-11（更新：2025-09-23）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Tao Wang, Mengyu Li, Geduo Zeng, Cheng Meng, Qiong Zhang  
- **📝说明**：26 pages, 15 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09534v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a powerful technique for radiance field rendering, but it typically requires millions of redundant Gaussian primitives, overwhelming memory and rendering budgets. Existing compaction approaches address this by pruning Gaussians based on heuristic importance scores, without global fidelity guarantee. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel optimal transport perspective that casts 3DGS compaction as global Gaussian mixture reduction. Specifically, we first minimize the composite transport divergence over a KD-tree partition to produce a compact geometric representation, and then decouple appearance from geometry by fine-tuning color and opacity attributes with far fewer Gaussian primitives. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that our method (i) yields negligible loss in rendering quality (PSNR, SSIM, LPIPS) compared to vanilla 3DGS with only 10% Gaussians; and (ii) consistently outperforms state-of-the-art 3DGS compaction techniques. Notably, our method is applicable to any stage of vanilla or accelerated 3DGS pipelines, providing an efficient and agnostic pathway to lightweight neural rendering. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/DrunkenPoet/GHAP  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种强大的辐射场渲染技术，但它通常需要数百万个冗余的高斯原元，导致内存和渲染预算极为庞大。现有的紧致方法通过基于启发式重要性评分剪枝高斯分布来解决这个问题，而没有全局保真度保证。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了一种新颖的最优输运视角，将3DGS紧缩视为全局高斯混合约简。具体来说，我们首先在KD树划分上最小化复合传输散度，以生成紧凑的几何表示，然后通过微调颜色和不透明度属性，使用更少的高斯原语，将外观与几何解耦。基准数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法（i）相比仅有10%高斯分布的原版3DGS相比，渲染质量（PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS）损失极小;并且（ii）持续优于最先进的3DGS压缩技术。值得注意的是，我们的方法适用于原版或加速3DGS流水线的任何阶段，提供了高效且中立的轻量级神经渲染路径。该代码公开于 https://github.com/DrunkenPoet/GHAP  

### [12] TinySplat: Feedforward Approach for Generating Compact 3D Scene Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zetian Song, Jiaye Fu, Jiaqi Zhang et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09479v1)    
- **📝摘要**：The recent development of feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) presents a new paradigm to reconstruct 3D scenes. Using neural networks trained on large-scale multi-view datasets, it can directly infer 3DGS representations from sparse input views. Although the feedforward approach achieves high reconstruction speed, it still suffers from the substantial storage cost of 3D Gaussians. Existing 3DGS compression methods relying on scene-wise optimization are not applicable due to architectural incompatibilities. To overcome this limitation, we propose TinySplat, a complete feedforward approach for generating compact 3D scene representations. Built upon standard feedforward 3DGS methods, TinySplat integrates a training-free compression framework that systematically eliminates key sources of redundancy. Specifically, we introduce View-Projection Transformation (VPT) to reduce geometric redundancy by projecting geometric parameters into a more compact space. We further present Visibility-Aware Basis Reduction (VABR), which mitigates perceptual redundancy by aligning feature energy along dominant viewing directions via basis transformation. Lastly, spatial redundancy is addressed through an off-the-shelf video codec. Comprehensive experimental results on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that TinySplat achieves over 100x compression for 3D Gaussian data generated by feedforward methods. Compared to the state-of-the-art compression approach, we achieve comparable quality with only 6% of the storage size. Meanwhile, our compression framework requires only 25% of the encoding time and 1% of the decoding time.  
- **📝翻译**: 近期发展的前馈三维高斯喷射（3DGS）为重建三维场景提供了新范式。利用训练于大规模多视图数据集的神经网络，它可以直接从稀疏的输入视图推导出3DGS的表示。尽管前馈方法实现了较高的重建速度，但仍面临三维高斯算法的高存储成本。由于架构不兼容，现有依赖场景优化的3DGS压缩方法不适用。为克服这一限制，我们提出了TinySplat，一种完整的前馈方法，用于生成紧凑的三维场景表示。TinySplat 基于标准前馈 3DGS 方法，集成了一个无训练的压缩框架，系统性地消除了关键的冗余来源。具体来说，我们引入了视图-投影变换（VPT），通过将几何参数投影到更紧凑的空间中来减少几何冗余。我们进一步介绍了可视化感知基准缩减（VABR），通过基底变换使特征能量沿主导视野方向对齐，从而减少感知冗余。最后，空间冗余通过现成的视频编码器解决。对多个基准数据集的综合实验结果表明，TinySplat 对通过前馈方法生成的三维高斯数据实现超过 100 倍的压缩。与最先进的压缩方法相比，我们仅用6%的存储容量就能实现相当的质量。而我们的压缩框架只需编码时间的25%，解码时间的1%。  

### [13] Speedy Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting: Fast Rendering and Compression of Dynamic Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-09  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Allen Tu, Haiyang Ying, Alex Hanson et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project Page: https://speede3dgs.github.io/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07917v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent extensions of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to dynamic scenes achieve high-quality novel view synthesis by using neural networks to predict the time-varying deformation of each Gaussian. However, performing per-Gaussian neural inference at every frame poses a significant bottleneck, limiting rendering speed and increasing memory and compute requirements. In this paper, we present Speedy Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (SpeeDe3DGS), a general pipeline for accelerating the rendering speed of dynamic 3DGS and 4DGS representations by reducing neural inference through two complementary techniques. First, we propose a temporal sensitivity pruning score that identifies and removes Gaussians with low contribution to the dynamic scene reconstruction. We also introduce an annealing smooth pruning mechanism that improves pruning robustness in real-world scenes with imprecise camera poses. Second, we propose GroupFlow, a motion analysis technique that clusters Gaussians by trajectory similarity and predicts a single rigid transformation per group instead of separate deformations for each Gaussian. Together, our techniques accelerate rendering by $10.37\times$, reduce model size by $7.71\times$, and shorten training time by $2.71\times$ on the NeRF-DS dataset. SpeeDe3DGS also improves rendering speed by $4.20\times$ and $58.23\times$ on the D-NeRF and HyperNeRF vrig datasets. Our methods are modular and can be integrated into any deformable 3DGS or 4DGS framework.  
- **📝翻译**: 近期将三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）扩展到动态场景，通过神经网络预测每个高斯分布随时间变化的变形，实现了高质量的新颖视图合成。然而，在每帧进行全高斯神经推断会带来重大瓶颈，限制渲染速度并增加内存和计算需求。本文介绍了快速可变形3D高斯喷溅（SpeeDe3DGS），这是一种通过两种互补技术减少神经推断，加速动态3DGS和4DGS表示渲染速度的通用流水线。首先，我们提出了一种时间敏感度剪枝评分，用于识别并去除对动态场景重建贡献较低的高斯分布。我们还引入了退火光滑剪枝机制，提升了在摄像机姿态不精确的现实场景中剪枝的稳健性。其次，我们提出GroupFlow，一种运动分析技术，通过轨迹相似性对高斯分布进行聚类，并在每个高斯分布预测单个刚性变换，而非为每个高斯分布单独的变形。我们的技术共同加速了NeRF-DS数据集渲染10.37美元，模型规模减少7.71倍倍，训练时间缩短2.71倍倍。SpeeDe3DGS还在D-NeRF和HyperNeRF虚拟设备数据集上提升了渲染速度4.20美元和58.23倍倍。我们的方法模块化，可以集成到任何可变形的3维空间系统或4次空间系统框架中。  

### [14] DSG-World: Learning a 3D Gaussian World Model from Dual State Videos  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-05  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Wenhao Hu, Xuexiang Wen, Xi Li, Gaoang Wang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05217v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Building an efficient and physically consistent world model from limited observations is a long standing challenge in vision and robotics. Many existing world modeling pipelines are based on implicit generative models, which are hard to train and often lack 3D or physical consistency. On the other hand, explicit 3D methods built from a single state often require multi-stage processing-such as segmentation, background completion, and inpainting-due to occlusions. To address this, we leverage two perturbed observations of the same scene under different object configurations. These dual states offer complementary visibility, alleviating occlusion issues during state transitions and enabling more stable and complete reconstruction. In this paper, we present DSG-World, a novel end-to-end framework that explicitly constructs a 3D Gaussian World model from Dual State observations. Our approach builds dual segmentation-aware Gaussian fields and enforces bidirectional photometric and semantic consistency. We further introduce a pseudo intermediate state for symmetric alignment and design collaborative co-pruning trategies to refine geometric completeness. DSG-World enables efficient real-to-simulation transfer purely in the explicit Gaussian representation space, supporting high-fidelity rendering and object-level scene manipulation without relying on dense observations or multi-stage pipelines. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong generalization to novel views and scene states, highlighting the effectiveness of our approach for real-world 3D reconstruction and simulation.  
- **📝翻译**: 从有限的观测中构建一个高效且物理一致的世界模型，是视觉和机器人领域长期以来的挑战。许多现有的世界建模流程基于隐式生成模型，这些模型难以训练，且常缺乏三维或物理一致性。另一方面，基于单一状态构建的显式三维方法通常需要多阶段处理——如分割、背景补全和修复——因为存在遮挡问题。为此，我们利用同一场景在不同物体配置下的两个扰动观测。这些双态提供了互补的可见性，缓解状态转换时的遮挡问题，并实现更稳定和完整的重建。本文介绍了DSG-World，一种全新的端到端框架，通过对偶态观测显式构建三维高斯世界模型。我们的方法构建了对偶分割感知的高斯场，并强制实现双向光度和语义一致性。我们进一步引入了一种伪中间状态用于对称对齐，并设计协作共剪的模式以优化几何完备性。DSG-World 实现了纯粹在显式高斯表示空间内高效的实物到仿真传输，支持高保真渲染和对象级场景作，无需依赖密集观测或多级流水线。大量实验证明了我们对新视角和场景状态的强烈推广，凸显了我们方法在现实世界三维重建和仿真中的有效性。  

### [15] FlexGS: Train Once, Deploy Everywhere with Many-in-One Flexible 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-04  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Hengyu Liu, Yuehao Wang, Chenxin Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：CVPR 2025; Project Page: https://flexgs.github.io  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04174v1)   · [Project](https://flexgs.github.io.)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has enabled various applications in 3D scene representation and novel view synthesis due to its efficient rendering capabilities. However, 3DGS demands relatively significant GPU memory, limiting its use on devices with restricted computational resources. Previous approaches have focused on pruning less important Gaussians, effectively compressing 3DGS but often requiring a fine-tuning stage and lacking adaptability for the specific memory needs of different devices. In this work, we present an elastic inference method for 3DGS. Given an input for the desired model size, our method selects and transforms a subset of Gaussians, achieving substantial rendering performance without additional fine-tuning. We introduce a tiny learnable module that controls Gaussian selection based on the input percentage, along with a transformation module that adjusts the selected Gaussians to complement the performance of the reduced model. Comprehensive experiments on ZipNeRF, MipNeRF and Tanks\&Temples scenes demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Code is available at https://flexgs.github.io.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）因其高效的渲染能力，使得3D场景表示和新颖视图合成的多种应用成为可能。然而，3DGS需要相对较大的GPU内存，限制了其在计算资源有限设备上的应用。以往的方法主要集中于修剪较不重要的高斯分布，有效压缩3DGS，但通常需要微调阶段，且缺乏针对不同设备特定内存需求的适应性。在本研究中，我们提出了一种3重重体的弹性推断方法。在输入所需模型尺寸时，我们的方法选择并转换高斯子集，实现显著的渲染性能，无需额外微调。我们引入了一个微小可学习模块，基于输入百分比控制高斯选择，以及一个变换模块，调整所选高斯以补充约简模型的性能。在ZipNeRF、MipNeRF和坦克/寺庙场景上的全面实验展示了我们方法的有效性。代码可在 https://flexgs.github.io 获取。  

### [16] Multi-Spectral Gaussian Splatting with Neural Color Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-03  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Lukas Meyer, Josef Grün, Maximilian Weiherer et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03407v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present MS-Splatting -- a multi-spectral 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) framework that is able to generate multi-view consistent novel views from images of multiple, independent cameras with different spectral domains. In contrast to previous approaches, our method does not require cross-modal camera calibration and is versatile enough to model a variety of different spectra, including thermal and near-infra red, without any algorithmic changes.   Unlike existing 3DGS-based frameworks that treat each modality separately (by optimizing per-channel spherical harmonics) and therefore fail to exploit the underlying spectral and spatial correlations, our method leverages a novel neural color representation that encodes multi-spectral information into a learned, compact, per-splat feature embedding. A shallow multi-layer perceptron (MLP) then decodes this embedding to obtain spectral color values, enabling joint learning of all bands within a unified representation.   Our experiments show that this simple yet effective strategy is able to improve multi-spectral rendering quality, while also leading to improved per-spectra rendering quality over state-of-the-art methods. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this new technique in agricultural applications to render vegetation indices, such as normalized difference vegetation index (NDVI).  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍MS-Splatting——一种多光谱3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）框架，能够从多个独立相机的图像生成多视角一致的新颖视图，这些图像具有不同光谱域。与以往方法不同，我们的方法无需跨模态相机校准，且足够灵活，能够模拟多种不同光谱，包括热成像和近红外红光，无需任何算法修改。  与现有基于3DGS的框架（通过优化每通道球谐波）分别处理每种模态，因此未能利用底层的频谱和空间相关性不同，我们的方法利用一种新颖的神经色彩表示，将多光谱信息编码成一个学习式的、紧凑的每splat特征嵌入中。然后，浅层多层感知器（MLP）对嵌入进行解码，获得光谱色彩值，从而实现统一表示中对所有频段的联合学习。  我们的实验表明，这一简单而有效的策略能够提升多光谱渲染质量，同时也使得比最先进方法更优的每光谱渲染质量。我们展示了该新技术在农业应用中生成植被指数的有效性，例如归一化差分植被指数（NDVI）。  

### [17] Voyager: Real-Time Splatting City-Scale Gaussians on Resource-Constrained Devices  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-03（更新：2025-09-26）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zheng Liu, He Zhu, Xinyang Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02774v3)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) is an emerging technique for photorealistic 3D scene rendering. However, rendering city-scale 3DGS scenes on resource-constrained mobile devices in real-time remains a significant challenge due to two compute-intensive stages: level-of-detail (LoD) search and rasterization.   In this paper, we propose Voyager, an effective solution to accelerate city-scale 3DGS rendering on mobile devices. Our key insight is that, under normal user motion, the number of newly visible Gaussians within the view frustum remains roughly constant. Leveraging this temporal correlation, we propose a temporal-aware LoD search to identify the necessary Gaussians for the remaining rendering stages. For the remaining rendering process, we accelerate the bottleneck stage, rasterization, via preemptive $α$-filtering. With all optimizations above, our system can deliver low-latency, city-scale 3DGS rendering on mobile devices. Compared to existing solutions, Voyager achieves up to 6.6$\times$ speedup and 85\% energy savings with superior rendering quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）是一种新兴的写实三维场景渲染技术。然而，由于涉及两个计算密集阶段：细节层级搜索（LoD）和光栅化，在资源受限的移动设备上实时渲染城市尺度的3DGS场景仍是一个重大挑战。  本文提出了Voyager，这是一种有效的解决方案，用于加速移动设备上城市尺度的3DGS渲染。我们的关键见解是，在正常用户运动下，视锥体内新可见的高斯分布数大致保持不变。利用这种时间相关性，我们提出了一种时间感知的 LoD 搜索方法，以识别剩余渲染阶段所需的高斯分布。在剩余的渲染过程中，我们通过抢占式的 $α $ 过滤加速瓶颈阶段的光栅化。通过上述所有优化，我们的系统可以在移动设备上实现低延迟、城市规模的3DGS渲染。与现有方案相比，Voyager实现了最高6.6美元\时间$的加速和85%的节能，且渲染质量更优。  

### [18] GSCodec Studio: A Modular Framework for Gaussian Splat Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-02  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Sicheng Li, Chengzhen Wu, Hao Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：Repository of the project: https://github.com/JasonLSC/GSCodec_Studio  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01822v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting and its extension to 4D dynamic scenes enable photorealistic, real-time rendering from real-world captures, positioning Gaussian Splats (GS) as a promising format for next-generation immersive media. However, their high storage requirements pose significant challenges for practical use in sharing, transmission, and storage. Despite various studies exploring GS compression from different perspectives, these efforts remain scattered across separate repositories, complicating benchmarking and the integration of best practices. To address this gap, we present GSCodec Studio, a unified and modular framework for GS reconstruction, compression, and rendering. The framework incorporates a diverse set of 3D/4D GS reconstruction methods and GS compression techniques as modular components, facilitating flexible combinations and comprehensive comparisons. By integrating best practices from community research and our own explorations, GSCodec Studio supports the development of compact representation and compression solutions for static and dynamic Gaussian Splats, namely our Static and Dynamic GSCodec, achieving competitive rate-distortion performance in static and dynamic GS compression. The code for our framework is publicly available at https://github.com/JasonLSC/GSCodec_Studio , to advance the research on Gaussian Splats compression.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅及其对4D动态场景的扩展，使得从真实世界捕获的实时渲染成为可能，使高斯喷溅（GS）成为下一代沉浸式媒体的前景。然而，其高存储需求在共享、传输和存储等实际应用中带来了重大挑战。尽管有多项研究从不同角度探讨GS压缩，但这些努力分散在不同的仓库中，增加了基准测试和最佳实践整合的复杂性。为弥补这一空白，我们推出了GSCodec Studio，一个统一且模块化的GS重建、压缩和渲染框架。该框架集成了多样化的3D/4D地球重建方法和GS压缩技术作为模块化组件，便于灵活组合和全面比较。通过整合社区研究和自身探索的最佳实践，GSCodec Studio支持开发静态和动态高斯分布的紧凑表示与压缩解决方案，即我们的静态与动态GSCodec，实现静态和动态GS压缩的竞争性速率失真性能。我们框架的代码已公开于 https://github.com/JasonLSC/GSCodec_Studio，以推动高斯Splats压缩的研究。  

### [19] CountingFruit: Language-Guided 3D Fruit Counting with Semantic Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-06-01（更新：2025-08-07）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Fengze Li, Yangle Liu, Jieming Ma et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01109v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Accurate 3D fruit counting in orchards is challenging due to heavy occlusion, semantic ambiguity between fruits and surrounding structures, and the high computational cost of volumetric reconstruction. Existing pipelines often rely on multi-view 2D segmentation and dense volumetric sampling, which lead to accumulated fusion errors and slow inference. We introduce FruitLangGS, a language-guided 3D fruit counting framework that reconstructs orchard-scale scenes using an adaptive-density Gaussian Splatting pipeline with radius-aware pruning and tile-based rasterization, enabling scalable 3D representation. During inference, compressed CLIP-aligned semantic vectors embedded in each Gaussian are filtered via a dual-threshold cosine similarity mechanism, retrieving Gaussians relevant to target prompts while suppressing common distractors (e.g., foliage), without requiring retraining or image-space masks. The selected Gaussians are then sampled into dense point clouds and clustered geometrically to estimate fruit instances, remaining robust under severe occlusion and viewpoint variation. Experiments on nine different orchard-scale datasets demonstrate that FruitLangGS consistently outperforms existing pipelines in instance counting recall, avoiding multi-view segmentation fusion errors and achieving up to 99.7% recall on Pfuji-Size_Orch2018 orchard dataset. Ablation studies further confirm that language-conditioned semantic embedding and dual-threshold prompt filtering are essential for suppressing distractors and improving counting accuracy under heavy occlusion. Beyond fruit counting, the same framework enables prompt-driven 3D semantic retrieval without retraining, highlighting the potential of language-guided 3D perception for scalable agricultural scene understanding.  
- **📝翻译**: 由于严重的遮挡、果实与周围结构之间的语义模糊以及体积重建的高计算成本，在果园中准确进行三维水果计数具有挑战性。现有的流水线通常依赖多视图二维分割和密集的体积采样，导致融合误差累积和推断速度变慢。我们介绍FruitLangGS，一个语言引导的三维水果计数框架，利用自适应密度高斯喷涂流水线重建果园规模场景，支持半径感知修剪和基于瓦片的光栅化，实现可扩展的三维表示。在推断过程中，嵌入每个高斯图中的压缩CLIP对齐语义矢量通过双阈值余弦相似机制进行过滤，检索与目标提示相关的高斯矢，同时抑制常见的干扰因素（如植被），无需重新训练或图像空间掩码。选定的高斯分布被采样到密集点云中，并按几何聚类方式估算果实实例，在严重遮挡和视角变化下保持稳健。在九个不同果园规模数据集上的实验表明，FruitLangGS在实例计数回忆率方面持续优于现有管道，避免多视图分割融合错误，并在Pfuji-Size_Orch2018果园数据集上实现高达99.7%的回忆率。消融研究进一步证实，语言条件语义嵌入和双阈提示过滤对于抑制干扰因素和提高重闭塞下计数准确性至关重要。除了水果计数，同一框架还支持基于提示的三维语义检索而无需重新训练，凸显了语言引导三维感知在可扩展农业场景理解中的潜力。  


## May 2025

### [1] Adaptive Voxelization for Transform coding of 3D Gaussian splatting data  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-30  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Chenjunjie Wang, Shashank N. Sridhara, Eduardo Pavez, Antonio Ortega, Cheng Chang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00271v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present a novel compression framework for 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) data that leverages transform coding tools originally developed for point clouds. Contrary to existing 3DGS compression methods, our approach can produce compressed 3DGS models at multiple bitrates in a computationally efficient way. Point cloud voxelization is a discretization technique that point cloud codecs use to improve coding efficiency while enabling the use of fast transform coding algorithms. We propose an adaptive voxelization algorithm tailored to 3DGS data, to avoid the inefficiencies introduced by uniform voxelization used in point cloud codecs. We ensure the positions of larger volume Gaussians are represented at high resolution, as these significantly impact rendering quality. Meanwhile, a low-resolution representation is used for dense regions with smaller Gaussians, which have a relatively lower impact on rendering quality. This adaptive voxelization approach significantly reduces the number of Gaussians and the bitrate required to encode the 3DGS data. After voxelization, many Gaussians are moved or eliminated. Thus, we propose to fine-tune/recolor the remaining 3DGS attributes with an initialization that can reduce the amount of retraining required. Experimental results on pre-trained datasets show that our proposed compression framework outperforms existing methods.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了一种全新的3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）数据压缩框架，利用最初为点云开发的变换编码工具。与现有的3DGS压缩方法不同，我们的方法能够以计算效率高的方式生成多比特率的压缩3DGS模型。点云体素化是一种离散化技术，点云编解码器用于提高编码效率，同时支持快速变换编码算法的使用。我们提出了一种针对3DGS数据的自适应体素化算法，以避免点云编解码器中使用的均匀体素化带来的低效。我们确保体积较大的高斯分布位置以高分辨率表示，因为这对渲染质量有显著影响。同时，对于高斯分布较小的密集区域则采用低分辨率表示，这对渲染质量的影响相对较小。这种自适应体素化方法显著减少了高斯分布量和编码3DGS数据所需的比特率。体素化后，许多高斯体被移动或消除。因此，我们建议通过初始化对剩余的3DGS属性进行微调/重新着色，以减少所需的再训练量。对预训练数据集的实验结果显示，我们提出的压缩框架优于现有方法。  

### [2] TC-GS: A Faster Gaussian Splatting Module Utilizing Tensor Cores  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-30（更新：2025-10-11）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zimu Liao, Jifeng Ding, Siwei Cui et al.  
- **📝说明**：15 pages, 6 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24796v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) renders pixels by rasterizing Gaussian primitives, where conditional alpha-blending dominates the computational cost in the rendering pipeline. This paper proposes TC-GS, an algorithm-independent universal module that expands the applicability of Tensor Core (TCU) for 3DGS, leading to substantial speedups and seamless integration into existing 3DGS optimization frameworks. The key innovation lies in mapping alpha computation to matrix multiplication, fully utilizing otherwise idle TCUs in existing 3DGS implementations. TC-GS provides plug-and-play acceleration for existing top-tier acceleration algorithms and integrates seamlessly with rendering pipeline designs, such as Gaussian compression and redundancy elimination algorithms. Additionally, we introduce a global-to-local coordinate transformation to mitigate rounding errors from quadratic terms of pixel coordinates caused by Tensor Core half-precision computation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method maintains rendering quality while providing an additional 2.18x speedup over existing Gaussian acceleration algorithms, thereby achieving a total acceleration of up to 5.6x.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射（3DGS）通过光栅化高斯原语渲染像素，其中条件α混合主导了渲染流程中的计算成本。本文提出了TC-GS，一个与算法无关的通用模块，扩展了张量核心（TCU）在3DGS中的适用性，从而实现了显著的加速和与现有3DGS优化框架的无缝集成。关键创新在于将α计算映射到矩阵乘法，充分利用现有3DGS实现中原本闲置的TCU。TC-GS为现有顶级加速算法提供即插即用加速，并与渲染流水线设计无缝集成，如高斯压缩和冗余消除算法。此外，我们引入了全局到局部坐标变换，以减少因张量核心半精度计算引起的像素坐标二次项的舍入误差。大量实验表明，我们的方法在保持渲染质量的同时，比现有高斯加速算法额外提升2.18倍，从而实现最高5.6倍的总加速。  

### [3] ZPressor: Bottleneck-Aware Compression for Scalable Feed-Forward 3DGS  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-29（更新：2025-11-05）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Weijie Wang, Donny Y. Chen, Zeyu Zhang et al.  
- **📝说明**：NeurIPS 2025, Project Page: https://lhmd.top/zpressor, Code: https://github.com/ziplab/ZPressor  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23734v3)   · [Project](https://lhmd.top/zpressor.)  
- **📝摘要**：Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) models have recently emerged as a promising solution for novel view synthesis, enabling one-pass inference without the need for per-scene 3DGS optimization. However, their scalability is fundamentally constrained by the limited capacity of their models, leading to degraded performance or excessive memory consumption as the number of input views increases. In this work, we analyze feed-forward 3DGS frameworks through the lens of the Information Bottleneck principle and introduce ZPressor, a lightweight architecture-agnostic module that enables efficient compression of multi-view inputs into a compact latent state $Z$ that retains essential scene information while discarding redundancy. Concretely, ZPressor enables existing feed-forward 3DGS models to scale to over 100 input views at 480P resolution on an 80GB GPU, by partitioning the views into anchor and support sets and using cross attention to compress the information from the support views into anchor views, forming the compressed latent state $Z$. We show that integrating ZPressor into several state-of-the-art feed-forward 3DGS models consistently improves performance under moderate input views and enhances robustness under dense view settings on two large-scale benchmarks DL3DV-10K and RealEstate10K. The video results, code and trained models are available on our project page: https://lhmd.top/zpressor.  
- **📝翻译**: 前馈三维高斯喷射（3DGS）模型近年来成为一种有前景的新型视图合成解决方案，实现了无需逐场景3DGS优化的单遍推断。然而，其可扩展性受到模型容量有限的根本限制，随着输入视图数量增加，性能下降或内存消耗过高。本研究通过信息瓶颈原则分析前馈3DGS框架，介绍ZPressor，一种轻量级架构无关模块，能够高效压缩多视图输入到紧凑的潜态$Z$中，保留关键场景信息同时舍弃冗余。具体来说，ZPressor 通过将视图划分为锚集和支持集，并利用交叉关注将支持视图中的信息压缩为锚视图，形成压缩潜态 $Z$，从而使现有的前馈 3DGS 模型能够在 80GB GPU 上扩展到 480P 分辨率下的超过 100 个输入视图。我们证明，将ZPressor集成到多个最先进的前馈3DGS模型中，在两个大型基准测试DL3DV-10K和RealEstate10K上，在中等输入视图下持续提升性能，并在密集视图设置下增强鲁棒性。视频结果、代码和训练好的模型可在我们的项目页面 https://lhmd.top/zpressor 获取。  

### [4] LODGE: Level-of-Detail Large-Scale Gaussian Splatting with Efficient Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-29（更新：2025-10-29）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jonas Kulhanek, Marie-Julie Rakotosaona, Fabian Manhardt et al.  
- **📝说明**：NeurIPS 2025; Web: https://lodge-gs.github.io/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23158v2)    
- **📝摘要**：In this work, we present a novel level-of-detail (LOD) method for 3D Gaussian Splatting that enables real-time rendering of large-scale scenes on memory-constrained devices. Our approach introduces a hierarchical LOD representation that iteratively selects optimal subsets of Gaussians based on camera distance, thus largely reducing both rendering time and GPU memory usage. We construct each LOD level by applying a depth-aware 3D smoothing filter, followed by importance-based pruning and fine-tuning to maintain visual fidelity. To further reduce memory overhead, we partition the scene into spatial chunks and dynamically load only relevant Gaussians during rendering, employing an opacity-blending mechanism to avoid visual artifacts at chunk boundaries. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both outdoor (Hierarchical 3DGS) and indoor (Zip-NeRF) datasets, delivering high-quality renderings with reduced latency and memory requirements.  
- **📝翻译**: 本研究提出了一种新的三维高斯喷溅细节层级（LOD）方法，使得在内存受限的设备上实时渲染大尺度场景成为可能。我们的方法引入了一种分层LOD表示，能够根据摄像机距离迭代选择高斯分布的最优子集，从而大幅减少渲染时间和GPU内存占用。我们通过应用深度感知的3D平滑滤波器构建每个LOD级别，随后基于重要性进行剪枝和微调以保持视觉真实性。为了进一步降低内存开销，我们将场景划分为空间块，并在渲染时动态加载相关的高斯分布，采用不透明度混合机制以避免块边界出现视觉伪影。我们的方法在户外（分层3DGS）和室内（Zip-NeRF）数据集上都实现了最先进的性能，提供高质量渲染，同时降低了延迟和内存需求。  

### [5] 3DGS Compression with Sparsity-guided Hierarchical Transform Coding  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-28  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Hao Xu, Xiaolin Wu, Xi Zhang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22908v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has gained popularity for its fast and high-quality rendering, but it has a very large memory footprint incurring high transmission and storage overhead. Recently, some neural compression methods, such as Scaffold-GS, were proposed for 3DGS but they did not adopt the approach of end-to-end optimized analysis-synthesis transforms which has been proven highly effective in neural signal compression. Without an appropriate analysis transform, signal correlations cannot be removed by sparse representation. Without such transforms the only way to remove signal redundancies is through entropy coding driven by a complex and expensive context modeling, which results in slower speed and suboptimal rate-distortion (R-D) performance. To overcome this weakness, we propose Sparsity-guided Hierarchical Transform Coding (SHTC), the first end-to-end optimized transform coding framework for 3DGS compression. SHTC jointly optimizes the 3DGS, transforms and a lightweight context model. This joint optimization enables the transform to produce representations that approach the best R-D performance possible. The SHTC framework consists of a base layer using KLT for data decorrelation, and a sparsity-coded enhancement layer that compresses the KLT residuals to refine the representation. The enhancement encoder learns a linear transform to project high-dimensional inputs into a low-dimensional space, while the decoder unfolds the Iterative Shrinkage-Thresholding Algorithm (ISTA) to reconstruct the residuals. All components are designed to be interpretable, allowing the incorporation of signal priors and fewer parameters than black-box transforms. This novel design significantly improves R-D performance with minimal additional parameters and computational overhead.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射（3DGS）因其快速且高质量的渲染而广受欢迎，但其内存占用非常大，导致传输和存储开销较高。最近，一些神经压缩方法，如Scaffold-GS，被提出用于3DGS，但它们并未采用已被证明在神经信号压缩中非常有效的端到端优化分析-综合转换方法。没有适当的分析变换，信号相关性无法通过稀疏表示来消除。没有此类变换，消除信号冗余的唯一方法是通过由复杂且昂贵的上下文建模驱动的熵编码，这会导致速度变慢和速率失真（R-D）性能不优。为克服这一弱点，我们提出了空间性引导层次变换编码（SHTC），这是首个用于3DGS压缩的端到端优化变换编码框架。SHTC联合优化3DGS、变换和轻量级上下文模型。这种联合优化使变换能够生成接近最佳R-D性能的表示。SHTC框架由一个使用KLT进行数据去相关的基层和一个稀疏编码的增强层组成，该层通过压缩KLT残差以精炼表示。增强编码器学习线性变换，将高维输入投射到低维空间，而解码器则展开迭代收缩阈值算法（ISTA）以重建残差。所有组件都设计为可解释性，允许包含信号先验，参数比黑箱变换更少。这一新颖设计显著提升了R-D性能，且新增参数和计算开销极小。  

### [6] CCL-LGS: Contrastive Codebook Learning for 3D Language Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-26（更新：2025-08-14）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Lei Tian, Xiaomin Li, Liqian Ma et al.  
- **📝说明**：ICCV 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20469v2)   · [Project](https://epsilontl.github.io/CCL-LGS/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Recent advances in 3D reconstruction techniques and vision-language models have fueled significant progress in 3D semantic understanding, a capability critical to robotics, autonomous driving, and virtual/augmented reality. However, methods that rely on 2D priors are prone to a critical challenge: cross-view semantic inconsistencies induced by occlusion, image blur, and view-dependent variations. These inconsistencies, when propagated via projection supervision, deteriorate the quality of 3D Gaussian semantic fields and introduce artifacts in the rendered outputs. To mitigate this limitation, we propose CCL-LGS, a novel framework that enforces view-consistent semantic supervision by integrating multi-view semantic cues. Specifically, our approach first employs a zero-shot tracker to align a set of SAM-generated 2D masks and reliably identify their corresponding categories. Next, we utilize CLIP to extract robust semantic encodings across views. Finally, our Contrastive Codebook Learning (CCL) module distills discriminative semantic features by enforcing intra-class compactness and inter-class distinctiveness. In contrast to previous methods that directly apply CLIP to imperfect masks, our framework explicitly resolves semantic conflicts while preserving category discriminability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CCL-LGS outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods. Our project page is available at https://epsilontl.github.io/CCL-LGS/.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D重建技术和视觉语言模型的最新进展推动了3D语义理解的重大进展，这一能力对机器人学、自动驾驶和虚拟/增强现实至关重要。然而，依赖二维先验的方法面临一个关键挑战：由遮挡、图像模糊和视角依赖变化引起的交叉视角语义不一致。这些不一致通过投影监督传播，会降低三维高斯语义场的质量，并在渲染输出中引入伪影。为缓解这一限制，我们提出了CCL-LGS，一种通过整合多视角语义线索来强制视角一致语义监督的新框架。具体来说，我们的方法首先使用零次追踪器对齐一组SAM生成的二维掩模，并可靠地识别其对应类别。接下来，我们利用CLIP在视图间提取强健的语义编码。最后，我们的对比码本学习（CCL）模块通过强制执行类内紧凑性和类间特征性，提炼了判别语义特征。与以往直接将CLIP应用于不完美掩码的方法不同，我们的框架在保持范畴可区分性的同时，明确解决了语义冲突。大量实验表明，CCL-LGS的表现优于以往的先进方法。我们的项目页面可在 https://epsilontl.github.io/CCL-LGS/ 访问。  

### [7] HaloGS: Loose Coupling of Compact Geometry and Gaussian Splats for 3D Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-26  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Changjian Jiang, Kerui Ren, Linning Xu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20267v1)    
- **📝摘要**：High fidelity 3D reconstruction and rendering hinge on capturing precise geometry while preserving photo realistic detail. Most existing methods either fuse these goals into a single cumbersome model or adopt hybrid schemes whose uniform primitives lead to a trade off between efficiency and fidelity. In this paper, we introduce HaloGS, a dual representation that loosely couples coarse triangles for geometry with Gaussian primitives for appearance, motivated by the lightweight classic geometry representations and their proven efficiency in real world applications. Our design yields a compact yet expressive model capable of photo realistic rendering across both indoor and outdoor environments, seamlessly adapting to varying levels of scene complexity. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method yields both compact, accurate geometry and high fidelity renderings, especially in challenging scenarios where robust geometric structure make a clear difference.  
- **📝翻译**: 高精度3D重建和渲染依赖于捕捉精确几何体，同时保持照片级真实细节。大多数现有方法要么将这些目标融合成一个繁琐的单一模型，要么采用混合方案，其统一原语导致效率与忠实度之间的权衡。本文介绍了HaloGS，这是一种对偶表示法，松散地将几何的粗三角形与高斯原形的表象耦合，灵感来自经典几何的轻量化表现及其在实际应用中已被验证的高效性。我们的设计打造了一个紧凑而富有表现力的模型，能够在室内外环境中实现照片级真实渲染，并无缝适应不同场景复杂度。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法既能实现紧凑、准确的几何形状，也能实现高精度渲染，尤其是在需要稳健几何结构的复杂场景下。  

### [8] VPGS-SLAM: Voxel-based Progressive 3D Gaussian SLAM in Large-Scale Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-25  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Tianchen Deng, Wenhua Wu, Junjie He et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18992v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting has recently shown promising results in dense visual SLAM. However, existing 3DGS-based SLAM methods are all constrained to small-room scenarios and struggle with memory explosion in large-scale scenes and long sequences. To this end, we propose VPGS-SLAM, the first 3DGS-based large-scale RGBD SLAM framework for both indoor and outdoor scenarios. We design a novel voxel-based progressive 3D Gaussian mapping method with multiple submaps for compact and accurate scene representation in large-scale and long-sequence scenes. This allows us to scale up to arbitrary scenes and improves robustness (even under pose drifts). In addition, we propose a 2D-3D fusion camera tracking method to achieve robust and accurate camera tracking in both indoor and outdoor large-scale scenes. Furthermore, we design a 2D-3D Gaussian loop closure method to eliminate pose drift. We further propose a submap fusion method with online distillation to achieve global consistency in large-scale scenes when detecting a loop. Experiments on various indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate the superiority and generalizability of the proposed framework. The code will be open source on https://github.com/dtc111111/vpgs-slam.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅最近在密集视觉SLAM中取得了有前景的效果。然而，现有基于3DGS的SLAM方法都受限于小房间场景，且在大规模场景和长序列中存在内存爆炸性问题。为此，我们提出了VPGS-SLAM，这是首个基于3DGS的大规模RGBD SLAM框架，适用于室内和室外场景。我们设计了一种基于体素的新型渐进三维高斯映射方法，具有多个子映射，实现了大尺度和长序列场景中的紧凑且准确的场景表示。这让我们能够扩展到任意场景，并提升了鲁棒性（即使在姿势漂移下也是如此）。此外，我们提出了一种2D-3D融合相机跟踪方法，以实现室内外大比例尺场景中稳健且准确的相机跟踪。此外，我们设计了一种2D-3D高斯环闭合方法以消除姿态漂移。我们还提出了一种带有在线蒸馏的子图融合方法，以在检测环路时实现大尺度场景的全局一致性。在各种室内和室外数据集上的实验证明了该框架的优越性和可推广性。代码将在 https://github.com/dtc111111/vpgs-slam 开源。  

### [9] SplatCo: Structure-View Collaborative Gaussian Splatting for Detail-Preserving Rendering of Large-Scale Unbounded Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-23  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haihong Xiao, Jianan Zou, Yuxin Zhou, Ying He, Wenxiong Kang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17951v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present SplatCo, a structure-view collaborative Gaussian splatting framework for high-fidelity rendering of complex outdoor environments. SplatCo builds upon two novel components: (1) a cross-structure collaboration module that combines global tri-plane representations, which capture coarse scene layouts, with local context grid features that represent fine surface details. This fusion is achieved through a novel hierarchical compensation strategy, ensuring both global consistency and local detail preservation; and (2) a cross-view assisted training strategy that enhances multi-view consistency by synchronizing gradient updates across viewpoints, applying visibility-aware densification, and pruning overfitted or inaccurate Gaussians based on structural consistency. Through joint optimization of structural representation and multi-view coherence, SplatCo effectively reconstructs fine-grained geometric structures and complex textures in large-scale scenes. Comprehensive evaluations on 13 diverse large-scale scenes, including Mill19, MatrixCity, Tanks & Temples, WHU, and custom aerial captures, demonstrate that SplatCo consistently achieves higher reconstruction quality than state-of-the-art methods, with PSNR improvements of 1-2 dB and SSIM gains of 0.1 to 0.2. These results establish a new benchmark for high-fidelity rendering of large-scale unbounded scenes. Code and additional information are available at https://github.com/SCUT-BIP-Lab/SplatCo.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍SplatCo，一个结构视图协作高斯喷涂框架，用于高保真渲染复杂户外环境。SplatCo基于两个新颖组件构建：（1）一个跨结构协作模块，结合了捕捉粗略场景布局的全局三平面表示与代表精细表面细节的局部上下文网格特征。这种融合通过一种新颖的层级补偿策略实现，确保了全局一致性和局部细节的保存;以及（2）一种交叉视角辅助训练策略，通过同步跨视角的梯度更新、应用可视感知密度化以及基于结构一致性修剪过拟合或不准确的高斯分布，从而增强多视图一致性。通过结构表示和多视角一致性的联合优化，SplatCo 能够有效重建大尺度场景中的细粒度几何结构和复杂纹理。对13个多样化大型场景的全面评估，包括Mill19、MatrixCity、Tanks & Temples、WHU以及定制航拍，显示SplatCo始终实现了优于最先进方法的重建质量，PSNR提升1-2 dB，SSIM提升为0.1至0.2。这些结果为大规模无界场景的高保真渲染树立了新的标杆。代码及更多信息可在 https://github.com/SCUT-BIP-Lab/SplatCo 获取。  

### [10] R3GS: Gaussian Splatting for Robust Reconstruction and Relocalization in Unconstrained Image Collections  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-21  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xu yan, Zhaohui Wang, Rong Wei et al.  
- **📝说明**：7 pages, 4 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15294v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We propose R3GS, a robust reconstruction and relocalization framework tailored for unconstrained datasets. Our method uses a hybrid representation during training. Each anchor combines a global feature from a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a local feature encoded by the multiresolution hash grids [2]. Subsequently, several shallow multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) predict the attributes of each Gaussians, including color, opacity, and covariance. To mitigate the adverse effects of transient objects on the reconstruction process, we ffne-tune a lightweight human detection network. Once ffne-tuned, this network generates a visibility map that efffciently generalizes to other transient objects (such as posters, banners, and cars) with minimal need for further adaptation. Additionally, to address the challenges posed by sky regions in outdoor scenes, we propose an effective sky-handling technique that incorporates a depth prior as a constraint. This allows the inffnitely distant sky to be represented on the surface of a large-radius sky sphere, signiffcantly reducing ffoaters caused by errors in sky reconstruction. Furthermore, we introduce a novel relocalization method that remains robust to changes in lighting conditions while estimating the camera pose of a given image within the reconstructed 3DGS scene. As a result, R3GS significantly enhances rendering ffdelity, improves both training and rendering efffciency, and reduces storage requirements. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to baseline methods on in-the-wild datasets. The code will be made open-source following the acceptance of the paper.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了R3GS，一个为无约束数据集量身定制的稳健重建与重新定位框架。我们的方法在培训中使用混合表示。每个锚点将卷积神经网络（CNN）中的全局特征与多分辨率哈希网格编码的局部特征结合起来[2]。随后，若干浅层多层感知器（MLP）预测每个高斯分布图的属性，包括颜色、不透明度和协方差。为了减轻短暂物体对重建过程的不利影响，我们进行了轻量级人体探测网络的ffne调谐。一旦ffne调优，该网络生成一个可视性图，高效地推广到其他瞬态对象（如海报、横幅和汽车），且几乎无需进一步适应。此外，为了解决户外场景中天空区域带来的挑战，我们提出了一种有效的天空处理技术，将深度先验作为约束。这使得无限遥远的天空可以在大半径天空球的表面上被表现，显著减少了因天空重建错误引起的偏重。此外，我们引入了一种新颖的重新定位方法，能够在重建后的3DGS场景中估计给定图像的摄像机姿态时，对光照条件的变化保持稳健性。因此，R3GS显著提升了渲染的精细，提升了训练和渲染效率，并减少了存储需求。与野外数据集的基线方法相比，我们的方法实现了最先进的性能。论文被接受后，代码将开源。  

### [11] Building LOD Representation for 3D Urban Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-21  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shanshan Pan, Runze Zhang, Yilin Liu, Minglun Gong, Hui Huang  
- **📝说明**：ISPRS Journal of Photogrammetry and Remote Sensing 2025 (Patent Protected); Project page: https://vcc.tech/research/2025/LODRecon  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15190v1)    
- **📝摘要**：The advances in 3D reconstruction technology, such as photogrammetry and LiDAR scanning, have made it easier to reconstruct accurate and detailed 3D models for urban scenes. Nevertheless, these reconstructed models often contain a large number of geometry primitives, making interactive manipulation and rendering challenging, especially on resource-constrained devices like virtual reality platforms. Therefore, the generation of appropriate levels-of-detail (LOD) representations for these models is crucial. Additionally, automatically reconstructed 3D models tend to suffer from noise and lack semantic information. Dealing with these issues and creating LOD representations that are robust against noise while capturing the semantic meaning present significant challenges. In this paper, we propose a novel algorithm to address these challenges. We begin by analysing the properties of planar primitives detected from the input and group these primitives into multiple level sets by forming meaningful 3D structures. These level sets form the nodes of our innovative LOD-Tree. By selecting nodes at appropriate depths within the LOD-Tree, different LOD representations can be generated. Experimental results on real and complex urban scenes demonstrate the merits of our approach in generating clean, accurate, and semantically meaningful LOD representations.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维重建技术的进步，如摄影测量和激光雷达扫描，使得重建城市场景中准确且细致的3D模型变得更容易。然而，这些重建模型通常包含大量几何原语，使得交互作和渲染变得具有挑战性，尤其是在资源有限的虚拟现实平台上。因此，为这些模型生成合适的细节层级（LOD）表示至关重要。此外，自动重建的3D模型往往存在噪声和语义信息。解决这些问题并创建既能抵抗噪声又能捕捉语义意义的LOD表示，面临重大挑战。本文提出了一种新颖算法来应对这些挑战。我们首先分析从输入中检测到的平面原语的性质，并通过构建有意义的三维结构将这些原语分组为多个层级集。这些层级集构成了我们创新LOD-Tree的节点。通过在LOD树中选择适当深度的节点，可以生成不同的LOD表示。对真实复杂城市场景的实验结果展示了我们方法在生成干净、准确且语义意义丰富的LOD表示方面的优点。  

### [12] A Novel Benchmark and Dataset for Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting with Gaussian Point Cloud Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-21  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Kangli Wang, Shihao Li, Qianxi Yi, Wei Gao  
- **📝说明**：22 pages, 13 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18197v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recently, immersive media and autonomous driving applications have significantly advanced through 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), which offers high-fidelity rendering and computational efficiency. Despite these advantages, 3DGS as a display-oriented representation requires substantial storage due to its numerous Gaussian attributes. Current compression methods have shown promising results but typically neglect the compression of Gaussian spatial positions, creating unnecessary bitstream overhead. We conceptualize Gaussian primitives as point clouds and propose leveraging point cloud compression techniques for more effective storage. AI-based point cloud compression demonstrates superior performance and faster inference compared to MPEG Geometry-based Point Cloud Compression (G-PCC). However, direct application of existing models to Gaussian compression may yield suboptimal results, as Gaussian point clouds tend to exhibit globally sparse yet locally dense geometric distributions that differ from conventional point cloud characteristics. To address these challenges, we introduce GausPcgc for Gaussian point cloud geometry compression along with a specialized training dataset GausPcc-1K. Our work pioneers the integration of AI-based point cloud compression into Gaussian compression pipelines, achieving superior compression ratios. The framework complements existing Gaussian compression methods while delivering significant performance improvements. All code, data, and pre-trained models will be publicly released to facilitate further research advances in this field.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，沉浸式媒体和自动驾驶应用通过3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）取得了显著进步，该技术提供了高保真渲染和计算效率。尽管有这些优势，3DGS作为面向显示的表示器，由于其众多高斯属性，仍需要大量存储空间。当前的压缩方法已显示出有希望的效果，但通常忽视了高斯空间位置的压缩，导致不必要的比特流开销。我们将高斯原语概念化为点云，并提出利用点云压缩技术以实现更有效的存储。基于AI的点云压缩相比基于MPEG的点云压缩（G-PCC）展现出更优的性能和更快的推断速度。然而，直接将现有模型应用于高斯压缩可能得出不理想的结果，因为高斯点云往往表现出全局稀疏但局部密集的几何分布，这些分布与传统点云特征不同。为应对这些挑战，我们引入了用于高斯点云几何压缩的GausPcgc，以及一个专门的训练数据集GausPcc-1K。我们的工作开创性地将基于人工智能的点云压缩集成到高斯压缩流水线中，实现了更优异的压缩比。该框架补充了现有的高斯压缩方法，同时实现了显著的性能提升。所有代码、数据和预训练模型都将公开发布，以促进该领域的进一步研究进展。  

### [13] EA-3DGS: Efficient and Adaptive 3D Gaussians with Highly Enhanced Quality for outdoor scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-16  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jianlin Guo, Haihong Xiao, Wenxiong Kang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10787v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Efficient scene representations are essential for many real-world applications, especially those involving spatial measurement. Although current NeRF-based methods have achieved impressive results in reconstructing building-scale scenes, they still suffer from slow training and inference speeds due to time-consuming stochastic sampling. Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has demonstrated excellent performance with its high-quality rendering and real-time speed, especially for objects and small-scale scenes. However, in outdoor scenes, its point-based explicit representation lacks an effective adjustment mechanism, and the millions of Gaussian points required often lead to memory constraints during training. To address these challenges, we propose EA-3DGS, a high-quality real-time rendering method designed for outdoor scenes. First, we introduce a mesh structure to regulate the initialization of Gaussian components by leveraging an adaptive tetrahedral mesh that partitions the grid and initializes Gaussian components on each face, effectively capturing geometric structures in low-texture regions. Second, we propose an efficient Gaussian pruning strategy that evaluates each 3D Gaussian's contribution to the view and prunes accordingly. To retain geometry-critical Gaussian points, we also present a structure-aware densification strategy that densifies Gaussian points in low-curvature regions. Additionally, we employ vector quantization for parameter quantization of Gaussian components, significantly reducing disk space requirements with only a minimal impact on rendering quality. Extensive experiments on 13 scenes, including eight from four public datasets (MatrixCity-Aerial, Mill-19, Tanks \& Temples, WHU) and five self-collected scenes acquired through UAV photogrammetry measurement from SCUT-CA and plateau regions, further demonstrate the superiority of our method.  
- **📝翻译**: 高效的场景表示对于许多现实应用至关重要，尤其是在涉及空间测量的应用中。尽管当前基于NeRF的方法在重建建筑规模场景方面取得了令人印象深刻的成果，但由于耗时的随机采样，它们仍存在训练和推断速度较慢的问题。最近，3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）凭借其高质量渲染和实时速度，在物体和小规模场景中展现了卓越的性能。然而，在户外场景中，其基于点的显式表示缺乏有效的调整机制，且所需的数百万高斯点常导致训练时内存受限。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了EA-3DGS，一种为户外场景设计的高质量实时渲染方法。首先，我们引入了一种网格结构，利用自适应四面体网格来调节高斯分量的初始化，该网格划分并在每个面初始化高斯分量，有效捕捉低纹理区域的几何结构。其次，我们提出一种高效的高斯剪枝策略，评估每个三维高斯对视图的贡献并相应修剪。为保留几何临界高斯点，我们还提出了一种结构感知密度化策略，使低曲率区域的高斯点趋密。此外，我们采用矢量量化对高斯分量的参数量子化，显著减少了磁盘空间需求，且对渲染质量影响极小。对13个场景进行了大量实验，其中包括来自四个公共数据集（MatrixCity-Aerial、Mill-19、Tanks \ & Temples、WHU）的八个场景，以及通过无人机摄影测量从SCUT-CA和高原地区自采集的五个场景，进一步证明了我们方法的优越性。  

### [14] GaussianFormer3D: Multi-Modal Gaussian-based Semantic Occupancy Prediction with 3D Deformable Attention  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-15  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Lingjun Zhao, Sizhe Wei, James Hays, Lu Gan  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10685v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D semantic occupancy prediction is critical for achieving safe and reliable autonomous driving. Compared to camera-only perception systems, multi-modal pipelines, especially LiDAR-camera fusion methods, can produce more accurate and detailed predictions. Although most existing works utilize a dense grid-based representation, in which the entire 3D space is uniformly divided into discrete voxels, the emergence of 3D Gaussians provides a compact and continuous object-centric representation. In this work, we propose a multi-modal Gaussian-based semantic occupancy prediction framework utilizing 3D deformable attention, named as GaussianFormer3D. We introduce a voxel-to-Gaussian initialization strategy to provide 3D Gaussians with geometry priors from LiDAR data, and design a LiDAR-guided 3D deformable attention mechanism for refining 3D Gaussians with LiDAR-camera fusion features in a lifted 3D space. We conducted extensive experiments on both on-road and off-road datasets, demonstrating that our GaussianFormer3D achieves high prediction accuracy that is comparable to state-of-the-art multi-modal fusion-based methods with reduced memory consumption and improved efficiency.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维语义占用预测对于实现安全可靠的自动驾驶至关重要。与仅摄像头感知系统相比，多模态流水线，尤其是激光雷达相机融合方法，能够产生更准确、更细致的预测。尽管大多数现有研究采用密集的网格表示，即整个三维空间均匀划分为离散体素，但三维高斯分布的出现提供了紧凑且连续的以对象为中心的表示。本研究提出一个多模态基于高斯的语义占有预测框架，利用三维可变形注意力，称为GaussianFormer3D。我们引入体素到高斯初始化策略，利用LiDAR数据为三维高斯图形提供几何先验，并设计了一种激光雷达引导的三维可变形注意力机制，用于在提升三维空间中精炼带有激光雷达相机融合特征的三维高斯分布。我们在公路和越野数据集上进行了大量实验，证明我们的GaussianFormer3D实现了与基于多模态的先进方法相当的高度预测精度，同时降低了内存消耗并提高了效率。  

### [15] ControlGS: Consistent Structural Compression Control for Deployment-Aware Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-15（更新：2025-11-07）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Fengdi Zhang, Yibao Sun, Hongkun Cao, Ruqi Huang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10473v3)   · [Project](https://zhang-fengdi.github.io/ControlGS/)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a highly deployable real-time method for novel view synthesis. In practice, it requires a universal, consistent control mechanism that adjusts the trade-off between rendering quality and model compression without scene-specific tuning, enabling automated deployment across different device performances and communication bandwidths. In this work, we present ControlGS, a control-oriented optimization framework that maps the trade-off between Gaussian count and rendering quality to a continuous, scene-agnostic, and highly responsive control axis. Extensive experiments across a wide range of scene scales and types (from small objects to large outdoor scenes) demonstrate that, by adjusting a globally unified control hyperparameter, ControlGS can flexibly generate models biased toward either structural compactness or high fidelity, regardless of the specific scene scale or complexity, while achieving markedly higher rendering quality with the same or fewer Gaussians compared to potential competing methods. Project page: https://zhang-fengdi.github.io/ControlGS/  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）是一种高度可部署的实时新颖视图合成方法。实际上，它需要一种通用且一致的控制机制，在渲染质量与模型压缩之间进行权衡，而无需针对特定场景进行调校，从而实现在不同设备性能和通信带宽间的自动部署。在本研究中，我们提出了ControlGS，一个以控制为导向的优化框架，将高斯计数与渲染质量之间的权衡映射到一个连续的、场景无关且高度响应的控制轴上。跨越多种场景尺度和类型（从小型物体到大型户外场景）的大量实验表明，通过调整全球统一的控制超参数，ControlGS能够灵活生成偏向结构紧凑或高保真度的模型，无论具体场景规模或复杂度如何，同时在相同或更少的高斯分布下，渲染质量显著优于潜在竞争方法。项目页面：https://zhang-fengdi.github.io/ControlGS/  

### [16] Neural Video Compression using 2D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-14  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Lakshya Gupta, Imran N. Junejo  
- **📝说明**：9 pages, 8 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09324v1)    
- **📝摘要**：The computer vision and image processing research community has been involved in standardizing video data communications for the past many decades, leading to standards such as AVC, HEVC, VVC, AV1, AV2, etc. However, recent groundbreaking works have focused on employing deep learning-based techniques to replace the traditional video codec pipeline to a greater affect. Neural video codecs (NVC) create an end-to-end ML-based solution that does not rely on any handcrafted features (motion or edge-based) and have the ability to learn content-aware compression strategies, offering better adaptability and higher compression efficiency than traditional methods. This holds a great potential not only for hardware design, but also for various video streaming platforms and applications, especially video conferencing applications such as MS-Teams or Zoom that have found extensive usage in classrooms and workplaces. However, their high computational demands currently limit their use in real-time applications like video conferencing. To address this, we propose a region-of-interest (ROI) based neural video compression model that leverages 2D Gaussian Splatting. Unlike traditional codecs, 2D Gaussian Splatting is capable of real-time decoding and can be optimized using fewer data points, requiring only thousands of Gaussians for decent quality outputs as opposed to millions in 3D scenes. In this work, we designed a video pipeline that speeds up the encoding time of the previous Gaussian splatting-based image codec by 88% by using a content-aware initialization strategy paired with a novel Gaussian inter-frame redundancy-reduction mechanism, enabling Gaussian splatting to be used for a video-codec solution, the first of its kind solution in this neural video codec space.  
- **📝翻译**: 计算机视觉与图像处理研究界在过去几十年里一直致力于视频数据通信的标准化，促成了AVC、HEVC、VVC、AV1、AV2等标准的诞生。然而，近期的突破性工作集中在利用基于深度学习的技术，以更大程度取代传统的视频编解码器管道。神经视频编解码器（NVC）创建了端到端的机器学习解决方案，不依赖任何手工设计的功能（基于运动或边缘），能够学习内容感知的压缩策略，提供比传统方法更好的适应性和更高的压缩效率。这不仅在硬件设计上具有巨大潜力，也适用于各种视频流媒体平台和应用，尤其是像MS-Teams或Zoom这样的视频会议应用，这些应用在课堂和工作场所中被广泛应用。然而，其高计算需求目前限制了它们在视频会议等实时应用中的应用。为此，我们提出了一种基于兴趣区域（ROI）的神经视频压缩模型，利用二维高斯散拍技术。与传统编解码器不同，2D高斯喷射能够实现实时解码，并且可以用更少的数据点进行优化，只需数千个高斯就能获得不错的输出，而在3D场景中则需要数百万个。在这项工作中，我们设计了一种视频流水线，通过内容感知初始化策略结合新颖的高斯帧间冗余减少机制，将之前基于高斯的涂片图像编码器编码时间提升了88%，使高斯喷涂技术能够用于视频编解码器解决方案，这是该神经视频编码器领域的首个解决方案。  

### [17] ADC-GS: Anchor-Driven Deformable and Compressed Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-13  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：He Huang, Qi Yang, Mufan Liu, Yiling Xu, Zhu Li  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08196v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Existing 4D Gaussian Splatting methods rely on per-Gaussian deformation from a canonical space to target frames, which overlooks redundancy among adjacent Gaussian primitives and results in suboptimal performance. To address this limitation, we propose Anchor-Driven Deformable and Compressed Gaussian Splatting (ADC-GS), a compact and efficient representation for dynamic scene reconstruction. Specifically, ADC-GS organizes Gaussian primitives into an anchor-based structure within the canonical space, enhanced by a temporal significance-based anchor refinement strategy. To reduce deformation redundancy, ADC-GS introduces a hierarchical coarse-to-fine pipeline that captures motions at varying granularities. Moreover, a rate-distortion optimization is adopted to achieve an optimal balance between bitrate consumption and representation fidelity. Experimental results demonstrate that ADC-GS outperforms the per-Gaussian deformation approaches in rendering speed by 300%-800% while achieving state-of-the-art storage efficiency without compromising rendering quality. The code is released at https://github.com/H-Huang774/ADC-GS.git.  
- **📝翻译**: 现有的四维高斯散画方法依赖于从规范空间到目标帧的全高斯变形，这忽视了相邻高斯原语之间的冗余性，导致性能不理想。为解决这一限制，我们提出了锚驱动可变形压缩高斯散画（ADC-GS），这是一种紧凑高效的动态场景重建表示。具体来说，ADC-GS将高斯原语组织为典范空间中的锚结构，并通过基于时间显著性的锚点细化策略进行增强。为减少变形冗余，ADC-GS引入了一条从粗到细的分层流水线，捕捉不同粒度的运动。此外，采用速率失真优化以实现比特率消耗与表示真实度之间的最佳平衡。实验结果表明，ADC-GS在渲染速度上比高斯变形方法高300%-800%，同时实现了最先进的存储效率，同时不影响渲染质量。代码于 https://github.com/H-Huang774/ADC-GS.git 年发布。  

### [18] Virtualized 3D Gaussians: Flexible Cluster-based Level-of-Detail System for Real-Time Rendering of Composed Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-10  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xijie Yang, Linning Xu, Lihan Jiang, Dahua Lin, Bo Dai  
- **📝说明**：project page: https://xijie-yang.github.io/V3DG/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06523v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables the reconstruction of intricate digital 3D assets from multi-view images by leveraging a set of 3D Gaussian primitives for rendering. Its explicit and discrete representation facilitates the seamless composition of complex digital worlds, offering significant advantages over previous neural implicit methods. However, when applied to large-scale compositions, such as crowd-level scenes, it can encompass numerous 3D Gaussians, posing substantial challenges for real-time rendering. To address this, inspired by Unreal Engine 5's Nanite system, we propose Virtualized 3D Gaussians (V3DG), a cluster-based LOD solution that constructs hierarchical 3D Gaussian clusters and dynamically selects only the necessary ones to accelerate rendering speed. Our approach consists of two stages: (1) Offline Build, where hierarchical clusters are generated using a local splatting method to minimize visual differences across granularities, and (2) Online Selection, where footprint evaluation determines perceptible clusters for efficient rasterization during rendering. We curate a dataset of synthetic and real-world scenes, including objects, trees, people, and buildings, each requiring 0.1 billion 3D Gaussians to capture fine details. Experiments show that our solution balances rendering efficiency and visual quality across user-defined tolerances, facilitating downstream interactive applications that compose extensive 3DGS assets for consistent rendering performance.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）通过利用一组3D高斯原语进行渲染，能够从多视图图像中重建复杂的数字3D资产。其显式和离散的表示方式促进了复杂数字世界的无缝组合，相较于以往的神经隐式方法具有显著优势。然而，当应用于大型构图，如人群层面场景时，它可以涵盖大量3D高斯分布，这对实时渲染带来了巨大挑战。为解决这个问题，受虚幻引擎5的纳米系统启发，我们提出了虚拟化3D高斯（V3DG）方案，这是一种基于集群的LOD解决方案，构建分层3D高斯簇，并动态选择仅必要部分以加速渲染速度。我们的方法包括两个阶段：（1）离线构建，利用局部散布方法生成分层簇，以最小化不同粒度的视觉差异;（2）在线选择，通过封装评估确定可感知的簇，以实现渲染过程中高效的光栅化。我们策划了一个合成和现实场景数据集，包括物体、树木、人物和建筑物，每个场景都需要1亿个三维高斯图来捕捉细节。实验表明，我们的解决方案在用户定义的公差范围内平衡了渲染效率和视觉质量，促进了下游交互应用，这些应用构建了大量3DGS资产，以实现稳定的渲染性能。  

### [19] Steepest Descent Density Control for Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-08  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Peihao Wang, Yuehao Wang, Dilin Wang et al.  
- **📝说明**：CVPR 2025, Project page: https://vita-group.github.io/SteepGS/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05587v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a powerful technique for real-time, high-resolution novel view synthesis. By representing scenes as a mixture of Gaussian primitives, 3DGS leverages GPU rasterization pipelines for efficient rendering and reconstruction. To optimize scene coverage and capture fine details, 3DGS employs a densification algorithm to generate additional points. However, this process often leads to redundant point clouds, resulting in excessive memory usage, slower performance, and substantial storage demands - posing significant challenges for deployment on resource-constrained devices. To address this limitation, we propose a theoretical framework that demystifies and improves density control in 3DGS. Our analysis reveals that splitting is crucial for escaping saddle points. Through an optimization-theoretic approach, we establish the necessary conditions for densification, determine the minimal number of offspring Gaussians, identify the optimal parameter update direction, and provide an analytical solution for normalizing off-spring opacity. Building on these insights, we introduce SteepGS, incorporating steepest density control, a principled strategy that minimizes loss while maintaining a compact point cloud. SteepGS achieves a ~50% reduction in Gaussian points without compromising rendering quality, significantly enhancing both efficiency and scalability.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种强大的实时、高分辨率新颖视图合成技术。通过将场景表示为高斯原语的混合，3DGS 利用 GPU 光栅化流水线实现高效的渲染和重建。为了优化场景覆盖和捕捉细节，3DGS采用密度化算法生成额外的点。然而，这一过程常常导致点云冗余，导致内存消耗过高、性能变慢和存储需求巨大——在资源有限的设备上部署时面临巨大挑战。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一个理论框架，旨在揭开并提升三维地理系统中的密度控制。我们的分析显示，分体对于逃离鞍点至关重要。通过优化理论方法，我们建立了密度化的必要条件，确定了子代高斯分布的最小数量，确定了最优参数更新方向，并提供了归一化衍生不透明度的解析解。基于这些见解，我们介绍了SteepGS，结合了最陡密度控制，这是一种原则性策略，既能最大限度地减少损失，又保持紧凑的点云。SteepGS在不牺牲渲染质量的情况下，实现了高斯点减少约50%，显著提升了效率和可扩展性。  

### [20] GSsplat: Generalizable Semantic Gaussian Splatting for Novel-view Synthesis in 3D Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-07  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Feng Xiao, Hongbin Xu, Wanlin Liang, Wenxiong Kang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04659v1)    
- **📝摘要**：The semantic synthesis of unseen scenes from multiple viewpoints is crucial for research in 3D scene understanding. Current methods are capable of rendering novel-view images and semantic maps by reconstructing generalizable Neural Radiance Fields. However, they often suffer from limitations in speed and segmentation performance. We propose a generalizable semantic Gaussian Splatting method (GSsplat) for efficient novel-view synthesis. Our model predicts the positions and attributes of scene-adaptive Gaussian distributions from once input, replacing the densification and pruning processes of traditional scene-specific Gaussian Splatting. In the multi-task framework, a hybrid network is designed to extract color and semantic information and predict Gaussian parameters. To augment the spatial perception of Gaussians for high-quality rendering, we put forward a novel offset learning module through group-based supervision and a point-level interaction module with spatial unit aggregation. When evaluated with varying numbers of multi-view inputs, GSsplat achieves state-of-the-art performance for semantic synthesis at the fastest speed.  
- **📝翻译**: 从多视角对未见场景的语义综合对于三维场景理解的研究至关重要。当前方法能够通过重建可推广的神经辐射场来渲染新颖视角图像和语义图。然而，它们常常在速度和分段性能上存在限制。我们提出了一种可推广的语义高斯喷涂法（GSsplat）用于高效新颖视图合成。我们的模型从一次输入预测场景自适应高斯分布的位置和属性，取代了传统场景特定高斯喷溅的致密和修剪过程。在多任务框架中，设计了一个混合网络以提取颜色和语义信息并预测高斯参数。为了增强高斯空间感知以实现高质量渲染，我们提出了一种通过基于群体的监督的新型偏移学习模块和一个带有空间单元聚合的点级交互模块。在多视图输入数量不同的情况下评估时，GSsplat 在语义综合方面实现了最先进的性能，且速度最快。  

### [21] 3D Gaussian Splatting Data Compression with Mixture of Priors  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-06（更新：2025-08-11）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Lei Liu, Zhenghao Chen, Dong Xu  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03310v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) data compression is crucial for enabling efficient storage and transmission in 3D scene modeling. However, its development remains limited due to inadequate entropy models and suboptimal quantization strategies for both lossless and lossy compression scenarios, where existing methods have yet to 1) fully leverage hyperprior information to construct robust conditional entropy models, and 2) apply fine-grained, element-wise quantization strategies for improved compression granularity. In this work, we propose a novel Mixture of Priors (MoP) strategy to simultaneously address these two challenges. Specifically, inspired by the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm, our MoP approach processes hyperprior information through multiple lightweight MLPs to generate diverse prior features, which are subsequently integrated into the MoP feature via a gating mechanism. To enhance lossless compression, the resulting MoP feature is utilized as a hyperprior to improve conditional entropy modeling.
