Meanwhile, for lossy compression, we employ the MoP feature as guidance information in an element-wise quantization procedure, leveraging a prior-guided Coarse-to-Fine Quantization (C2FQ) strategy with a predefined quantization step value. Specifically, we expand the quantization step value into a matrix and adaptively refine it from coarse to fine granularity, guided by the MoP feature, thereby obtaining a quantization step matrix that facilitates element-wise quantization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed 3DGS data compression framework achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, including Mip-NeRF360, BungeeNeRF, DeepBlending, and Tank&Temples.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）数据压缩对于实现3D场景建模中的高效存储和传输至关重要。然而，由于熵模型不足且在无损和有损压缩场景下量子化策略不够优，其发展仍然有限，现有方法尚未1）充分利用超先验信息构建稳健的条件熵模型，2）应用细粒度、按元素的量子化策略以提升压缩粒度。在本研究中，我们提出了一种新的先验混合（MoP）策略，以同时应对这两个挑战。具体地，受专家混合（MoE）范式启发，我们的MoP方法通过多个轻量级MLP处理超先验信息，生成多样的先验特征，随后通过门控机制整合进MoP特征中。为了增强无损压缩，所得的MoP特征被用作超先验，以改善条件熵建模。与此同时，对于有损压缩，我们采用MoP功能作为元素级量化过程的指导信息，采用预设量化步长的先验引导粗细量化（C2FQ）策略。具体来说，我们将量化步长值扩展为矩阵，并根据MoP特性从粗粒度到细粒度自适应地细化，从而得到便于元素级量子化的量子化步矩阵。大量实验表明，我们提出的3DGS数据压缩框架在多个基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，包括Mip-NeRF360、BungeeNeRF、DeepBlending和Tank&Temples。  

### [22] SignSplat: Rendering Sign Language via Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-04  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Maksym Ivashechkin, Oscar Mendez, Richard Bowden  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02108v1)    
- **📝摘要**：State-of-the-art approaches for conditional human body rendering via Gaussian splatting typically focus on simple body motions captured from many views. This is often in the context of dancing or walking. However, for more complex use cases, such as sign language, we care less about large body motion and more about subtle and complex motions of the hands and face. The problems of building high fidelity models are compounded by the complexity of capturing multi-view data of sign. The solution is to make better use of sequence data, ensuring that we can overcome the limited information from only a few views by exploiting temporal variability. Nevertheless, learning from sequence-level data requires extremely accurate and consistent model fitting to ensure that appearance is consistent across complex motions. We focus on how to achieve this, constraining mesh parameters to build an accurate Gaussian splatting framework from few views capable of modelling subtle human motion. We leverage regularization techniques on the Gaussian parameters to mitigate overfitting and rendering artifacts. Additionally, we propose a new adaptive control method to densify Gaussians and prune splat points on the mesh surface. To demonstrate the accuracy of our approach, we render novel sequences of sign language video, building on neural machine translation approaches to sign stitching. On benchmark datasets, our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance; and on highly articulated and complex sign language motion, we significantly outperform competing approaches.  
- **📝翻译**: 通过高斯喷溅进行条件人体渲染的先进方法通常关注从多个视角捕捉的简单人体运动。这通常发生在跳舞或散步的语境中。然而，对于更复杂的用例，比如手语，我们关注的不是大幅度的身体动作，而是手和面部的细微复杂动作。构建高精度模型的问题因捕获符号多视图数据的复杂性而加剧。解决方案是更好地利用序列数据，确保通过利用时间变异性克服仅有少数视角的信息限制。然而，从序列级数据中学习需要极其准确且一致的模型拟合，以确保复杂运动中的表现一致。我们专注于如何实现这一点，通过约束网格参数，从能够模拟细微人体运动的少数视角构建精确的高斯溅落框架。我们利用高斯参数的正则化技术来减轻过拟合和渲染伪影。此外，我们提出了一种新的自适应控制方法，用于密度化高斯分布并修剪网格表面的喷溅点。为了展示我们方法的准确性，我们渲染了新颖的手语视频序列，基于神经机器翻译方法进行手语缝合。在基准数据集上，我们的方法实现了最先进的性能;在高度清晰且复杂的手语动作上，我们的表现显著优于其他竞争方法。  

### [23] HybridGS: High-Efficiency Gaussian Splatting Data Compression using Dual-Channel Sparse Representation and Point Cloud Encoder  
- **⏳发布**：2025-05-03  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Qi Yang, Le Yang, Geert Van Der Auwera, Zhu Li  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by ICML2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01938v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Most existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) compression schemes focus on producing compact 3DGS representation via implicit data embedding. They have long coding times and highly customized data format, making it difficult for widespread deployment. This paper presents a new 3DGS compression framework called HybridGS, which takes advantage of both compact generation and standardized point cloud data encoding. HybridGS first generates compact and explicit 3DGS data. A dual-channel sparse representation is introduced to supervise the primitive position and feature bit depth. It then utilizes a canonical point cloud encoder to perform further data compression and form standard output bitstreams. A simple and effective rate control scheme is proposed to pivot the interpretable data compression scheme. At the current stage, HybridGS does not include any modules aimed at improving 3DGS quality during generation. But experiment results show that it still provides comparable reconstruction performance against state-of-the-art methods, with evidently higher encoding and decoding speed. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Qi-Yangsjtu/HybridGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 大多数现有的3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）压缩方案侧重于通过隐式数据嵌入实现紧凑的3DGS表示。它们编码时间长且数据格式高度定制化，难以广泛部署。本文提出了一种名为HybridGS的新型3DGS压缩框架，它利用了紧凑生成和标准化点云数据编码。HybridGS首先生成紧凑且显式的3DGS数据。引入了双通道稀疏表示以监督原始位置和特征位深度。然后它利用规范的点云编码器进行进一步的数据压缩，并形成标准输出比特流。提出了一种简单有效的速率控制方案，用于调整可解释的数据压缩方案。目前阶段，HybridGS尚未包含任何旨在提升3DGS生成质量的模块。但实验结果显示，它在与最先进方法相比，仍能提供相当的重建性能，且编码和解码速度明显更高。该代码在 https://github.com/Qi-Yangsjtu/HybridGS 年公开发布。  


## April 2025

### [1] 4DGS-CC: A Contextual Coding Framework for 4D Gaussian Splatting Data Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-26（更新：2025-04-30）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zicong Chen, Zhenghao Chen, Wei Jiang et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18925v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Storage is a significant challenge in reconstructing dynamic scenes with 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) data. In this work, we introduce 4DGS-CC, a contextual coding framework that compresses 4DGS data to meet specific storage constraints. Building upon the established deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) method, our approach decomposes 4DGS data into 4D neural voxels and a canonical 3DGS component, which are then compressed using Neural Voxel Contextual Coding (NVCC) and Vector Quantization Contextual Coding (VQCC), respectively. Specifically, we first decompose the 4D neural voxels into distinct quantized features by separating the temporal and spatial dimensions. To losslessly compress each quantized feature, we leverage the previously compressed features from the temporal and spatial dimensions as priors and apply NVCC to generate the spatiotemporal context for contextual coding. Next, we employ a codebook to store spherical harmonics information from canonical 3DGS as quantized vectors, which are then losslessly compressed by using VQCC with the auxiliary learned hyperpriors for contextual coding, thereby reducing redundancy within the codebook. By integrating NVCC and VQCC, our contextual coding framework, 4DGS-CC, enables multi-rate 4DGS data compression tailored to specific storage requirements. Extensive experiments on three 4DGS data compression benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves an average storage reduction of approximately 12 times while maintaining rendering fidelity compared to our baseline 4DGS approach.  
- **📝翻译**: 存储在用4D高斯溅射（4DGS）数据重建动态场景时是一大挑战。在本研究中，我们介绍了4DGS-CC，一种上下文编码框架，能够压缩4DGS数据以满足特定的存储约束。基于已建立的可变形三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）方法，我们的方法将4DGS数据分解为四维神经体素和一个典型的3DGS分量，然后分别使用神经体素上下文编码（NVCC）和矢量量化上下文编码（VQCC）进行压缩。具体来说，我们首先通过分离时间维度和空间维度，将四维神经体素分解为不同的量子化特征。为了无损压缩每个量子化特征，我们利用时间和空间维度中先前压缩的特征作为先验，并应用NVCC生成用于上下文编码的时空上下文。接下来，我们使用码本将正则3DGS中的球面谐波信息作为量化向量存储，然后通过VQCC与辅助学习超先验进行上下文编码无损压缩，从而减少码本内部的冗余。通过集成NVCC和VQCC，我们的上下文编码框架4DGS-CC实现了针对特定存储需求的多速率4DGS数据压缩。在三个4DGS数据压缩基准测试上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法相比基线4DGS方法，平均存储量减少约12倍，同时保持渲染忠实度。  

### [2] 3D Gaussian Head Avatars with Expressive Dynamic Appearances by Compact Tensorial Representations  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-21  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yating Wang, Xuan Wang, Ran Yi et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14967v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent studies have combined 3D Gaussian and 3D Morphable Models (3DMM) to construct high-quality 3D head avatars. In this line of research, existing methods either fail to capture the dynamic textures or incur significant overhead in terms of runtime speed or storage space. To this end, we propose a novel method that addresses all the aforementioned demands. In specific, we introduce an expressive and compact representation that encodes texture-related attributes of the 3D Gaussians in the tensorial format. We store appearance of neutral expression in static tri-planes, and represents dynamic texture details for different expressions using lightweight 1D feature lines, which are then decoded into opacity offset relative to the neutral face. We further propose adaptive truncated opacity penalty and class-balanced sampling to improve generalization across different expressions. Experiments show this design enables accurate face dynamic details capturing while maintains real-time rendering and significantly reduces storage costs, thus broadening the applicability to more scenarios.  
- **📝翻译**: 近期研究结合了3D高斯和3D可变形模型（3DMM），构建了高质量的3D头部化身。在这条研究方向上，现有方法要么无法捕捉动态纹理，要么在运行速度或存储空间方面产生了显著的开销。为此，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，以应对上述所有需求。具体来说，我们引入了一种表达性强且紧凑的表示，将三维高斯的纹理相关属性编码为张量格式。我们将中性表情的外观存储在静态三平面中，并用轻量级一维特征线表示不同表情的动态纹理细节，然后将其解码为相对于中性面的不透明度偏移。我们进一步提出自适应截断不透明度惩罚和类平衡抽样，以提升跨不同表达式的泛化能力。实验表明，这种设计能够实现面部动态细节的精确捕捉，同时保持实时渲染，并显著降低存储成本，从而扩大了适用于更多场景的应用。  

### [3] Volume Encoding Gaussians: Transfer Function-Agnostic 3D Gaussians for Volume Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Landon Dyken, Andres Sewell, Will Usher, Steve Petruzza, Sidharth Kumar  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13339v1)    
- **📝摘要**：While HPC resources are increasingly being used to produce adaptively refined or unstructured volume datasets, current research in applying machine learning-based representation to visualization has largely ignored this type of data. To address this, we introduce Volume Encoding Gaussians (VEG), a novel 3D Gaussian-based representation for scientific volume visualization focused on unstructured volumes. Unlike prior 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods that store view-dependent color and opacity for each Gaussian, VEG decouple the visual appearance from the data representation by encoding only scalar values, enabling transfer-function-agnostic rendering of 3DGS models for interactive scientific visualization. VEG are directly initialized from volume datasets, eliminating the need for structure-from-motion pipelines like COLMAP. To ensure complete scalar field coverage, we introduce an opacity-guided training strategy, using differentiable rendering with multiple transfer functions to optimize our data representation. This allows VEG to preserve fine features across the full scalar range of a dataset while remaining independent of any specific transfer function. Each Gaussian is scaled and rotated to adapt to local geometry, allowing for efficient representation of unstructured meshes without storing mesh connectivity and while using far fewer primitives. Across a diverse set of data, VEG achieve high reconstruction quality, compress large volume datasets by up to 3600x, and support lightning-fast rendering on commodity GPUs, enabling interactive visualization of large-scale structured and unstructured volumes.  
- **📝翻译**: 尽管高性能计算资源越来越多地用于生成自适应细化或非结构化的体积数据集，但当前基于机器学习的可视化研究在很大程度上忽视了这类数据。为此，我们引入了体积编码高斯分布（VEG），这是一种基于3D高斯的新型表示方法，用于科学体积可视化，聚焦于非结构化体积。与以往存储每个高斯分布视图相关的颜色和不透明度的3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）方法不同，VEG通过仅编码标量值，将视觉外观与数据表示解耦，实现3DGS模型的传递函数无关渲染，实现交互式科学可视化。VEG直接从卷数据集初始化，无需像COLMAP这样的动态结构流水线。为确保标量场的完整覆盖，我们引入了不透明度引导训练策略，利用多重传递函数的可微渲染优化数据表示。这使得VEG能够在整个标量范围内保留精细特征，同时保持独立于任何特定传递函数。每个高斯图都经过缩放和旋转以适应局部几何，从而在不存储网格连通性的情况下高效表示非结构化网格，同时使用更少的原元。在多样化的数据集中，VEG实现高质量的重建质量，将大批量数据集压缩至3600倍，并支持在普通GPU上实现极快渲染，实现大规模结构化和非结构化体积的交互式可视化。  

### [4] Training-Free Hierarchical Scene Understanding for Gaussian Splatting with Superpoint Graphs  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shaohui Dai, Yansong Qu, Zheyan Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13153v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Bridging natural language and 3D geometry is a crucial step toward flexible, language-driven scene understanding. While recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled fast and high-quality scene reconstruction, research has also explored incorporating open-vocabulary understanding into 3DGS. However, most existing methods require iterative optimization over per-view 2D semantic feature maps, which not only results in inefficiencies but also leads to inconsistent 3D semantics across views. To address these limitations, we introduce a training-free framework that constructs a superpoint graph directly from Gaussian primitives. The superpoint graph partitions the scene into spatially compact and semantically coherent regions, forming view-consistent 3D entities and providing a structured foundation for open-vocabulary understanding. Based on the graph structure, we design an efficient reprojection strategy that lifts 2D semantic features onto the superpoints, avoiding costly multi-view iterative training. The resulting representation ensures strong 3D semantic coherence and naturally supports hierarchical understanding, enabling both coarse- and fine-grained open-vocabulary perception within a unified semantic field. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art open-vocabulary segmentation performance, with semantic field reconstruction completed over $30\times$ faster. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Atrovast/THGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 连接自然语言与三维几何是实现灵活、语言驱动场景理解的关键一步。虽然3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）的最新进展使场景重建变得快速且高质量，研究也开始探索将开放词汇理解融入3DGS。然而，大多数现有方法都需要对每视角的二维特征映射进行迭代优化，这不仅导致效率低下，还导致不同视图间的三维语义不一致。为解决这些限制，我们引入了一个无训练框架，直接从高斯原语构造一个叠加点图。叠加点图将场景划分为空间紧凑且语义连贯的区域，形成视角一致的三维实体，并为开放词汇理解提供结构化基础。基于图结构，我们设计了一种高效的重投影策略，将二维语义特征提升到叠加点上，避免了昂贵的多视角迭代训练。由此产生的表示确保了强大的三维语义连贯性，自然支持层级理解，使得在统一语义场内实现粗粒度和细粒度的开放词汇感知。大量实验表明，我们的方法实现了最先进的开放词汇分割性能，语义场重建速度快了30多美元。我们的代码将在 https://github.com/Atrovast/THGS 年提供。  

### [5] CompGS++: Compressed Gaussian Splatting for Static and Dynamic Scene Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xiangrui Liu, Xinju Wu, Shiqi Wang, Zhu Li, Sam Kwong  
- **📝说明**：Submitted to a journal  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13022v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Gaussian splatting demonstrates proficiency for 3D scene modeling but suffers from substantial data volume due to inherent primitive redundancy. To enable future photorealistic 3D immersive visual communication applications, significant compression is essential for transmission over the existing Internet infrastructure. Hence, we propose Compressed Gaussian Splatting (CompGS++), a novel framework that leverages compact Gaussian primitives to achieve accurate 3D modeling with substantial size reduction for both static and dynamic scenes. Our design is based on the principle of eliminating redundancy both between and within primitives. Specifically, we develop a comprehensive prediction paradigm to address inter-primitive redundancy through spatial and temporal primitive prediction modules. The spatial primitive prediction module establishes predictive relationships for scene primitives and enables most primitives to be encoded as compact residuals, substantially reducing the spatial redundancy. We further devise a temporal primitive prediction module to handle dynamic scenes, which exploits primitive correlations across timestamps to effectively reduce temporal redundancy. Moreover, we devise a rate-constrained optimization module that jointly minimizes reconstruction error and rate consumption. This module effectively eliminates parameter redundancy within primitives and enhances the overall compactness of scene representations. Comprehensive evaluations across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that CompGS++ significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving superior compression performance while preserving accurate scene modeling. Our implementation will be made publicly available on GitHub to facilitate further research.  
- **📝翻译**: 高斯喷溅显示出三维场景建模的熟练度，但由于固有的原始冗余，数据量较大。为了实现未来的写实3D沉浸式视觉通信应用，通过现有互联网基础设施传输必须进行大量压缩。因此，我们提出了压缩高斯喷涂（Compressed Gaussian Splatting，简称CompGS++），这是一个新颖的框架，利用紧凑的高斯原语实现精确的三维建模，同时大幅缩小静态和动态场景的尺寸。我们的设计基于消除原件之间及内部冗余的原则。具体来说，我们开发了一个全面的预测范式，通过空间和时间的原始预测模块解决原始间冗余问题。空间原语预测模块为场景原语建立了预测关系，并使大多数原语能够编码为紧残差，从而大幅减少空间冗余。我们还设计了一个时间原始预测模块来处理动态场景，利用跨时间戳的原始相关性，有效减少时间冗余。此外，我们还设计了一个速率约束的优化模块，能够共同最小化重建误差和速率消耗。该模块有效消除了原语中的参数冗余，并增强了场景表示的整体紧凑性。对多个基准数据集的全面评估表明，CompGS++ 远超现有方法，在保持场景建模准确的同时实现了更优越的压缩性能。我们的实现将公开在GitHub上，以便进一步研究。  

### [6] SIGMAN:Scaling 3D Human Gaussian Generation with Millions of Assets  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-09  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuhang Yang, Fengqi Liu, Yixing Lu et al.  
- **📝说明**：project page:https://yyvhang.github.io/SIGMAN_3D/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06982v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D human digitization has long been a highly pursued yet challenging task. Existing methods aim to generate high-quality 3D digital humans from single or multiple views, but remain primarily constrained by current paradigms and the scarcity of 3D human assets. Specifically, recent approaches fall into several paradigms: optimization-based and feed-forward (both single-view regression and multi-view generation with reconstruction). However, they are limited by slow speed, low quality, cascade reasoning, and ambiguity in mapping low-dimensional planes to high-dimensional space due to occlusion and invisibility, respectively. Furthermore, existing 3D human assets remain small-scale, insufficient for large-scale training. To address these challenges, we propose a latent space generation paradigm for 3D human digitization, which involves compressing multi-view images into Gaussians via a UV-structured VAE, along with DiT-based conditional generation, we transform the ill-posed low-to-high-dimensional mapping problem into a learnable distribution shift, which also supports end-to-end inference. In addition, we employ the multi-view optimization approach combined with synthetic data to construct the HGS-1M dataset, which contains $1$ million 3D Gaussian assets to support the large-scale training. Experimental results demonstrate that our paradigm, powered by large-scale training, produces high-quality 3D human Gaussians with intricate textures, facial details, and loose clothing deformation.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D人类数字化长期以来一直是一项高度追求但充满挑战的任务。现有方法旨在从单一或多视角生成高质量的3D数字人类，但主要受限于当前范式和3D人类资源的稀缺。具体来说，近期的方法分为几种范式：基于优化和前馈（包括单视角回归和带重建的多视角生成）。然而，它们受限于速度缓慢、质量低、级联推理以及由于遮挡和隐形导致低维平面映射到高维空间时的模糊性。此外，现有的三维人力资源仍小规模，不足以进行大规模培训。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种潜空间生成范式用于3D人类数字化，该范式通过紫外结构的VAE将多视角图像压缩为高斯分布，结合基于DiT的条件生成，我们将错误姿态的低维到高维映射问题转化为可学习的分布偏移，同时支持端到端推断。此外，我们结合多视角优化方法与合成数据构建了HGS-1M数据集，其中包含价值100万美元的三维高斯资产，以支持大规模训练。实验结果表明，我们的范式通过大规模训练驱动，能够生成高质量的三维人类高斯分布图，具有复杂的纹理、面部细节和松散的服装变形。  

### [7] GSta: Efficient Training Scheme with Siestaed Gaussians for Monocular 3D Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-09  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Anil Armagan, Albert Saà-Garriga, Bruno Manganelli, Kyuwon Kim, M. Kerim Yucel  
- **📝说明**：9 pages. In submission to an IEEE conference  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06716v1)   · [Project](https://anilarmagan.github.io/SRUK-GSta.)  
- **📝摘要**：Gaussian Splatting (GS) is a popular approach for 3D reconstruction, mostly due to its ability to converge reasonably fast, faithfully represent the scene and render (novel) views in a fast fashion. However, it suffers from large storage and memory requirements, and its training speed still lags behind the hash-grid based radiance field approaches (e.g. Instant-NGP), which makes it especially difficult to deploy them in robotics scenarios, where 3D reconstruction is crucial for accurate operation. In this paper, we propose GSta that dynamically identifies Gaussians that have converged well during training, based on their positional and color gradient norms. By forcing such Gaussians into a siesta and stopping their updates (freezing) during training, we improve training speed with competitive accuracy compared to state of the art. We also propose an early stopping mechanism based on the PSNR values computed on a subset of training images. Combined with other improvements, such as integrating a learning rate scheduler, GSta achieves an improved Pareto front in convergence speed, memory and storage requirements, while preserving quality. We also show that GSta can improve other methods and complement orthogonal approaches in efficiency improvement; once combined with Trick-GS, GSta achieves up to 5x faster training, 16x smaller disk size compared to vanilla GS, while having comparable accuracy and consuming only half the peak memory. More visualisations are available at https://anilarmagan.github.io/SRUK-GSta.  
- **📝翻译**: 高斯溅射（GS）是一种流行的三维重建方法，主要因为它能够较快地收敛，忠实地还原场景，并以快速的方式渲染（新颖的）视图。然而，它存在较大的存储和内存需求，且训练速度仍落后于基于哈希网格的辐射场方法（如Instant-NGP），这使得在机器人场景中部署这些方法尤其困难，因为三维重建对准确作至关重要。本文提出一种GSta，基于其位置和颜色梯度范数动态识别训练过程中收敛良好的高斯分布图。通过强制这些高斯学员进入午睡并停止其更新（冻结），我们提升了训练速度，具有竞争力，相较于最先进的技术。我们还提出了一种基于部分训练图像中PSNR值的早期停止机制。结合其他改进，如集成学习率调度器，GSta在收敛速度、内存和存储需求上实现了更优的帕累托前沿，同时保持了质量。我们还表明GSta可以改进其他方法，并补充正交方法以提高效率;与 Trick-GS 结合后，GSta 训练速度可提升 5 倍，磁盘尺寸比原版 GS 小 16 倍，精度相当，峰值内存占用仅一半。更多可视化资料可在 https://anilarmagan.github.io/SRUK-GSta 获取。  

### [8] Micro-splatting: Multistage Isotropy-informed Covariance Regularization Optimization for High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-08（更新：2025-09-02）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jee Won Lee, Hansol Lim, Sooyeun Yang, Jongseong Brad Choi  
- **📝说明**：This work has been submitted to journal for potential publication  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05740v2)    
- **📝摘要**：High-fidelity 3D Gaussian Splatting methods excel at capturing fine textures but often overlook model compactness, resulting in massive splat counts, bloated memory, long training, and complex post-processing. We present Micro-Splatting: Two-Stage Adaptive Growth and Refinement, a unified, in-training pipeline that preserves visual detail while drastically reducing model complexity without any post-processing or auxiliary neural modules. In Stage I (Growth), we introduce a trace-based covariance regularization to maintain near-isotropic Gaussians, mitigating low-pass filtering in high-frequency regions and improving spherical-harmonic color fitting. We then apply gradient-guided adaptive densification that subdivides splats only in visually complex regions, leaving smooth areas sparse. In Stage II (Refinement), we prune low-impact splats using a simple opacity-scale importance score and merge redundant neighbors via lightweight spatial and feature thresholds, producing a lean yet detail-rich model. On four object-centric benchmarks, Micro-Splatting reduces splat count and model size by up to 60% and shortens training by 20%, while matching or surpassing state-of-the-art PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS in real-time rendering. These results demonstrate that Micro-Splatting delivers both compactness and high fidelity in a single, efficient, end-to-end framework.  
- **📝翻译**: 高精度3D高斯喷涂方法擅长捕捉细腻纹理，但常忽视模型的紧凑性，导致大量喷溅数量、内存膨胀、长时间训练和复杂的后期处理。我们呈现“微喷涂：两阶段适应性增长与精炼”，这是一条统一的培训中流程，既保留视觉细节，又大幅降低模型复杂度，无需任何后处理或辅助神经模块。在第一阶段（增长），我们引入基于迹的协方差正则化，以维持近各向同性的高斯分布，减轻高频区域的低通滤波，并改善球谐色彩拟合。然后我们应用梯度引导自适应密度化，仅在视觉复杂区域细分斑点，平滑区域则稀疏。在第二阶段（精细化），我们使用简单的不透明度尺度重要性评分修剪低影响的碎片，并通过轻量级空间和特征阈值合并冗余邻居，形成精简但细节丰富的模型。在四个以对象为中心的基准测试中，Micro-Splatting可将飞溅数量和模型规模减少多达60%，训练时间缩短20%，同时在实时渲染方面可与最先进的PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS匹敌甚至超越。这些结果表明，微拼图在单一高效的端到端框架中实现了紧凑性和高保真度。  

### [9] L3GS: Layered 3D Gaussian Splats for Efficient 3D Scene Delivery  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-07  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yi-Zhen Tsai, Xuechen Zhang, Zheng Li, Jiasi Chen  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05517v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Traditional 3D content representations include dense point clouds that consume large amounts of data and hence network bandwidth, while newer representations such as neural radiance fields suffer from poor frame rates due to their non-standard volumetric rendering pipeline. 3D Gaussian splats (3DGS) can be seen as a generalization of point clouds that meet the best of both worlds, with high visual quality and efficient rendering for real-time frame rates. However, delivering 3DGS scenes from a hosting server to client devices is still challenging due to high network data consumption (e.g., 1.5 GB for a single scene). The goal of this work is to create an efficient 3D content delivery framework that allows users to view high quality 3D scenes with 3DGS as the underlying data representation. The main contributions of the paper are: (1) Creating new layered 3DGS scenes for efficient delivery, (2) Scheduling algorithms to choose what splats to download at what time, and (3) Trace-driven experiments from users wearing virtual reality headsets to evaluate the visual quality and latency. Our system for Layered 3D Gaussian Splats delivery L3GS demonstrates high visual quality, achieving 16.9% higher average SSIM compared to baselines, and also works with other compressed 3DGS representations.  
- **📝翻译**: 传统的3D内容表示包括密集点云，消耗大量数据，因此网络带宽大;而较新的表现形式如神经辐射场则因非标准体积渲染流水线而帧率较差。3D高斯分布（3DGS）可以看作点云的推广，兼具高质量的视觉质量和高效的实时帧率渲染。然而，由于网络数据消耗较高（例如单个场景需1.5 GB），将3DGS场景从主机服务器传输到客户端设备仍然具有挑战性。这项工作的目标是创建一个高效的3D内容分发框架，允许用户以3DGS作为底层数据表示，查看高质量的3D场景。论文的主要贡献包括：（1）创建新的分层3DGS场景以实现高效传输，（2）调度算法以选择在何时下载哪些splat，以及（3）对佩戴虚拟现实头显的用户进行的追踪驱动实验，以评估视觉质量和延迟。我们的分层3D高斯斯普拉特传输L3GS系统展现了高画质，平均SSIM比基线高出16.9%，并且还能支持其他压缩3DGS表示。  

### [10] 3D Gaussian Particle Approximation of VDB Datasets: A Study for Scientific Visualization  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-07（更新：2025-04-16）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Isha Sharma, Dieter Schmalstieg  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04857v2)    
- **📝摘要**：The complexity and scale of Volumetric and Simulation datasets for Scientific Visualization(SciVis) continue to grow. And the approaches and advantages of memory-efficient data formats and storage techniques for such datasets vary. OpenVDB library and its VDB data format excels in memory efficiency through its hierarchical and dynamic tree structure, with active and inactive sub-trees for data storage. It is heavily used in current production renderers for both animation and rendering stages in VFX pipelines and photorealistic rendering of volumes and fluids. However, it still remains to be fully leveraged in SciVis where domains dealing with sparse scalar fields like porous media, time varying volumes such as tornado and weather simulation or high resolution simulation of Computational Fluid Dynamics present ample number of large challenging data sets. The goal of this paper hence is not only to explore the use of OpenVDB in SciVis but also to explore a level of detail(LOD) technique using 3D Gaussian particles approximating voxel regions. For rendering, we utilize NVIDIA OptiX library for ray marching through the Gaussians particles. Data modeling using 3D Gaussians has been very popular lately due to success in stereoscopic image to 3D scene conversion using Gaussian Splatting and Gaussian approximation and mixture models aren't entirely new in SciVis as well. Our work explores the integration with rendering software libraries like OpenVDB and OptiX to take advantage of their built-in memory compaction and hardware acceleration features, while also leveraging the performance capabilities of modern GPUs. Thus, we present a SciVis rendering approach that uses 3D Gaussians at varying LOD in a lossy scheme derived from VDB datasets, rather than focusing on photorealistic volume rendering.  
- **📝翻译**: 科学可视化（SciVis）的体积和仿真数据集的复杂性和规模持续增长。而高效内存数据格式和存储技术在此类数据集中的方法和优势各不相同。OpenVDB 库及其 VDB 数据格式通过其层级和动态树结构，在内存效率方面表现出色，数据存储有活跃和非活跃子树。它在当前制作渲染器中被广泛应用于动画和渲染阶段的视觉特效管线，以及体积和流体的写实渲染。然而，在涉及稀疏标量场（如多孔介质）、时间变化体积（如龙卷风和天气模拟）或计算流体动力学高分辨率模拟等领域，仍需充分发挥该技术。因此，本文的目标不仅是探索OpenVDB在SciVis中的应用，还将利用三维高斯粒子近似体素区域，探索一种细节层次（LOD）技术。渲染方面，我们使用 NVIDIA OptiX 库进行高斯粒子的光线行进。由于利用高斯溅射技术将立体图像转换为三维场景的成功，使用3D高斯数据建模最近非常流行，高斯近似和混合模型在SciVis中也并非全新技术。我们的研究探讨了与 OpenVDB 和 OptiX 等渲染软件库的集成，以利用其内置的内存压缩和硬件加速功能，同时利用现代 GPU 的性能能力。因此，我们提出了一种Scivis 渲染方法，采用基于VDB数据集的有损方案，采用变化LOD的3D高斯分布，而非专注于写实体积渲染。  

### [11] Compressing 3D Gaussian Splatting by Noise-Substituted Vector Quantization  
- **⏳发布**：2025-04-03（更新：2025-04-08）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haishan Wang, Mohammad Hassan Vali, Arno Solin  
- **📝说明**：Appearing in Scandinavian Conference on Image Analysis (SCIA) 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03059v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has demonstrated remarkable effectiveness in 3D reconstruction, achieving high-quality results with real-time radiance field rendering. However, a key challenge is the substantial storage cost: reconstructing a single scene typically requires millions of Gaussian splats, each represented by 59 floating-point parameters, resulting in approximately 1 GB of memory. To address this challenge, we propose a compression method by building separate attribute codebooks and storing only discrete code indices. Specifically, we employ noise-substituted vector quantization technique to jointly train the codebooks and model features, ensuring consistency between gradient descent optimization and parameter discretization. Our method reduces the memory consumption efficiently (around $45\times$) while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality on standard 3D benchmark scenes. Experiments on different codebook sizes show the trade-off between compression ratio and image quality. Furthermore, the trained compressed model remains fully compatible with popular 3DGS viewers and enables faster rendering speed, making it well-suited for practical applications.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）在3D重建方面展现出显著效果，实现了高质量的实时辐射场渲染。然而，一个关键挑战是高昂的存储成本：重建单个场景通常需要数百万个高斯碎片，每个碎片由59个浮点参数表示，约占1 GB内存。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种压缩方法，通过构建独立的属性码本，并仅存储离散的代码索引。具体来说，我们采用噪声替代向量量化技术联合训练码本和模型特征，确保梯度下降优化与参数离散化的一致性。我们的方法高效地降低了内存消耗（约45美元/时间美元），同时在标准3D基准场景上保持了竞争性的重建质量。对不同编码本尺寸的实验显示了压缩比与图像质量之间的权衡。此外，训练好的压缩模型与流行的3DGS浏览器完全兼容，并实现更快的渲染速度，非常适合实际应用。  


## March 2025

### [1] Enhancing 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression via Spatial Condition-based Prediction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-30  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jingui Ma, Yang Hu, Luyang Tang et al.  
- **📝说明**：The paper has been accepted by ICME2025 in March,2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23337v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recently, 3D Gaussian Spatting (3DGS) has gained widespread attention in Novel View Synthesis (NVS) due to the remarkable real-time rendering performance. However, the substantial cost of storage and transmission of vanilla 3DGS hinders its further application (hundreds of megabytes or even gigabytes for a single scene). Motivated by the achievements of prediction in video compression, we introduce the prediction technique into the anchor-based Gaussian representation to effectively reduce the bit rate. Specifically, we propose a spatial condition-based prediction module to utilize the grid-captured scene information for prediction, with a residual compensation strategy designed to learn the missing fine-grained information. Besides, to further compress the residual, we propose an instance-aware hyper prior, developing a structure-aware and instance-aware entropy model. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our prediction-based compression framework and each technical component. Even compared with SOTA compression method, our framework still achieves a bit rate savings of 24.42 percent. Code is to be released!  
- **📝翻译**: 最近，3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）因其卓越的实时渲染性能，在新颖视角合成（NVS）中引起了广泛关注。然而，原版3DGS的高昂存储和传输成本限制了其后续应用（单个场景需数百兆字节甚至GB）。受视频压缩预测成功启发，我们将预测技术引入基于锚点的高斯表示，以有效降低比特率。具体来说，我们提出了一个基于空间条件的预测模块，利用网格捕获的场景信息进行预测，并采用残差补偿策略学习缺失的细粒度信息。此外，为了进一步压缩残差，我们提出了一个实例感知超先验，发展出结构感知和实例感知熵模型。大量实验证明了我们基于预测的压缩框架及各技术组件的有效性。即使与SOTA压缩方法相比，我们的框架仍实现了24.42%的比特率节省。代码即将发布！  

### [2] NeuralGS: Bridging Neural Fields and 3D Gaussian Splatting for Compact 3D Representations  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-29（更新：2025-08-13）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhenyu Tang, Chaoran Feng, Xinhua Cheng et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://pku-yuangroup.github.io/NeuralGS/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23162v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves impressive quality and rendering speed, but with millions of 3D Gaussians and significant storage and transmission costs. In this paper, we aim to develop a simple yet effective method called NeuralGS that compresses the original 3DGS into a compact representation. Our observation is that neural fields like NeRF can represent complex 3D scenes with Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) neural networks using only a few megabytes. Thus, NeuralGS effectively adopts the neural field representation to encode the attributes of 3D Gaussians with MLPs, only requiring a small storage size even for a large-scale scene. To achieve this, we adopt a clustering strategy and fit the Gaussians within each cluster using different tiny MLPs, based on importance scores of Gaussians as fitting weights. We experiment on multiple datasets, achieving a 91-times average model size reduction without harming the visual quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射（3DGS）实现了令人印象深刻的画质和渲染速度，但涉及数百万个3D高斯图以及显著的存储和传输成本。本文旨在开发一种简单但有效的方法，称为NeuralGS，将原始3DGS压缩为紧凑的表示。我们的观察是，像NeRF这样的神经场可以用多层感知器（MLP）神经网络仅用几兆字节来表示复杂的三维场景。因此，NeuralGS实际上采用了神经场表示法，用MLP编码三维高斯的属性，即使是大型场景，也只需较小的存储空间。为此，我们采用聚类策略，利用不同微小的MLP拟合每个聚类中的高斯分布，基于高斯分布作为拟合权重的重要性评分。我们在多个数据集上进行实验，实现了平均模型规模的91倍缩小，同时不影响视觉质量。  

### [3] Audio-Plane: Audio Factorization Plane Gaussian Splatting for Real-Time Talking Head Synthesis  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-28（更新：2025-06-27）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shuai Shen, Wanhua Li, Yunpeng Zhang, Yap-Peng Tan, Jiwen Lu  
- **📝说明**：Demo video at \url{https://sstzal.github.io/Audio-Plane/}  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22605v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Talking head synthesis has emerged as a prominent research topic in computer graphics and multimedia, yet most existing methods often struggle to strike a balance between generation quality and computational efficiency, particularly under real-time constraints. In this paper, we propose a novel framework that integrates Gaussian Splatting with a structured Audio Factorization Plane (Audio-Plane) to enable high-quality, audio-synchronized, and real-time talking head generation. For modeling a dynamic talking head, a 4D volume representation, which consists of three axes in 3D space and one temporal axis aligned with audio progression, is typically required. However, directly storing and processing a dense 4D grid is impractical due to the high memory and computation cost, and lack of scalability for longer durations. We address this challenge by decomposing the 4D volume representation into a set of audio-independent spatial planes and audio-dependent planes, forming a compact and interpretable representation for talking head modeling that we refer to as the Audio-Plane. This factorized design allows for efficient and fine-grained audio-aware spatial encoding, and significantly enhances the model's ability to capture complex lip dynamics driven by speech signals. To further improve region-specific motion modeling, we introduce an audio-guided saliency splatting mechanism based on region-aware modulation, which adaptively emphasizes highly dynamic regions such as the mouth area. This allows the model to focus its learning capacity on where it matters most for accurate speech-driven animation. Extensive experiments on both the self-driven and the cross-driven settings demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art visual quality, precise audio-lip synchronization, and real-time performance, outperforming prior approaches across both 2D- and 3D-based paradigms.  
- **📝翻译**: 头部合成已成为计算机图形学和多媒体领域的重要研究课题，但大多数现有方法在生成质量与计算效率之间常常难以平衡，尤其是在实时限制下。本文提出了一个新颖框架，将高斯喷溅与结构化音频分解平面（Audio-Plane）集成，实现高质量、音频同步且实时的对话头生成。对于建模动态对话头像，通常需要一个四维体积表示，即三维空间中的三个轴和一个与音频进程对齐的时间轴。然而，由于内存和计算成本高，且缺乏较长时间的可扩展性，直接存储和处理密集的四维网格并不切实际。我们通过将四维体积表示分解为一组音频无关的空间平面和音频相关的平面，形成一个紧凑且易于解释的对讲头建模表示，我们称之为音频平面。这种分解设计实现了高效且细粒度的音频感知空间编码，显著增强了模型捕捉由语音信号驱动的复杂唇型动态的能力。为进一步提升区域特定运动建模，我们引入了基于区域感知调制的音频引导显著性溅射机制，自适应地强调如口腔区域等高度动态区域。这使得模型能够将学习能力集中在对语音驱动动画最关键的部分。在自驱动和交叉驱动环境下的广泛实验表明，我们的方法实现了最先进的视觉质量、精准的音频-唇同步和实时性能，在2D和3D范式上均优于以往方法。  

### [4] TC-GS: Tri-plane based compression for 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-26  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Taorui Wang, Zitong Yu, Yong Xu  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by ICME 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20221v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a prominent framework for novel view synthesis, providing high fidelity and rapid rendering speed. However, the substantial data volume of 3DGS and its attributes impede its practical utility, requiring compression techniques for reducing memory cost. Nevertheless, the unorganized shape of 3DGS leads to difficulties in compression. To formulate unstructured attributes into normative distribution, we propose a well-structured tri-plane to encode Gaussian attributes, leveraging the distribution of attributes for compression. To exploit the correlations among adjacent Gaussians, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN) is used when decoding Gaussian distribution from the Tri-plane. We also introduce Gaussian position information as a prior of the position-sensitive decoder. Additionally, we incorporate an adaptive wavelet loss, aiming to focus on the high-frequency details as iterations increase. Our approach has achieved results that are comparable to or surpass that of SOTA 3D Gaussians Splatting compression work in extensive experiments across multiple datasets. The codes are released at https://github.com/timwang2001/TC-GS.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）成为新颖视图合成的重要框架，提供高保真度和快速渲染速度。然而，3DGS庞大的数据量及其特性限制了其实用性，因此需要压缩技术来降低内存成本。然而，3DGS的无序形状导致压缩困难。为了将非结构化属性表述为规范分布，我们提出了一种良好结构化的三平面来编码高斯属性，利用属性分布进行压缩。为了利用相邻高斯分布之间的相关性，解码三平面高斯分布时使用了K最近邻（KNN）。我们还引入了高斯位置信息作为位置敏感解码器的先驱。此外，我们还采用了自适应小波损耗技术，随着迭代次数增加，专注于高频细节。我们的方法在多个数据集的广泛实验中取得了与SOTA 3D Gauss-Splatting压缩工作相当甚至更胜一筹的结果。代码于 https://github.com/timwang2001/TC-GS 年发布。  

### [5] NexusGS: Sparse View Synthesis with Epipolar Depth Priors in 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-24  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yulong Zheng, Zicheng Jiang, Shengfeng He et al.  
- **📝说明**：This paper is accepted by CVPR 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18794v1)   · [Project](https://usmizuki.github.io/NexusGS/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Neural Radiance Field (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have noticeably advanced photo-realistic novel view synthesis using images from densely spaced camera viewpoints. However, these methods struggle in few-shot scenarios due to limited supervision. In this paper, we present NexusGS, a 3DGS-based approach that enhances novel view synthesis from sparse-view images by directly embedding depth information into point clouds, without relying on complex manual regularizations. Exploiting the inherent epipolar geometry of 3DGS, our method introduces a novel point cloud densification strategy that initializes 3DGS with a dense point cloud, reducing randomness in point placement while preventing over-smoothing and overfitting. Specifically, NexusGS comprises three key steps: Epipolar Depth Nexus, Flow-Resilient Depth Blending, and Flow-Filtered Depth Pruning. These steps leverage optical flow and camera poses to compute accurate depth maps, while mitigating the inaccuracies often associated with optical flow. By incorporating epipolar depth priors, NexusGS ensures reliable dense point cloud coverage and supports stable 3DGS training under sparse-view conditions. Experiments demonstrate that NexusGS significantly enhances depth accuracy and rendering quality, surpassing state-of-the-art methods by a considerable margin. Furthermore, we validate the superiority of our generated point clouds by substantially boosting the performance of competing methods. Project page: https://usmizuki.github.io/NexusGS/.  
- **📝翻译**: 神经辐射场（NeRF）和三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）在使用密集摄影机视角图像的写实新颖视图合成方面，具有明显的先进技术。然而，由于监督有限，这些方法在少数几次拍摄场景中表现不佳。本文介绍了NexusGS，一种基于3DGS的方法，通过直接将深度信息嵌入点云，增强从稀疏视图图像中新颖的视图合成，而无需依赖复杂的人工正则化。利用3DGS固有的极性几何，我们引入了一种新颖的点云致密策略，使3DGS初始化为密集的点云，减少点位的随机性，同时防止过度平滑和过拟合。具体来说，NexusGS包含三个关键步骤：极地深度连接、流韧深度混合和流滤深度修剪。这些步骤利用光流和相机姿态来计算准确的深度图，同时减少光流常见的不准确性。通过引入极区深度先验，NexusGS确保了可靠的密集点云覆盖，并支持在稀疏视野条件下稳定的3DGS训练。实验表明，NexusGS显著提升了深度精度和渲染质量，远远超越了最先进的方法。此外，我们通过大幅提升竞争方法的性能，验证了生成点云的优越性。项目页面：https://usmizuki.github.io/NexusGS/。  

### [6] GS-LTS: 3D Gaussian Splatting-Based Adaptive Modeling for Long-Term Service Robots  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-22  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Bin Fu, Jialin Li, Bin Zhang, Ruiping Wang, Xilin Chen  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17733v1)   · [Project](https://vipl-vsu.github.io/3DGS-LTS.)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has garnered significant attention in robotics for its explicit, high fidelity dense scene representation, demonstrating strong potential for robotic applications. However, 3DGS-based methods in robotics primarily focus on static scenes, with limited attention to the dynamic scene changes essential for long-term service robots. These robots demand sustained task execution and efficient scene updates-challenges current approaches fail to meet. To address these limitations, we propose GS-LTS (Gaussian Splatting for Long-Term Service), a 3DGS-based system enabling indoor robots to manage diverse tasks in dynamic environments over time. GS-LTS detects scene changes (e.g., object addition or removal) via single-image change detection, employs a rule-based policy to autonomously collect multi-view observations, and efficiently updates the scene representation through Gaussian editing. Additionally, we propose a simulation-based benchmark that automatically generates scene change data as compact configuration scripts, providing a standardized, user-friendly evaluation benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate GS-LTS's advantages in reconstruction, navigation, and superior scene updates-faster and higher quality than the image training baseline-advancing 3DGS for long-term robotic operations. Code and benchmark are available at: https://vipl-vsu.github.io/3DGS-LTS.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）因其显式、高保真且密集的场景表现而在机器人领域备受关注，展现出在机器人应用中的强大潜力。然而，基于3DGS的机器人方法主要关注静态场景，对长期服务机器人必不可少的动态场景变化关注有限。这些机器人要求持续的任务执行和高效的场景更新——而现有方法未能满足这些挑战。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了GS-LTS（高斯长期服务高斯喷溅）系统，这是一种基于3DGS的系统，使室内机器人能够在动态环境中随时间管理多样化任务。GS-LTS通过单幅图像变化检测场景变化（例如对象的添加或删除），采用基于规则的策略自主收集多视图观测，并通过高斯编辑高效更新场景表示。此外，我们还提出了一种基于仿真的基准测试，自动生成场景切换数据，作为紧凑的配置脚本，提供标准化且用户友好的评估基准。实验结果显示，GS-LTS在重建、导航和更优场景更新方面具有优势——比图像训练基线更快、质量更高，推动3DGS实现长期机器人作。代码和基准可访问：https://vipl-vsu.github.io/3DGS-LTS。  

### [7] ProtoGS: Efficient and High-Quality Rendering with 3D Gaussian Prototypes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-21（更新：2025-04-08）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhengqing Gao, Dongting Hu, Jia-Wang Bian et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17486v3)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has made significant strides in novel view synthesis but is limited by the substantial number of Gaussian primitives required, posing challenges for deployment on lightweight devices. Recent methods address this issue by compressing the storage size of densified Gaussians, yet fail to preserve rendering quality and efficiency. To overcome these limitations, we propose ProtoGS to learn Gaussian prototypes to represent Gaussian primitives, significantly reducing the total Gaussian amount without sacrificing visual quality. Our method directly uses Gaussian prototypes to enable efficient rendering and leverage the resulting reconstruction loss to guide prototype learning. To further optimize memory efficiency during training, we incorporate structure-from-motion (SfM) points as anchor points to group Gaussian primitives. Gaussian prototypes are derived within each group by clustering of K-means, and both the anchor points and the prototypes are optimized jointly. Our experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets prove that we outperform existing methods, achieving a substantial reduction in the number of Gaussians, and enabling high rendering speed while maintaining or even enhancing rendering fidelity.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）在新颖视图合成方面取得了显著进展，但受限于大量高斯原语，这在轻量化设备上的部署带来了挑战。近期方法通过压缩高斯密度存储容量来解决这一问题，但未能保持渲染质量和效率。为克服这些限制，我们提出ProtoGS学习高斯原型以表示高斯原语，显著减少总高斯数量而不牺牲视觉质量。我们的方法直接使用高斯原型实现高效渲染，并利用由此产生的重建损失来指导原型学习。为了进一步优化训练中的记忆效率，我们将结构运动（SfM）点作为锚点，用于分组高斯原语。高斯原型通过K-均值的聚类在每个组内推导出来，锚点和原型均联合优化。我们在现实世界和合成数据集上的实验证明，我们优于现有方法，显著减少了高斯分布量，实现了高速渲染，同时保持甚至提升了渲染精度。  

### [8] Optimized Minimal 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-21（更新：2025-11-06）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Joo Chan Lee, Jong Hwan Ko, Eunbyung Park  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://maincold2.github.io/omg/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16924v2)   · [Project](https://maincold2.github.io/omg/.)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a powerful representation for real-time, high-performance rendering, enabling a wide range of applications. However, representing 3D scenes with numerous explicit Gaussian primitives imposes significant storage and memory overhead. Recent studies have shown that high-quality rendering can be achieved with a substantially reduced number of Gaussians when represented with high-precision attributes. Nevertheless, existing 3DGS compression methods still rely on a relatively large number of Gaussians, focusing primarily on attribute compression. This is because a smaller set of Gaussians becomes increasingly sensitive to lossy attribute compression, leading to severe quality degradation. Since the number of Gaussians is directly tied to computational costs, it is essential to reduce the number of Gaussians effectively rather than only optimizing storage. In this paper, we propose Optimized Minimal Gaussians representation (OMG), which significantly reduces storage while using a minimal number of primitives. First, we determine the distinct Gaussian from the near ones, minimizing redundancy without sacrificing quality. Second, we propose a compact and precise attribute representation that efficiently captures both continuity and irregularity among primitives. Additionally, we propose a sub-vector quantization technique for improved irregularity representation, maintaining fast training with a negligible codebook size. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OMG reduces storage requirements by nearly 50% compared to the previous state-of-the-art and enables 600+ FPS rendering while maintaining high rendering quality. Our source code is available at https://maincold2.github.io/omg/.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）已成为实时高性能渲染的强大表现方式，支持广泛的应用。然而，用大量显式高斯原语表示三维场景会带来显著的存储和内存开销。最新研究表明，当用高精度属性表示高斯分布量时，可以大幅减少高斯数来实现高质量渲染。尽管如此，现有的3DGS压缩方法仍然依赖相对较多的高斯算法，主要集中在属性压缩。这是因为较小的高斯集对有损属性压缩越来越敏感，导致严重的质量下降。由于高斯数量直接与计算成本相关，因此有效减少高斯数量而不仅仅是优化存储至关重要。本文提出了优化最小高斯表示（OMG），该方法在使用最少原语数量的同时显著减少存储。首先，我们确定高斯分布与近高斯分布，最大限度地减少冗余而不牺牲质量。其次，我们提出一种紧凑且精确的属性表示，能够高效捕捉原节点之间的连续性和不规则性。此外，我们提出了一种子向量量化技术，以改进不规则表示，保持快速训练，且码本大小可忽略。大量实验表明，OMG相比之前的先进技术将存储需求降低近50%，实现600+ FPS渲染，同时保持高渲染质量。我们的源代码可在 https://maincold2.github.io/omg/ 获取。  

### [9] SAGE: Semantic-Driven Adaptive Gaussian Splatting in Extended Reality  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-20  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Chiara Schiavo, Elena Camuffo, Leonardo Badia, Simone Milani  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16747v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has significantly improved the efficiency and realism of three-dimensional scene visualization in several applications, ranging from robotics to eXtended Reality (XR). This work presents SAGE (Semantic-Driven Adaptive Gaussian Splatting in Extended Reality), a novel framework designed to enhance the user experience by dynamically adapting the Level of Detail (LOD) of different 3DGS objects identified via a semantic segmentation. Experimental results demonstrate how SAGE effectively reduces memory and computational overhead while keeping a desired target visual quality, thus providing a powerful optimization for interactive XR applications.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）显著提升了三维场景可视化的效率和真实度，应用于机器人技术到扩展现实（XR）。本研究提出了SAGE（扩展现实中的语义驱动自适应高斯喷涂），这是一个新颖框架，旨在通过语义分割动态调整不同3DGS对象的细节级别（LOD），提升用户体验。实验结果表明，SAGE如何有效降低内存和计算开销，同时保持理想的视觉质量，从而为交互式XR应用提供强有力的优化。  

### [10] 1000+ FPS 4D Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Scene Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-20  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuheng Yuan, Qiuhong Shen, Xingyi Yang, Xinchao Wang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16422v1)   · [Project](https://4DGS-1K.github.io.)  
- **📝摘要**：4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) has recently gained considerable attention as a method for reconstructing dynamic scenes. Despite achieving superior quality, 4DGS typically requires substantial storage and suffers from slow rendering speed. In this work, we delve into these issues and identify two key sources of temporal redundancy. (Q1) \textbf{Short-Lifespan Gaussians}: 4DGS uses a large portion of Gaussians with short temporal span to represent scene dynamics, leading to an excessive number of Gaussians. (Q2) \textbf{Inactive Gaussians}: When rendering, only a small subset of Gaussians contributes to each frame. Despite this, all Gaussians are processed during rasterization, resulting in redundant computation overhead. To address these redundancies, we present \textbf{4DGS-1K}, which runs at over 1000 FPS on modern GPUs. For Q1, we introduce the Spatial-Temporal Variation Score, a new pruning criterion that effectively removes short-lifespan Gaussians while encouraging 4DGS to capture scene dynamics using Gaussians with longer temporal spans. For Q2, we store a mask for active Gaussians across consecutive frames, significantly reducing redundant computations in rendering. Compared to vanilla 4DGS, our method achieves a $41\times$ reduction in storage and $9\times$ faster rasterization speed on complex dynamic scenes, while maintaining comparable visual quality. Please see our project page at https://4DGS-1K.github.io.  
- **📝翻译**: 4D高斯喷溅（4DGS）近年来作为重建动态场景的方法受到了广泛关注。尽管质量更高，4DGS通常需要大量存储空间，且渲染速度较慢。在本研究中，我们深入探讨这些问题，并识别出时间冗余的两个关键来源。（Q1） \textbf{短寿命高斯分布}：4DGS使用大量短时间跨度的高斯分布来表示场景动态，导致高斯分布过多。（Q2） \textbf{非活跃高斯分布}：在渲染时，每个帧中只有一小部分高斯分布。尽管如此，所有高斯分布在光栅化过程中都会被处理，导致计算开销增加冗余。为了解决这些冗余问题，我们介绍了 \textbf{4DGS-1K}，它在现代 GPU 上运行速度超过 1000 FPS。在Q1，我们引入了时空变异评分，这是一种新的剪枝标准，有效去除寿命短的高斯分布，同时鼓励4DGS利用时间跨度较长的高斯分布来捕捉场景动态。对于Q2，我们为连续帧间的活跃高斯量存储掩码，显著减少渲染中的冗余计算。与原版4DGS相比，我们的方法在复杂动态场景中实现了存储空间减少41美元/倍/美元，光栅化速度提升9美元/倍/倍，同时保持了相当的视觉质量。请参阅我们的项目页面，https://4DGS-1K.github.io。  

### [11] Deep Polycuboid Fitting for Compact 3D Representation of Indoor Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-19（更新：2025-03-24）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Gahye Lee, Hyejeong Yoon, Jungeon Kim, Seungyong Lee  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to 3DV 2025. For project page, see this https://waldstein94.github.io/deep-polycuboid-fitting/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14912v2)    
- **📝摘要**：This paper presents a novel framework for compactly representing a 3D indoor scene using a set of polycuboids through a deep learning-based fitting method. Indoor scenes mainly consist of man-made objects, such as furniture, which often exhibit rectilinear geometry. This property allows indoor scenes to be represented using combinations of polycuboids, providing a compact representation that benefits downstream applications like furniture rearrangement. Our framework takes a noisy point cloud as input and first detects six types of cuboid faces using a transformer network. Then, a graph neural network is used to validate the spatial relationships of the detected faces to form potential polycuboids. Finally, each polycuboid instance is reconstructed by forming a set of boxes based on the aggregated face labels. To train our networks, we introduce a synthetic dataset encompassing a diverse range of cuboid and polycuboid shapes that reflect the characteristics of indoor scenes. Our framework generalizes well to real-world indoor scene datasets, including Replica, ScanNet, and scenes captured with an iPhone. The versatility of our method is demonstrated through practical applications, such as virtual room tours and scene editing.  
- **📝翻译**: 本文提出了一种新颖框架，用于通过基于深度学习的拟合方法，利用一组多方体紧凑地表示三维室内场景。室内场景主要由人造物品组成，如家具，常表现出直线几何形状。该特性允许通过多方体组合来表示室内场景，提供紧凑的表示，有利于家具重新布置等后续应用。我们的框架以噪声点云为输入，首先利用变压器网络检测六种长方体面。然后，利用图神经网络验证检测面的空间关系，以形成潜在的多方体。最后，每个多方体实例通过基于聚合的面标签组成一组方框来重建。为了训练网络，我们引入了一个综合数据集，涵盖了反映室内场景特征的多方体和多方体形状。我们的框架很好地推广到现实世界的室内场景数据集，包括Replica、ScanNet以及用iPhone捕获的场景。我们方法的多功能性通过实际应用得以体现，如虚拟房间导览和场景剪辑。  

### [12] Improving Adaptive Density Control for 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-18  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Glenn Grubert, Florian Barthel, Anna Hilsmann, Peter Eisert  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14274v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become one of the most influential works in the past year. Due to its efficient and high-quality novel view synthesis capabilities, it has been widely adopted in many research fields and applications. Nevertheless, 3DGS still faces challenges to properly manage the number of Gaussian primitives that are used during scene reconstruction. Following the adaptive density control (ADC) mechanism of 3D Gaussian Splatting, new Gaussians in under-reconstructed regions are created, while Gaussians that do not contribute to the rendering quality are pruned. We observe that those criteria for densifying and pruning Gaussians can sometimes lead to worse rendering by introducing artifacts. We especially observe under-reconstructed background or overfitted foreground regions. To encounter both problems, we propose three new improvements to the adaptive density control mechanism. Those include a correction for the scene extent calculation that does not only rely on camera positions, an exponentially ascending gradient threshold to improve training convergence, and significance-aware pruning strategy to avoid background artifacts. With these adaptions, we show that the rendering quality improves while using the same number of Gaussians primitives. Furthermore, with our improvements, the training converges considerably faster, allowing for more than twice as fast training times while yielding better quality than 3DGS. Finally, our contributions are easily compatible with most existing derivative works of 3DGS making them relevant for future works.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）成为过去一年中最具影响力的作品之一。由于其高效且高质量的新颖视图合成能力，它已被广泛应用于许多研究领域和应用中。尽管如此，3DGS在正确管理场景重建中使用的高斯原语数量方面仍面临挑战。根据三维高斯喷溅的自适应密度控制（ADC）机制，在未重建区域生成新的高斯分布，而不对渲染质量贡献不大的则被修剪。我们观察到，这些高斯密度化和修剪的标准有时会导致渲染质量下降，因为引入了伪影。我们特别观察到背景重建不足或前景区域过拟合。为解决这两个问题，我们提出了三项适应性密度控制机制的新改进方案。这些包括对场景扩展计算的修正，不仅依赖摄像机位置;采用指数级递增的梯度阈值以改善训练收敛性;以及显著性感知修剪策略以避免背景伪影。通过这些调整，我们证明在使用相同数量的高斯原语时，渲染质量会有所提升。此外，随着我们的改进，训练收敛速度显著加快，训练时间是3DGS的两倍多，同时产出的质量也更好。最后，我们的贡献与大多数现有的3DGS衍生作品高度兼容，因此对未来作品具有相关意义。  

### [13] BG-Triangle: Bézier Gaussian Triangle for 3D Vectorization and Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-18  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Minye Wu, Haizhao Dai, Kaixin Yao, Tinne Tuytelaars, Jingyi Yu  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13961v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Differentiable rendering enables efficient optimization by allowing gradients to be computed through the rendering process, facilitating 3D reconstruction, inverse rendering and neural scene representation learning. To ensure differentiability, existing solutions approximate or re-formulate traditional rendering operations using smooth, probabilistic proxies such as volumes or Gaussian primitives. Consequently, they struggle to preserve sharp edges due to the lack of explicit boundary definitions. We present a novel hybrid representation, Bézier Gaussian Triangle (BG-Triangle), that combines Bézier triangle-based vector graphics primitives with Gaussian-based probabilistic models, to maintain accurate shape modeling while conducting resolution-independent differentiable rendering. We present a robust and effective discontinuity-aware rendering technique to reduce uncertainties at object boundaries. We also employ an adaptive densification and pruning scheme for efficient training while reliably handling level-of-detail (LoD) variations. Experiments show that BG-Triangle achieves comparable rendering quality as 3DGS but with superior boundary preservation. More importantly, BG-Triangle uses a much smaller number of primitives than its alternatives, showcasing the benefits of vectorized graphics primitives and the potential to bridge the gap between classic and emerging representations.  
- **📝翻译**: 可微渲染通过渲染过程计算梯度实现高效优化，促进3D重建、逆向渲染和神经场景表示学习。为确保可微性，现有解会用平滑的概率代理（如体积或高斯原语）近似或重新表述传统渲染作。因此，由于缺乏明确的边界定义，它们难以保持锋利的边缘。我们提出了一种新颖的混合表示方式——贝塞尔高斯三角形（BG-Triangle），它结合了基于贝济尔三角形的矢量图形原语与基于高斯的概率模型，以在进行分辨率无关的可微渲染的同时保持形状建模的准确性。我们提出了一种稳健且有效的不连续感知渲染技术，以减少物体边界的不确定性。我们还采用自适应的密度化和修剪方案，以高效训练，同时可靠地处理细节层级（LoD）变化。实验表明，BG-Triangle的渲染质量与3DGS相当，但边界保持更优。更重要的是，BG-Triangle 使用的基元数量远少于其他替代方案，展示了矢量图形基元的优势以及弥合经典与新兴表现方式之间的潜力。  

### [14] Light4GS: Lightweight Compact 4D Gaussian Splatting Generation via Context Model  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-18  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Mufan Liu, Qi Yang, He Huang et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13948v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an efficient and high-fidelity paradigm for novel view synthesis. To adapt 3DGS for dynamic content, deformable 3DGS incorporates temporally deformable primitives with learnable latent embeddings to capture complex motions. Despite its impressive performance, the high-dimensional embeddings and vast number of primitives lead to substantial storage requirements. In this paper, we introduce a \textbf{Light}weight \textbf{4}D\textbf{GS} framework, called Light4GS, that employs significance pruning with a deep context model to provide a lightweight storage-efficient dynamic 3DGS representation. The proposed Light4GS is based on 4DGS that is a typical representation of deformable 3DGS. Specifically, our framework is built upon two core components: (1) a spatio-temporal significance pruning strategy that eliminates over 64\% of the deformable primitives, followed by an entropy-constrained spherical harmonics compression applied to the remainder; and (2) a deep context model that integrates intra- and inter-prediction with hyperprior into a coarse-to-fine context structure to enable efficient multiscale latent embedding compression. Our approach achieves over 120x compression and increases rendering FPS up to 20\% compared to the baseline 4DGS, and also superior to frame-wise state-of-the-art 3DGS compression methods, revealing the effectiveness of our Light4GS in terms of both intra- and inter-prediction methods without sacrificing rendering quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种高效且高精度的新型视图合成范式。为了适应动态内容，可变形3DGS包含了具有可学习潜在嵌入的时间变形原件以捕捉复杂运动。尽管性能令人印象深刻，但高维嵌入和大量图元导致存储需求较大。本文介绍了一个名为Light4GS的\textbf{Light}weight\textbf{4}D\textbf{GS}框架，采用深度上下文模型进行显著修剪，提供轻量级存储高效的动态3DGS表示。所提议的Light4GS基于4DGS，这是可变形3DGS的典型表现。具体来说，我们的框架建立在两个核心组成部分之上：（1）时空意义修剪策略，去除超过64%的可变形原件，随后对剩余部分施加熵约束的球谐波压缩;以及（2）将预测内和间预测与超先验整合为粗到细的上下文结构，从而实现高效的多尺度潜在嵌入压缩。我们的方法实现了超过 120 倍的压缩，渲染帧率比基线 4DGS 提升了高达 20\p 的表现，同时在帧数方面优于最先进的 3DGS 压缩方法，展现了 Light4GS 在预测内和预测间方法上的高效表现，同时不牺牲渲染质量。  

### [15] CAT-3DGS Pro: A New Benchmark for Efficient 3DGS Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yu-Ting Zhan, He-bi Yang, Cheng-Yuan Ho, Jui-Chiu Chiang, Wen-Hsiao Peng  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12862v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown immense potential for novel view synthesis. However, achieving rate-distortion-optimized compression of 3DGS representations for transmission and/or storage applications remains a challenge. CAT-3DGS introduces a context-adaptive triplane hyperprior for end-to-end optimized compression, delivering state-of-the-art coding performance. Despite this, it requires prolonged training and decoding time. To address these limitations, we propose CAT-3DGS Pro, an enhanced version of CAT-3DGS that improves both compression performance and computational efficiency. First, we introduce a PCA-guided vector-matrix hyperprior, which replaces the triplane-based hyperprior to reduce redundant parameters. To achieve a more balanced rate-distortion trade-off and faster encoding, we propose an alternate optimization strategy (A-RDO). Additionally, we refine the sampling rate optimization method in CAT-3DGS, leading to significant improvements in rate-distortion performance. These enhancements result in a 46.6% BD-rate reduction and 3x speedup in training time on BungeeNeRF, while achieving 5x acceleration in decoding speed for the Amsterdam scene compared to CAT-3DGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）展现出了新颖视图合成的巨大潜力。然而，实现传输和/或存储应用中3DGS表示的速率失真优化压缩仍是一项挑战。CAT-3DGS引入了上下文自适应三平面超先后，实现端到端优化压缩，实现了最先进的编码性能。尽管如此，这需要长时间的训练和解码时间。为解决这些限制，我们提出了CAT-3DGS Pro，这是CAT-3DGS的增强版，提升了压缩性能和计算效率。首先，我们引入了主控主成分A（PCA）引导的向量矩阵超先验，取代了基于三平面的超先验，以减少冗余参数。为了实现更平衡的速率-失真权衡和更快的编码，我们提出了一种替代优化策略（A-RDO）。此外，我们在CAT-3DGS中优化采样率优化方法进行了改进，显著提升了速率-失真性能。这些改进使BungeeNeRF的BD速率降低了46.6%，训练时间提升了3倍，同时在阿姆斯特丹场景的解码速度上相比CAT-3DGS提升了5倍。  

### [16] CompMarkGS: Robust Watermarking for Compressed 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-17（更新：2025-09-29）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Sumin In, Youngdong Jang, Utae Jeong et al.  
- **📝说明**：33 pages, 19 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12836v6)    
- **📝摘要**：As 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is increasingly adopted in various academic and commercial applications due to its high-quality and real-time rendering capabilities, the need for copyright protection is growing. At the same time, its large model size requires efficient compression for storage and transmission. However, compression techniques, especially quantization-based methods, degrade the integrity of existing 3DGS watermarking methods, thus creating the need for a novel methodology that is robust against compression. To ensure reliable watermark detection under compression, we propose a compression-tolerant 3DGS watermarking method that preserves watermark integrity and rendering quality. Our approach utilizes an anchor-based 3DGS, embedding the watermark into anchor attributes, particularly the anchor feature, to enhance security and rendering quality. We also propose a quantization distortion layer that injects quantization noise during training, preserving the watermark after quantization-based compression. Moreover, we employ a frequency-aware anchor growing strategy that enhances rendering quality by effectively identifying Gaussians in high-frequency regions, and an HSV loss to mitigate color artifacts for further rendering quality improvement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed method preserves the watermark even under compression and maintains high rendering quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 随着3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）因其高质量和实时渲染能力被越来越多的学术和商业应用采用，版权保护的需求也在不断增长。同时，其庞大的模型体积要求高效的压缩以实现存储和传输。然而，压缩技术，尤其是基于量化的方法，会降低现有3DGS水印方法的完整性，因此需要一种对压缩具有鲁棒性的新型方法论。为了确保压缩下的可靠水印检测，我们提出了一种耐压缩的3DGS水印方法，既能保持水印完整性，也能保持渲染质量。我们的方法采用基于锚点的3DGS，将水印嵌入锚属性中，尤其是锚点功能，以提升安全性和渲染质量。我们还提出了一种量化失真层，在训练过程中注入量化噪声，在基于量化的压缩后保持水印。此外，我们采用了频率感知锚点增长策略，通过有效识别高斯分布（Gaussian）来提升渲染质量，同时通过HSV损失来减轻色彩伪影，进一步提升渲染质量。大量实验表明，我们提出的方法即使在压缩下也能保持水印，并保持高质量的渲染质量。  

### [17] Swift4D:Adaptive divide-and-conquer Gaussian Splatting for compact and efficient reconstruction of dynamic scene  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-16  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiahao Wu, Rui Peng, Zhiyan Wang et al.  
- **📝说明**：ICLR 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12307v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Novel view synthesis has long been a practical but challenging task, although the introduction of numerous methods to solve this problem, even combining advanced representations like 3D Gaussian Splatting, they still struggle to recover high-quality results and often consume too much storage memory and training time. In this paper we propose Swift4D, a divide-and-conquer 3D Gaussian Splatting method that can handle static and dynamic primitives separately, achieving a good trade-off between rendering quality and efficiency, motivated by the fact that most of the scene is the static primitive and does not require additional dynamic properties. Concretely, we focus on modeling dynamic transformations only for the dynamic primitives which benefits both efficiency and quality. We first employ a learnable decomposition strategy to separate the primitives, which relies on an additional parameter to classify primitives as static or dynamic. For the dynamic primitives, we employ a compact multi-resolution 4D Hash mapper to transform these primitives from canonical space into deformation space at each timestamp, and then mix the static and dynamic primitives to produce the final output. This divide-and-conquer method facilitates efficient training and reduces storage redundancy. Our method not only achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while being 20X faster in training than previous SOTA methods with a minimum storage requirement of only 30MB on real-world datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/WuJH2001/swift4d.  
- **📝翻译**: 新颖视图合成长期以来是一项实用但具有挑战性的任务，尽管引入了多种方法来解决这一问题，甚至结合了三维高斯喷溅等先进表示，但它们仍然难以恢复高质量的结果，且常常消耗过多的存储内存和训练时间。本文提出了Swift4D，一种分治三维高斯喷涂方法，可以分别处理静态和动态图元，在渲染质量和效率之间取得了良好权衡，原因是大部分场景是静态图元，不需要额外的动态属性。具体来说，我们只专注于对动态原语建模动态变换，这既有利于效率，也提升了质量。我们首先采用可学习的分解策略来分离原语，这依赖于一个额外的参数来区分原语为静态或动态。对于动态图元，我们使用紧凑的多分辨率4D哈希映射器，在每个时间戳处将这些图元从规范空间转换为变形空间，然后将静态图元和动态图元混合生成最终输出。这种分而治之的方法有助于高效培训并减少存储冗余。我们的方法不仅实现了最先进的渲染质量，同时训练速度比以往的SOTA方法快20倍，且在真实数据集上最低存储需求仅为30MB。代码可在 https://github.com/WuJH2001/swift4d 获取。  

### [18] PCGS: Progressive Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yihang Chen, Mengyao Li, Qianyi Wu et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project Page: https://yihangchen-ee.github.io/project_pcgs/ Code: https://github.com/YihangChen-ee/PCGS  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08511v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves impressive rendering fidelity and speed for novel view synthesis. However, its substantial data size poses a significant challenge for practical applications. While many compression techniques have been proposed, they fail to efficiently utilize existing bitstreams in on-demand applications due to their lack of progressivity, leading to a waste of resource. To address this issue, we propose PCGS (Progressive Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting), which adaptively controls both the quantity and quality of Gaussians (or anchors) to enable effective progressivity for on-demand applications. Specifically, for quantity, we introduce a progressive masking strategy that incrementally incorporates new anchors while refining existing ones to enhance fidelity. For quality, we propose a progressive quantization approach that gradually reduces quantization step sizes to achieve finer modeling of Gaussian attributes. Furthermore, to compact the incremental bitstreams, we leverage existing quantization results to refine probability prediction, improving entropy coding efficiency across progressive levels. Overall, PCGS achieves progressivity while maintaining compression performance comparable to SoTA non-progressive methods. Code available at: github.com/YihangChen-ee/PCGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）实现了令人印象深刻的渲染精细和速度，实现了新颖视角合成。然而，其庞大的数据量对实际应用构成了重大挑战。虽然已有许多压缩技术被提出，但由于缺乏渐进性，它们无法有效利用现有比特流，导致资源浪费。为解决这一问题，我们提出了PCGS（3D高斯散画的渐进压缩），该方法自适应地控制高斯分布（或锚点）的数量和质量，从而实现按需应用的有效渐进性。具体来说，在数量方面，我们引入了渐进式掩码策略，逐步引入新锚点，同时精炼现有锚点以提升真实度。为了提高质量，我们提出了一种渐进量子化方法，逐步减少量子化步长，以实现高斯属性的更精细建模。此外，为了压缩增量比特流，我们利用现有的量化结果优化概率预测，提升了跨递进级熵编码的效率。总体而言，PCGS在保持压缩性能的同时实现了与SoTA非渐进方法相当的渐进性。代码可访问：github.com/YihangChen-ee/PCGS。  

### [19] MVGSR: Multi-View Consistency Gaussian Splatting for Robust Surface Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-11（更新：2025-03-13）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Chenfeng Hou, Qi Xun Yeo, Mengqi Guo et al.  
- **📝说明**：project page https://mvgsr.github.io  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08093v2)   · [Project](https://mvgsr.github.io).)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has gained significant attention for its high-quality rendering capabilities, ultra-fast training, and inference speeds. However, when we apply 3DGS to surface reconstruction tasks, especially in environments with dynamic objects and distractors, the method suffers from floating artifacts and color errors due to inconsistency from different viewpoints. To address this challenge, we propose Multi-View Consistency Gaussian Splatting for the domain of Robust Surface Reconstruction (\textbf{MVGSR}), which takes advantage of lightweight Gaussian models and a {heuristics-guided distractor masking} strategy for robust surface reconstruction in non-static environments. Compared to existing methods that rely on MLPs for distractor segmentation strategies, our approach separates distractors from static scene elements by comparing multi-view feature consistency, allowing us to obtain precise distractor masks early in training. Furthermore, we introduce a pruning measure based on multi-view contributions to reset transmittance, effectively reducing floating artifacts. Finally, a multi-view consistency loss is applied to achieve high-quality performance in surface reconstruction tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that MVGSR achieves competitive geometric accuracy and rendering fidelity compared to the state-of-the-art surface reconstruction algorithms. More information is available on our project page (https://mvgsr.github.io).  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）因其高质量渲染能力、超高速训练和推理速度而备受关注。然而，当我们将3DGS应用于表面重建任务时，尤其是在具有动态对象和干扰的环境中，由于不同视角的不一致，该方法存在漂浮伪影和色彩错误。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了多视图一致性高斯喷涂，应用于鲁棒表面重建（\textbf{MVGSR}）领域，利用轻量级高斯模型和{启发式引导干扰掩蔽}策略，在非静态环境中实现稳健表面重建。与依赖MLP进行分散策略的方法相比，我们的方法通过比较多视图特征一致性，将分散因素与静态场景元素区分开来，使我们能够在训练早期获得精确的干扰掩码。此外，我们引入了基于多视角贡献的剪枝措施，以重置传输，有效减少浮动伪影。最后，应用多视图一致性丢失以实现表面重建任务的高质量性能。实验结果表明，MVGSR在几何精度和渲染准确度上均优于最先进的曲面重建算法。更多信息可见我们的项目页面（https://mvgsr.github.io）。  

### [20] DirectTriGS: Triplane-based Gaussian Splatting Field Representation for 3D Generation  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-10  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xiaoliang Ju, Hongsheng Li  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by CVPR 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06900v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present DirectTriGS, a novel framework designed for 3D object generation with Gaussian Splatting (GS). GS-based rendering for 3D content has gained considerable attention recently. However, there has been limited exploration in directly generating 3D Gaussians compared to traditional generative modeling approaches. The main challenge lies in the complex data structure of GS represented by discrete point clouds with multiple channels. To overcome this challenge, we propose employing the triplane representation, which allows us to represent Gaussian Splatting as an image-like continuous field. This representation effectively encodes both the geometry and texture information, enabling smooth transformation back to Gaussian point clouds and rendering into images by a TriRenderer, with only 2D supervisions. The proposed TriRenderer is fully differentiable, so that the rendering loss can supervise both texture and geometry encoding. Furthermore, the triplane representation can be compressed using a Variational Autoencoder (VAE), which can subsequently be utilized in latent diffusion to generate 3D objects. The experiments demonstrate that the proposed generation framework can produce high-quality 3D object geometry and rendering results in the text-to-3D task.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍DirectTriGS，一个为高斯喷溅（GS）三维对象生成设计的新框架。基于GS的3D内容渲染近年来引起了广泛关注。然而，与传统生成建模方法相比，直接生成三维高斯图的探索还有限。主要挑战在于GS复杂的数据结构，由多通道的离散点云表示。为克服这一挑战，我们提出采用三平面表示法，使高斯喷溅表现为类似图像的连续场。该表示方式有效编码几何和纹理信息，使得平滑地变换回高斯点云，并可通过TriRenderer仅需二维监督即可渲染为图像。拟议的TriRenderer是完全可微的，因此渲染损耗可以同时监督纹理和几何编码。此外，三平面表示还可以通过变分自编码器（VAE）进行压缩，随后可在潜在扩散中生成三维物体。实验表明，所提出的生成框架能够在文本转三维任务中生成高质量的三维物体几何和渲染结果。  

### [21] Feature-EndoGaussian: Feature Distilled Gaussian Splatting in Surgical Deformable Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-08（更新：2025-11-12）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Kai Li, Junhao Wang, William Han, Ding Zhao  
- **📝说明**：17 pages, 5 figures; Accepted to ML4H 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06161v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Minimally invasive surgery (MIS) requires high-fidelity, real-time visual feedback of dynamic and low-texture surgical scenes. To address these requirements, we introduce FeatureEndo-4DGS (FE-4DGS), the first real time pipeline leveraging feature-distilled 4D Gaussian Splatting for simultaneous reconstruction and semantic segmentation of deformable surgical environments. Unlike prior feature-distilled methods restricted to static scenes, and existing 4D approaches that lack semantic integration, FE-4DGS seamlessly leverages pre-trained 2D semantic embeddings to produce a unified 4D representation-where semantics also deform with tissue motion. This unified approach enables the generation of real-time RGB and semantic outputs through a single, parallelized rasterization process. Despite the additional complexity from feature distillation, FE-4DGS sustains real-time rendering (61 FPS) with a compact footprint, achieves state-of-the-art rendering fidelity on EndoNeRF (39.1 PSNR) and SCARED (27.3 PSNR), and delivers competitive EndoVis18 segmentation, matching or exceeding strong 2D baselines for binary segmentation tasks (0.93 DSC) and remaining competitive for multi-label segmentation (0.77 DSC).  
- **📝翻译**: 微创手术（MIS）需要对动态且低质地的手术场景进行高精度、实时的视觉反馈。为满足这些需求，我们推出了FeatureEndo-4DGS（FE-4DGS），这是首个利用特征提炼4D高斯喷溅技术实现可变形手术环境同时重建和语义分割的实时流水线。与之前仅限于静态场景的特征提炼方法和缺乏语义整合的现有4D方法不同，FE-4DGS无缝利用预训练的2D语义嵌入，生成统一的4D表示——语义还会随着组织运动而变形。这种统一的方法使得通过单一的并行光栅化过程生成实时RGB和语义输出成为可能。尽管特征提取增加了复杂度，FE-4DGS 仍能以紧凑的体积维持实时渲染（61 FPS），在 EndoNeRF（39.1 PSNR）和 SCARED（27.3 PSNR）上实现最先进的渲染精度，并在 EndoVis18 分割中具有竞争力，在二元分割任务中匹配甚至超过强 2D 基线（0.93 DSC），并在多标签分割（0.77 DSC）中保持竞争力。  

### [22] SecureGS: Boosting the Security and Fidelity of 3D Gaussian Splatting Steganography  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-08  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xuanyu Zhang, Jiarui Meng, Zhipei Xu et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by ICLR 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06118v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a premier method for 3D representation due to its real-time rendering and high-quality outputs, underscoring the critical need to protect the privacy of 3D assets. Traditional NeRF steganography methods fail to address the explicit nature of 3DGS since its point cloud files are publicly accessible. Existing GS steganography solutions mitigate some issues but still struggle with reduced rendering fidelity, increased computational demands, and security flaws, especially in the security of the geometric structure of the visualized point cloud. To address these demands, we propose a SecureGS, a secure and efficient 3DGS steganography framework inspired by Scaffold-GS's anchor point design and neural decoding. SecureGS uses a hybrid decoupled Gaussian encryption mechanism to embed offsets, scales, rotations, and RGB attributes of the hidden 3D Gaussian points in anchor point features, retrievable only by authorized users through privacy-preserving neural networks. To further enhance security, we propose a density region-aware anchor growing and pruning strategy that adaptively locates optimal hiding regions without exposing hidden information. Extensive experiments show that SecureGS significantly surpasses existing GS steganography methods in rendering fidelity, speed, and security.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）因其实时渲染和高质量输出，已成为3D表现的首选方法，凸显了保护3D资产隐私的重要性。传统的NeRF隐写方法未能解决3DGS的显式特性，因为其点云文件是公开可访问的。现有的GS隐写解决方案缓解了一些问题，但仍面临渲染精度下降、计算需求增加和安全缺陷，尤其是在可视化点云几何结构的安全性方面。为满足这些需求，我们提出了一个SecureGS，这是一个受Scaffold-GS锚点设计和神经解码启发的安全高效3DGS隐写框架。SecureGS采用混合解耦高斯加密机制，将隐藏3D高斯点的偏移量、比例、旋转和RGB属性嵌入锚点特征中，只有授权用户通过隐私保护的神经网络才能检索。为进一步提升安全性，我们提出了一种密度区域感知锚点生长和修剪策略，能够自适应定位最佳隐藏区域，同时不暴露隐藏信息。大量实验表明，SecureGS在实现保真度、速度和安全性方面显著优于现有的GS隐写方法。  

### [23] D2GV: Deformable 2D Gaussian Splatting for Video Representation in 400FPS  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-07  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Mufan Liu, Qi Yang, Miaoran Zhao et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05600v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) have emerged as a powerful approach for video representation, offering versatility across tasks such as compression and inpainting. However, their implicit formulation limits both interpretability and efficacy, undermining their practicality as a comprehensive solution. We propose a novel video representation based on deformable 2D Gaussian splatting, dubbed D2GV, which aims to achieve three key objectives: 1) improved efficiency while delivering superior quality; 2) enhanced scalability and interpretability; and 3) increased friendliness for downstream tasks. Specifically, we initially divide the video sequence into fixed-length Groups of Pictures (GoP) to allow parallel training and linear scalability with video length. For each GoP, D2GV represents video frames by applying differentiable rasterization to 2D Gaussians, which are deformed from a canonical space into their corresponding timestamps. Notably, leveraging efficient CUDA-based rasterization, D2GV converges fast and decodes at speeds exceeding 400 FPS, while delivering quality that matches or surpasses state-of-the-art INRs. Moreover, we incorporate a learnable pruning and quantization strategy to streamline D2GV into a more compact representation. We demonstrate D2GV's versatility in tasks including video interpolation, inpainting and denoising, underscoring its potential as a promising solution for video representation. Code is available at: https://github.com/Evan-sudo/D2GV.  
- **📝翻译**: 隐式神经表征（INR）已成为视频表现的强大方法，在压缩和修饰等任务中具有多样性。然而，其隐含表述限制了可解释性和有效性，削弱了其作为全面解决方案的实用性。我们提出了一种基于可变形二维高斯喷溅的新视频表示方式，称为D2GV，旨在实现三个关键目标：1）在提供卓越质量的同时提高效率;2）增强可扩展性和可解释性;以及3）提升下游任务的友好性。具体来说，我们最初将视频序列划分为固定长度的图片组（GoP），以便并行训练并根据视频长度线性扩展。对于每个GoP，D2GV通过对二维高斯量应用可微光栅化来表示视频帧，这些高斯从典范空间变形为对应的时间戳。值得注意的是，借助高效的CUDA光栅化技术，D2GV收敛速度快，解码速度超过400 FPS，同时提供媲美甚至超越最先进INR的质量。此外，我们还采用了可学习的剪枝和量子化策略，将D2GV简化为更紧凑的表示。我们展示了D2GV在视频插值、修复和去噪等任务中的多功能性，凸显其作为视频表现解决方案的潜力。代码可访问：https://github.com/Evan-sudo/D2GV。  

### [24] Persistent Object Gaussian Splat (POGS) for Tracking Human and Robot Manipulation of Irregularly Shaped Objects  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-07  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Justin Yu, Kush Hari, Karim El-Refai et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to ICRA 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05189v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Tracking and manipulating irregularly-shaped, previously unseen objects in dynamic environments is important for robotic applications in manufacturing, assembly, and logistics. Recently introduced Gaussian Splats efficiently model object geometry, but lack persistent state estimation for task-oriented manipulation. We present Persistent Object Gaussian Splat (POGS), a system that embeds semantics, self-supervised visual features, and object grouping features into a compact representation that can be continuously updated to estimate the pose of scanned objects. POGS updates object states without requiring expensive rescanning or prior CAD models of objects. After an initial multi-view scene capture and training phase, POGS uses a single stereo camera to integrate depth estimates along with self-supervised vision encoder features for object pose estimation. POGS supports grasping, reorientation, and natural language-driven manipulation by refining object pose estimates, facilitating sequential object reset operations with human-induced object perturbations and tool servoing, where robots recover tool pose despite tool perturbations of up to 30°. POGS achieves up to 12 consecutive successful object resets and recovers from 80% of in-grasp tool perturbations.  
- **📝翻译**: 在动态环境中追踪和作不规则形状、此前未见过的物体对于制造、组装和物流中的机器人应用至关重要。新近引入的高斯Splat高效建模对象几何，但缺乏针对任务作的持久状态估计。我们介绍持久对象高斯分布（POGS），这是一种将语义、自监督视觉特征和对象分组特征嵌入紧凑表示的系统，该表述可持续更新以估计扫描对象的姿态。POGS更新对象状态时无需昂贵的重新扫描或之前的CAD模型。经过初步多视角场景捕捉和训练阶段后，POGS使用单台立体相机整合深度估计及自监督视觉编码器功能，用于物体姿态估计。POGS支持抓取、重新定向和自然语言驱动的作，通过细化物体姿态估计，促进基于人为物体扰动和工具伺服的顺序重置作，机器人在工具扰动高达30°的情况下恢复工具姿势。POGS可连续实现多达12次成功重置对象，并能从80%的抓握工具扰动中恢复。  

### [25] EvolvingGS: High-Fidelity Streamable Volumetric Video via Evolving 3D Gaussian Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2025-03-07  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Chao Zhang, Yifeng Zhou, Shuheng Wang et al.
