To achieve this, we approximate the contribution of each Gaussian to the final scene and construct an order of prioritization on their inclusion in the rendering process. Additionally, we demonstrate that our approach can be combined with existing compression methods to progressively render (and stream) 3DGS scenes, optimizing bandwidth usage by focusing on the most important splats within a scene. Overall, our work establishes a foundation for making remotely hosted 3DGS content more quickly accessible to end-users in over-the-top consumption scenarios, with our results showing significant improvements in quality across all metrics compared to existing methods.  
- **📝翻译**: 在过去一年里，3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）因其能够以感知准确的方式表现3D场景而受到广泛关注。然而，由于每个splat的单独数据都必须存储，因此可能需要大量存储空间。虽然压缩技术通过减少内存占用提供了潜在解决方案，但它们仍然需要在渲染任何场景部分之前先取回整个场景。在本研究中，我们引入了一种新颖的方法，用于逐步渲染此类场景，旨在尽早显示接近最终场景的可见内容，同时不将整个场景加载到内存中。这种方法既有利于受内存限制的设备内渲染应用，也适用于偏好最小带宽使用率的流媒体应用。为此，我们近似每个高斯对最终场景的贡献，并根据其在渲染过程中的包含构建优先排序。此外，我们还展示了我们的方法可以与现有压缩方法结合，逐步渲染（并流式传输）3DGS场景，通过聚焦场景中最重要的碎片来优化带宽使用。总体而言，我们的工作为在OTP消费场景下更快地让终端用户访问远程托管的3DGS内容奠定了基础，结果显示所有指标的质量相较于现有方法都有显著提升。  


## August 2024

### [1] OG-Mapping: Octree-based Structured 3D Gaussians for Online Dense Mapping  
- **⏳发布**：2024-08-30  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Meng Wang, Junyi Wang, Changqun Xia, Chen Wang, Yue Qi  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.17223v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has recently demonstrated promising advancements in RGB-D online dense mapping. Nevertheless, existing methods excessively rely on per-pixel depth cues to perform map densification, which leads to significant redundancy and increased sensitivity to depth noise. Additionally, explicitly storing 3D Gaussian parameters of room-scale scene poses a significant storage challenge. In this paper, we introduce OG-Mapping, which leverages the robust scene structural representation capability of sparse octrees, combined with structured 3D Gaussian representations, to achieve efficient and robust online dense mapping. Moreover, OG-Mapping employs an anchor-based progressive map refinement strategy to recover the scene structures at multiple levels of detail. Instead of maintaining a small number of active keyframes with a fixed keyframe window as previous approaches do, a dynamic keyframe window is employed to allow OG-Mapping to better tackle false local minima and forgetting issues. Experimental results demonstrate that OG-Mapping delivers more robust and superior realism mapping results than existing Gaussian-based RGB-D online mapping methods with a compact model, and no additional post-processing is required.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷涂（3DGS）最近在RGB-D在线密集映射方面取得了有前景的进展。然而，现有方法过度依赖每像素深度线索来进行地图密度化，导致大量冗余和对深度噪声的敏感度增加。此外，显式存储室尺度场景的三维高斯参数也带来了重大存储挑战。本文介绍了OG-Mapping，利用稀疏八叉树的场景结构表示能力与结构化三维高斯表示相结合，实现高效且稳健的在线密集映射。此外，OG-Mapping 采用基于锚点的渐进式地图细化策略，在多个细节层面恢复场景结构。与以往方法中固定的关键帧窗口保持少量活跃关键帧不同，采用动态关键帧窗口，以便OG-Mapping更好地解决假本地最小值和遗忘问题。实验结果表明，OG-Mapping比现有基于高斯的高斯的RGB-D在线映射方法提供更稳健且更优的真实性映射结果，且模型紧凑，且无需额外的后处理。  

### [2] LapisGS: Layered Progressive 3D Gaussian Splatting for Adaptive Streaming  
- **⏳发布**：2024-08-27（更新：2025-02-10）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuang Shi, Géraldine Morin, Simone Gasparini, Wei Tsang Ooi  
- **📝说明**：3DV 2025; Project Page: https://yuang-ian.github.io/lapisgs/ ; Code: https://github.com/nus-vv-streams/lapis-gs  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14823v2)    
- **📝摘要**：The rise of Extended Reality (XR) requires efficient streaming of 3D online worlds, challenging current 3DGS representations to adapt to bandwidth-constrained environments. This paper proposes LapisGS, a layered 3DGS that supports adaptive streaming and progressive rendering. Our method constructs a layered structure for cumulative representation, incorporates dynamic opacity optimization to maintain visual fidelity, and utilizes occupancy maps to efficiently manage Gaussian splats. This proposed model offers a progressive representation supporting a continuous rendering quality adapted for bandwidth-aware streaming. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our approach in balancing visual fidelity with the compactness of the model, with up to 50.71% improvement in SSIM, 286.53% improvement in LPIPS with 23% of the original model size, and shows its potential for bandwidth-adapted 3D streaming and rendering applications.  
- **📝翻译**: 扩展现实（XR）的兴起要求高效流传输3D在线世界，挑战现有3DGS的表现方式以适应带宽受限的环境。本文提出了LapisGS，一种支持自适应流和渐进渲染的分层3DGS。我们的方法构建了叠加结构以实现累积表示，结合动态不透明度优化以保持视觉真实性，并利用占用图高效管理高斯散块。该模型提供了一种渐进式表示，支持连续渲染质量，适用于带宽感知流媒体。大量实验验证了我们方法在平衡视觉忠实度与模型紧凑度方面的有效性，SSIM提升高达50.71%，LPIPS提升286.53%，且模型规模为原始模型的23%，并展示了其在带宽适应3D流式和渲染应用中的潜力。  

### [3] H3D-DGS: Exploring Heterogeneous 3D Motion Representation for Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-08-23（更新：2025-10-21）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Bing He, Yunuo Chen, Guo Lu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13036v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Dynamic scene reconstruction poses a persistent challenge in 3D vision. Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as an effective method for this task, offering real-time rendering and high visual fidelity. This approach decomposes a dynamic scene into a static representation in a canonical space and time-varying scene motion. Scene motion is defined as the collective movement of all Gaussian points, and for compactness, existing approaches commonly adopt implicit neural fields or sparse control points. However, these methods predominantly rely on gradient-based optimization for all motion information. Due to the high degree of freedom, they struggle to converge on real-world datasets exhibiting complex motion. To preserve the compactness of motion representation and address convergence challenges, this paper proposes heterogeneous 3D control points, termed \textbf{H3D control points}, whose attributes are obtained using a hybrid strategy combining optical flow back-projection and gradient-based methods. This design decouples directly observable motion components from those that are geometrically occluded. Specifically, components of 3D motion that project onto the image plane are directly acquired via optical flow back projection, while unobservable portions are refined through gradient-based optimization. Experiments on the Neu3DV and CMU-Panoptic datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art deformable 3D Gaussian splatting techniques. Remarkably, our method converges within just 100 iterations and achieves a per-frame processing speed of 2 seconds on a single NVIDIA RTX 4070 GPU.  
- **📝翻译**: 动态场景重建在3D视觉中是一个持续的挑战。可变形3D高斯喷溅已成为该任务的有效方法，提供实时渲染和高视觉真实度。这种方法将动态场景分解为规范空间中的静态表示，并伴随时间变化的场景运动。场景运动被定义为所有高斯点的集体运动，为了简化，现有方法通常采用隐式神经场或稀疏控制点。然而，这些方法主要依赖基于梯度的优化来获取所有运动信息。由于高度自由度，它们难以与具有复杂运动的现实数据集收敛。为保持运动表示的紧凑性并解决收敛挑战，本文提出了异构三维控制点，称为\textbf{H3D控制点}，其属性通过结合光流反投影和梯度方法的混合策略获得。该设计将可直接观察的运动分量与几何遮挡分量分离。具体来说，投射到图像平面的三维运动分量通过光流回投影直接获取，而不可观察部分则通过基于梯度的优化进行精细化。在Neu3DV和CMU-Panoptic数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在性能上优于最先进的可变形三维高斯喷溅技术。令人惊讶的是，我们的方法仅需100次迭代就能收敛，单块NVIDIA RTX 4070显卡上每帧处理速度可达2秒。  

### [4] FLoD: Integrating Flexible Level of Detail into 3D Gaussian Splatting for Customizable Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2024-08-23（更新：2025-06-11）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yunji Seo, Young Sun Choi, Hyun Seung Son, Youngjung Uh  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://3dgs-flod.github.io/flod/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12894v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and its subsequent works are restricted to specific hardware setups, either on only low-cost or on only high-end configurations. Approaches aimed at reducing 3DGS memory usage enable rendering on low-cost GPU but compromise rendering quality, which fails to leverage the hardware capabilities in the case of higher-end GPU. Conversely, methods that enhance rendering quality require high-end GPU with large VRAM, making such methods impractical for lower-end devices with limited memory capacity. Consequently, 3DGS-based works generally assume a single hardware setup and lack the flexibility to adapt to varying hardware constraints.   To overcome this limitation, we propose Flexible Level of Detail (FLoD) for 3DGS. FLoD constructs a multi-level 3DGS representation through level-specific 3D scale constraints, where each level independently reconstructs the entire scene with varying detail and GPU memory usage. A level-by-level training strategy is introduced to ensure structural consistency across levels. Furthermore, the multi-level structure of FLoD allows selective rendering of image regions at different detail levels, providing additional memory-efficient rendering options. To our knowledge, among prior works which incorporate the concept of Level of Detail (LoD) with 3DGS, FLoD is the first to follow the core principle of LoD by offering adjustable options for a broad range of GPU settings.   Experiments demonstrate that FLoD provides various rendering options with trade-offs between quality and memory usage, enabling real-time rendering under diverse memory constraints. Furthermore, we show that FLoD generalizes to different 3DGS frameworks, indicating its potential for integration into future state-of-the-art developments.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）及其后续工作仅限于特定硬件配置，要么仅适用于低成本配置，要么仅限高端配置。旨在减少3DGS内存占用的方法，虽然允许在低成本GPU上渲染，但会牺牲渲染质量，无法充分利用高端GPU的硬件能力。相反，提升渲染质量的方法需要配备大显存的高端GPU，这使得这些方法对于内存容量有限的低端设备来说不切实际。因此，基于3DGS的工作通常假设单一硬件配置，缺乏适应不同硬件约束的灵活性。  为克服这一限制，我们提出了3DGS的灵活细节级别（FLoD）。FLoD 通过关卡特定的 3D 比例约束构建多层级 3DGS 表示，每个关卡独立重建整个场景，细节和 GPU 内存占用量各异。引入了逐级培训策略，以确保各级结构的一致性。此外，FLoD的多层结构允许在不同细节级别下选择性渲染图像区域，提供额外的内存高效渲染选项。据我们所知，在之前将细节层级（Level of Detail，LoD）概念融入3DGS的作品中，FLoD是首个遵循LoD核心原则，提供适用于广泛GPU设置的可调节选项的作品。  实验表明，FLoD提供了多种渲染选项，在质量和内存占用之间做出权衡，从而实现了在不同内存约束下实时渲染的可能。此外，我们展示了FLoD可推广到不同的3DGS框架，显示其未来集成到最先进开发中的潜力。  

### [5] GSFusion: Online RGB-D Mapping Where Gaussian Splatting Meets TSDF Fusion  
- **⏳发布**：2024-08-22（更新：2024-11-15）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiaxin Wei, Stefan Leutenegger  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12677v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Traditional volumetric fusion algorithms preserve the spatial structure of 3D scenes, which is beneficial for many tasks in computer vision and robotics. However, they often lack realism in terms of visualization. Emerging 3D Gaussian splatting bridges this gap, but existing Gaussian-based reconstruction methods often suffer from artifacts and inconsistencies with the underlying 3D structure, and struggle with real-time optimization, unable to provide users with immediate feedback in high quality. One of the bottlenecks arises from the massive amount of Gaussian parameters that need to be updated during optimization. Instead of using 3D Gaussian as a standalone map representation, we incorporate it into a volumetric mapping system to take advantage of geometric information and propose to use a quadtree data structure on images to drastically reduce the number of splats initialized. In this way, we simultaneously generate a compact 3D Gaussian map with fewer artifacts and a volumetric map on the fly. Our method, GSFusion, significantly enhances computational efficiency without sacrificing rendering quality, as demonstrated on both synthetic and real datasets. Code will be available at https://github.com/goldoak/GSFusion.  
- **📝翻译**: 传统的体积融合算法保留了三维场景的空间结构，这对计算机视觉和机器人领域的许多任务都非常有利。然而，它们在可视化方面往往缺乏真实感。新兴的三维高斯拼图弥合了这一空白，但现有基于高斯的高斯拼图方法常常存在伪影和与底层三维结构不一致的问题，且在实时优化方面遇到困难，无法为用户提供高质量的即时反馈。瓶颈之一来自优化过程中需要更新的大量高斯参数。我们没有将3D高斯作为独立的地图表示，而是将其集成到体积映射系统中，以利用几何信息，并提议在图像上使用四叉树数据结构，大幅减少初始化的溅点数量。通过这种方式，我们同时生成一个伪影较少的紧凑三维高斯映射和一个即时体积映射。我们的方法GSFusion显著提升了计算效率，同时不牺牲渲染质量，这一点在合成和真实数据集中均有体现。代码将在 https://github.com/goldoak/GSFusion 时提供。  

### [6] Implicit Gaussian Splatting with Efficient Multi-Level Tri-Plane Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2024-08-19（更新：2024-11-09）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Minye Wu, Tinne Tuytelaars  
- **📝说明**：Please note that the authors discovered configuration errors in the comparisons within the experiment section, resulting in unreliable quantitative results. We advise referencing the results in this paper with caution  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10041v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent advancements in photo-realistic novel view synthesis have been significantly driven by Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Nevertheless, the explicit nature of 3DGS data entails considerable storage requirements, highlighting a pressing need for more efficient data representations. To address this, we present Implicit Gaussian Splatting (IGS), an innovative hybrid model that integrates explicit point clouds with implicit feature embeddings through a multi-level tri-plane architecture. This architecture features 2D feature grids at various resolutions across different levels, facilitating continuous spatial domain representation and enhancing spatial correlations among Gaussian primitives. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a level-based progressive training scheme, which incorporates explicit spatial regularization. This method capitalizes on spatial correlations to enhance both the rendering quality and the compactness of the IGS representation. Furthermore, we propose a novel compression pipeline tailored for both point clouds and 2D feature grids, considering the entropy variations across different levels. Extensive experimental evaluations demonstrate that our algorithm can deliver high-quality rendering using only a few MBs, effectively balancing storage efficiency and rendering fidelity, and yielding results that are competitive with the state-of-the-art.  
- **📝翻译**: 照片级真实小说视图合成的最新进展主要得益于高斯喷溅（3DGS）。然而，3DGS数据的显式特性带来了相当大的存储需求，凸显了对更高效数据表示的紧迫需求。为此，我们提出了隐式高斯喷涂（IGS），这是一种创新的混合模型，通过多层三平面架构将显式点云与隐式特征嵌入集成。该架构采用不同分辨率、不同层次的二维特征网格，促进连续的空间域表示，并增强高斯原语之间的空间相关性。在此基础上，我们引入了基于层级的渐进式训练方案，其中包含明确的空间正则化。该方法利用空间相关性，提升IGS表示的渲染质量和紧凑性。此外，我们提出了一种针对点云和二维特征网格的新型压缩流水线，考虑不同层级间熵的变化。大量实验评估表明，我们的算法仅用几MB就能实现高质量渲染，在存储效率和渲染真实度之间取得了有效平衡，并产生了与最先进技术竞争的结果。  

### [7] Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting for Static and Dynamic Radiance Fields  
- **⏳发布**：2024-08-07  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Joo Chan Lee, Daniel Rho, Xiangyu Sun, Jong Hwan Ko, Eunbyung Park  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://maincold2.github.io/c3dgs/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.03822v1)   · [Project](https://maincold2.github.io/c3dgs/.)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has recently emerged as an alternative representation that leverages a 3D Gaussian-based representation and introduces an approximated volumetric rendering, achieving very fast rendering speed and promising image quality. Furthermore, subsequent studies have successfully extended 3DGS to dynamic 3D scenes, demonstrating its wide range of applications. However, a significant drawback arises as 3DGS and its following methods entail a substantial number of Gaussians to maintain the high fidelity of the rendered images, which requires a large amount of memory and storage. To address this critical issue, we place a specific emphasis on two key objectives: reducing the number of Gaussian points without sacrificing performance and compressing the Gaussian attributes, such as view-dependent color and covariance. To this end, we propose a learnable mask strategy that significantly reduces the number of Gaussians while preserving high performance. In addition, we propose a compact but effective representation of view-dependent color by employing a grid-based neural field rather than relying on spherical harmonics. Finally, we learn codebooks to compactly represent the geometric and temporal attributes by residual vector quantization. With model compression techniques such as quantization and entropy coding, we consistently show over 25x reduced storage and enhanced rendering speed compared to 3DGS for static scenes, while maintaining the quality of the scene representation. For dynamic scenes, our approach achieves more than 12x storage efficiency and retains a high-quality reconstruction compared to the existing state-of-the-art methods. Our work provides a comprehensive framework for 3D scene representation, achieving high performance, fast training, compactness, and real-time rendering. Our project page is available at https://maincold2.github.io/c3dgs/.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）最近作为一种替代表示形式出现，利用基于3D高斯的表示，并引入近似体积渲染，实现极快的渲染速度和有前景的图像质量。此外，后续研究成功将3DGS扩展到动态三维场景，展示了其广泛的应用。然而，一个重大缺点是3DGS及其后续方法需要大量高斯分布器来维持渲染图像的高保真度，这需要大量内存和存储空间。为解决这一关键问题，我们特别强调两个关键目标：在不牺牲性能的前提下减少高斯点数，以及压缩高斯属性，如视角依赖的颜色和协方差。为此，我们提出了一种可学习的掩膜策略，显著减少高斯分布数量，同时保持高性能。此外，我们提出了一种紧凑但有效的视角依赖色彩表示方法，采用基于网格的神经场，而非依赖球面谐波。最后，我们学习码本，通过残差矢量化紧凑地表示几何和时间属性。通过量化和熵编码等模型压缩技术，我们持续显示静态场景存储量比3DGS减少超过25倍，渲染速度提升，同时保持场景表现质量。对于动态场景，我们的方法实现了超过12倍的存储效率，并且与现有最先进的方法相比，还保持了高质量的重建。我们的工作为3D场景表示提供了全面的框架，实现高性能、快速训练、紧凑性和实时渲染。我们的项目页面可在 https://maincold2.github.io/c3dgs/ 访问。  

### [8] 3D hard sphere Boltzmann equation: explicit structure and the transition process from polynomial tail to Gaussian tail  
- **⏳发布**：2024-08-05  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yu-Chu Lin, Haitao Wang, Kung-Chien Wu  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02183v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We study the Boltzmann equation with hard sphere in a near-equilibrium setting. The initial data is compactly supported in the space variable and has a polynomial tail in the microscopic velocity. We show that the solution can be decomposed into a particle-like part (polynomial tail) and a fluid-like part (Gaussian tail). The particle-like part decays exponentially in both space and time, while the fluid-like part corresponds to the behavior of the compressible Navier-Stokes equation, which dominates the long time behavior and exhibits rich wave motion. The nonlinear wave interactions in the fluid-like part are precisely characterized and therefore we are able to distinguish the linear and nonlinear wave of the solution. It is notable that although the solution has polynomial tail in the velocity initially, the transition process from the polynomial to the Gaussian tail can be quantitatively revealed due to the collision with the background global Maxwellian.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们在近平衡条件下研究硬球面玻尔兹曼方程。初始数据在空间变量中紧密支持，并在微观速度下具有多项式尾部。我们证明该解可以分解为粒子状部分（多项式尾部）和流体状部分（高斯尾部）。粒子状部分在空间和时间上呈指数衰变，而流体状部分对应于可压缩纳维-斯托克斯方程的行为，该方程主导长时间行为并表现出丰富的波动运动。流体部分的非线性波相互作用被精确表征，因此我们能够区分解的线性波和非线性波。值得注意的是，尽管解最初速度带有多项式尾部，但由于与背景全局麦克斯韦的碰撞，可以定量揭示从多项式尾部到高斯尾部的转变过程。  


## July 2024

### [1] A Novel Gaussian filter-based Pressure Correction Technique with Super Compact Scheme for Unsteady 3D Incompressible, Viscous Flows  
- **⏳发布**：2024-07-26  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Ashwani Punia, Rajendra K. Ray  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19116v1)    
- **📝摘要**：This work deals with a novel Gaussian filter-based pressure correction technique with a super compact higher order finite difference scheme for solving unsteady three-dimensional (3D) incompressible, viscous flows. This pressure correction technique offers significant advantages in terms of optimizing computational time by taking minimum iterations to reach the required accuracy, making it highly efficient and cost-effective. Pressure fields often exhibit highly nonlinear behavior, and employing the Gaussian filter can help to enhance their reliability by reducing noise and uncertainties. On the other hand, the super compact scheme uses minimum grid points to produce second-order accuracy in time and fourth-order accuracy in space variables. The main focus of this study is to enhance the accuracy and efficiency and minimize the computational cost of solving complex fluid flow problems. The super compact scheme utilizes 19 grid points at the known time level (i.e., $n^{th}$ time level) and only seven grid points from the unknown time level (i.e., $n + 1$ time level). By employing the above strategies, it becomes possible to notably decrease computational expenses while maintaining the accuracy of the computational scheme for solving complex fluid flow problems. We have implemented our methodology across three distinct scenarios: the 3D Burger's equation having analytical solution, and two variations of the lid-driven cavity problem. The outcomes of our numerical simulations exhibit a remarkable concordance, aligning exceptionally well with both the analytical benchmarks and previously validated numerical findings for the cavity problems.  
- **📝翻译**: 这项工作涉及一种基于高斯滤波器的新型压力修正技术，采用超紧致的高阶有限差分方案，用于求解非稳态三维（3D）不可压缩粘性流动。这种压力校正技术在优化计算时间方面具有显著优势，通过最小迭代次数达到所需精度，使其高效且具成本效益。压力场通常表现出高度非线性的行为，采用高斯滤波器可以通过减少噪声和不确定性来提升其可靠性。另一方面，超紧致方案使用最小网格点，实现时间上的二阶精度和空间变量的四阶精度。本研究的主要目标是提高复杂流体流动问题的准确性和效率，并最小化计算成本。超紧方案利用已知时间层级（即$n^{th}$时间层级）上的19个网格点，而未知时间层级（即$n + 1$时间层级）仅有7个网格点。通过采用上述策略，可以在保持复杂流体流动问题计算方案精度的同时，显著降低计算成本。我们已在三种不同场景中实施了方法：具有解析解的三维伯格方程，以及两种由盖子驱动的腔问题变体。我们的数值模拟结果表现出显著的一致性，与解析基准和此前验证的空腔问题数值结果高度吻合。  

### [2] A Benchmark for Gaussian Splatting Compression and Quality Assessment Study  
- **⏳发布**：2024-07-19  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Qi Yang, Kaifa Yang, Yuke Xing, Yiling Xu, Zhu Li  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.14197v1)    
- **📝摘要**：To fill the gap of traditional GS compression method, in this paper, we first propose a simple and effective GS data compression anchor called Graph-based GS Compression (GGSC). GGSC is inspired by graph signal processing theory and uses two branches to compress the primitive center and attributes. We split the whole GS sample via KDTree and clip the high-frequency components after the graph Fourier transform. Followed by quantization, G-PCC and adaptive arithmetic coding are used to compress the primitive center and attribute residual matrix to generate the bitrate file. GGSS is the first work to explore traditional GS compression, with advantages that can reveal the GS distortion characteristics corresponding to typical compression operation, such as high-frequency clipping and quantization. Second, based on GGSC, we create a GS Quality Assessment dataset (GSQA) with 120 samples. A subjective experiment is conducted in a laboratory environment to collect subjective scores after rendering GS into Processed Video Sequences (PVS). We analyze the characteristics of different GS distortions based on Mean Opinion Scores (MOS), demonstrating the sensitivity of different attributes distortion to visual quality. The GGSC code and the dataset, including GS samples, MOS, and PVS, are made publicly available at https://github.com/Qi-Yangsjtu/GGSC.  
- **📝翻译**: 为了填补传统GS压缩方法的空白，本文首先提出了一种简单有效的GS数据压缩锚点，称为基于图的GS压缩（GGSC）。GGSC 受图信号处理理论启发，使用两个分支来压缩原始中心和属性。我们通过KDTree拆分整个GS样本，并在傅里叶变换图后裁剪高频成分。随后进行量化、G-PCC和自适应算术编码，压缩原中心并赋予残差矩阵属性，生成比特率文件。GGSS是首个探索传统GS压缩的作品，其优点是能够揭示典型压缩作对应的GS失真特性，如高频削波和量化。其次，基于GGSC，我们创建了一个包含120个样本的GS质量评估数据集（GSQA）。在实验室环境中进行主观实验，在将GS渲染成处理视频序列（PVS）后收集主观分数。我们基于平均意见评分（MOS）分析不同GS畸变的特征，展示了不同属性畸变对视觉质量的敏感性。GGSC代码及数据集，包括GS样本、MOS和PVS，均于 https://github.com/Qi-Yangsjtu/GGSC 公开公开。  

### [3] MVG-Splatting: Multi-View Guided Gaussian Splatting with Adaptive Quantile-Based Geometric Consistency Densification  
- **⏳发布**：2024-07-16  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhuoxiao Li, Shanliang Yao, Yijie Chu et al.  
- **📝说明**：https://mvgsplatting.github.io  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11840v1)    
- **📝摘要**：In the rapidly evolving field of 3D reconstruction, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) represent significant advancements. Although 2DGS compresses 3D Gaussian primitives into 2D Gaussian surfels to effectively enhance mesh extraction quality, this compression can potentially lead to a decrease in rendering quality. Additionally, unreliable densification processes and the calculation of depth through the accumulation of opacity can compromise the detail of mesh extraction. To address this issue, we introduce MVG-Splatting, a solution guided by Multi-View considerations. Specifically, we integrate an optimized method for calculating normals, which, combined with image gradients, helps rectify inconsistencies in the original depth computations. Additionally, utilizing projection strategies akin to those in Multi-View Stereo (MVS), we propose an adaptive quantile-based method that dynamically determines the level of additional densification guided by depth maps, from coarse to fine detail. Experimental evidence demonstrates that our method not only resolves the issues of rendering quality degradation caused by depth discrepancies but also facilitates direct mesh extraction from dense Gaussian point clouds using the Marching Cubes algorithm. This approach significantly enhances the overall fidelity and accuracy of the 3D reconstruction process, ensuring that both the geometric details and visual quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 在快速发展的三维重建领域，三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）和二维高斯喷溅（2DGS）代表了重大进展。虽然2DGS将3D高斯原语压缩到2D高斯波尔中以有效提升网格提取质量，但这种压缩可能导致渲染质量下降。此外，不可靠的致密过程以及通过不透明度积累计算深度，也可能影响网格提取的细节。为解决这个问题，我们引入了基于多视图考虑的MVG-Splatting解决方案。具体来说，我们集成了一种优化的法线计算方法，结合图像梯度，有助于纠正原始深度计算中的不一致。此外，我们采用类似多视角立体（MVS）的投影策略，提出了一种基于自适应的分位数方法，通过深度图动态决定从粗细到细致的额外密度水平。实验证据表明，我们的方法不仅解决了因深度差异导致的渲染质量下降问题，还能利用行进立方体算法直接从密集的高斯点云中提取网格。这种方法显著提升了3D重建过程的整体真实性和准确性，确保几何细节和视觉质量。  


## June 2024

### [1] Lightweight Predictive 3D Gaussian Splats  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-27  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Junli Cao, Vidit Goel, Chaoyang Wang et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project Page: https://plumpuddings.github.io/LPGS//  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19434v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent approaches representing 3D objects and scenes using Gaussian splats show increased rendering speed across a variety of platforms and devices. While rendering such representations is indeed extremely efficient, storing and transmitting them is often prohibitively expensive. To represent large-scale scenes, one often needs to store millions of 3D Gaussians, occupying gigabytes of disk space. This poses a very practical limitation, prohibiting widespread adoption.Several solutions have been proposed to strike a balance between disk size and rendering quality, noticeably reducing the visual quality. In this work, we propose a new representation that dramatically reduces the hard drive footprint while featuring similar or improved quality when compared to the standard 3D Gaussian splats. When compared to other compact solutions, ours offers higher quality renderings with significantly reduced storage, being able to efficiently run on a mobile device in real-time. Our key observation is that nearby points in the scene can share similar representations. Hence, only a small ratio of 3D points needs to be stored. We introduce an approach to identify such points which are called parent points. The discarded points called children points along with attributes can be efficiently predicted by tiny MLPs.  
- **📝翻译**: 最近用高斯碎片表示3D物体和场景的方法显示，在各种平台和设备上渲染速度都更快。虽然渲染此类表示确实极为高效，但存储和传输它们往往成本高昂。为了表示大尺度场景，通常需要存储数百万个三维高斯，占用数GB的磁盘空间。这带来了非常实际的限制，禁止广泛采用。已有多种解决方案被提出，旨在平衡磁盘大小和渲染质量，显著降低了视觉质量。在本研究中，我们提出了一种新的表示方式，可以大幅减少硬盘占地面积，同时与标准3D高斯散块相比，质量相当甚至更好。与其他紧凑型解决方案相比，我们的渲染质量更高，存储空间显著减少，能够高效地在移动设备上实时运行。我们的关键观察是，场景中相邻的点可以共享相似的表示。因此，只需存储少量三维点。我们引入一种方法来识别被称为父点的点。被丢弃的点称为子点和属性，可以被极小的MLP高效预测。  

### [2] Trimming the Fat: Efficient Compression of 3D Gaussian Splats through Pruning  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-26（更新：2024-07-29）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Muhammad Salman Ali, Maryam Qamar, Sung-Ho Bae, Enzo Tartaglione  
- **📝说明**：Accepted at BMVC 2024  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18214v2)    
- **📝摘要**：In recent times, the utilization of 3D models has gained traction, owing to the capacity for end-to-end training initially offered by Neural Radiance Fields and more recently by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) models. The latter holds a significant advantage by inherently easing rapid convergence during training and offering extensive editability. However, despite rapid advancements, the literature still lives in its infancy regarding the scalability of these models. In this study, we take some initial steps in addressing this gap, showing an approach that enables both the memory and computational scalability of such models. Specifically, we propose "Trimming the fat", a post-hoc gradient-informed iterative pruning technique to eliminate redundant information encoded in the model. Our experimental findings on widely acknowledged benchmarks attest to the effectiveness of our approach, revealing that up to 75% of the Gaussians can be removed while maintaining or even improving upon baseline performance. Our approach achieves around 50$\times$ compression while preserving performance similar to the baseline model, and is able to speed-up computation up to 600 FPS.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，三维模型的应用日益受到关注，这得益于最初由神经辐射场提供的端到端训练能力，最近则由三维高斯喷射（3DGS）模型提供。后者具有显著优势，即在训练过程中简化快速收敛并提供广泛的可编辑性。然而，尽管这些模型的可扩展性文献仍有快速进展。本研究初步采取措施弥补这一空白，展示了一种既能实现内存又具计算可扩展性的方法。具体来说，我们提出了“修剪脂肪”，一种事后梯度知情的迭代剪枝技术，旨在消除模型中编码的冗余信息。我们在广泛认可的基准测试上的实验结果证明了我们方法的有效性，显示在保持甚至提升基线性能的同时，可以去除多达75%的高斯分布。我们的方法在保持与基线模型相似的性能的同时，实现了大约50美元\时间的压缩，并能将计算速度提升至600 FPS。  

### [3] Reducing the Memory Footprint of 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-24  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Panagiotis Papantonakis, Georgios Kopanas, Bernhard Kerbl, Alexandre Lanvin, George Drettakis  
- **📝说明**：Project website: https://repo-sam.inria.fr/fungraph/reduced_3dgs/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.17074v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian splatting provides excellent visual quality for novel view synthesis, with fast training and real-time rendering; unfortunately, the memory requirements of this method for storing and transmission are unreasonably high. We first analyze the reasons for this, identifying three main areas where storage can be reduced: the number of 3D Gaussian primitives used to represent a scene, the number of coefficients for the spherical harmonics used to represent directional radiance, and the precision required to store Gaussian primitive attributes. We present a solution to each of these issues. First, we propose an efficient, resolution-aware primitive pruning approach, reducing the primitive count by half. Second, we introduce an adaptive adjustment method to choose the number of coefficients used to represent directional radiance for each Gaussian primitive, and finally a codebook-based quantization method, together with a half-float representation for further memory reduction. Taken together, these three components result in a 27 reduction in overall size on disk on the standard datasets we tested, along with a 1.7 speedup in rendering speed. We demonstrate our method on standard datasets and show how our solution results in significantly reduced download times when using the method on a mobile device.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷涂为新颖视图合成提供了极佳的视觉质量，支持快速训练和实时渲染;遗憾的是，这种方法在存储和传输时对内存的需求过高。我们首先分析原因，确定三个主要可减少存储空间的领域：用于表示场景的三维高斯原语数量、用于表示方向辐射的球面谐波系数，以及存储高斯原元素所需的精度。我们为这些问题提出了解决方案。首先，我们提出一种高效且分辨率感知的原始剪枝方法，将原始数量减半。其次，我们引入了自适应调整方法，用于选择每个高斯原的方向辐射度表示系数，最后采用基于码本的量子化方法，并结合半浮点表示以进一步减少内存。综合来看，这三个部分在我们测试的标准数据集中，磁盘整体体积减少了27%，渲染速度提升了1.7%。我们在标准数据集上演示了我们的方法，并展示了在移动设备上使用该方法时，我们的解决方案如何显著缩短下载时间。  

### [4] ClotheDreamer: Text-Guided Garment Generation with 3D Gaussians  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-24  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yufei Liu, Junshu Tang, Chu Zheng et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project Page: https://ggxxii.github.io/clothedreamer  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16815v1)   · [Project](https://ggxxii.github.io/clothedreamer.)  
- **📝摘要**：High-fidelity 3D garment synthesis from text is desirable yet challenging for digital avatar creation. Recent diffusion-based approaches via Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) have enabled new possibilities but either intricately couple with human body or struggle to reuse. We introduce ClotheDreamer, a 3D Gaussian-based method for generating wearable, production-ready 3D garment assets from text prompts. We propose a novel representation Disentangled Clothe Gaussian Splatting (DCGS) to enable separate optimization. DCGS represents clothed avatar as one Gaussian model but freezes body Gaussian splats. To enhance quality and completeness, we incorporate bidirectional SDS to supervise clothed avatar and garment RGBD renderings respectively with pose conditions and propose a new pruning strategy for loose clothing. Our approach can also support custom clothing templates as input. Benefiting from our design, the synthetic 3D garment can be easily applied to virtual try-on and support physically accurate animation. Extensive experiments showcase our method's superior and competitive performance. Our project page is at https://ggxxii.github.io/clothedreamer.  
- **📝翻译**: 高保真3D服装从文本合成是理想但具有挑战性的数字虚拟形象创建。近年来，基于扩散的方法通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）开辟了新的可能性，但要么与人体紧密结合，要么难以再利用。我们介绍ClotheDreamer，一种基于3D高斯的文本提示生成可穿戴、适量生产服装资产的方法。我们提出了一种新颖的表示法——解缠高斯喷溅（DCGS），以实现独立优化。DCGS将穿着衣服的化身呈现为一个高斯模型，但会冻结身体高斯溅射。为了提升质量和完整性，我们采用双向SDS来分别监控带有姿势条件的服装头像和服装RGBD渲染图，并提出了宽松服装的新修剪策略。我们的方法还可以支持自定义服装模板作为输入。借助我们的设计，合成3D服装可以轻松应用于虚拟试穿，并支持物理准确的动画。广泛的实验展示了我们方法的优越性和竞争力。我们的项目页面在 https://ggxxii.github.io/clothedreamer。  

### [5] LGS: A Light-weight 4D Gaussian Splatting for Efficient Surgical Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-23  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Hengyu Liu, Yifan Liu, Chenxin Li, Wuyang Li, Yixuan Yuan  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by MICCAI 2024. Project page: https://lgs-endo.github.io/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16073v1)    
- **📝摘要**：The advent of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) techniques and their dynamic scene modeling variants, 4D-GS, offers promising prospects for real-time rendering of dynamic surgical scenarios. However, the prerequisite for modeling dynamic scenes by a large number of Gaussian units, the high-dimensional Gaussian attributes and the high-resolution deformation fields, all lead to serve storage issues that hinder real-time rendering in resource-limited surgical equipment. To surmount these limitations, we introduce a Lightweight 4D Gaussian Splatting framework (LGS) that can liberate the efficiency bottlenecks of both rendering and storage for dynamic endoscopic reconstruction. Specifically, to minimize the redundancy of Gaussian quantities, we propose Deformation-Aware Pruning by gauging the impact of each Gaussian on deformation. Concurrently, to reduce the redundancy of Gaussian attributes, we simplify the representation of textures and lighting in non-crucial areas by pruning the dimensions of Gaussian attributes. We further resolve the feature field redundancy caused by the high resolution of 4D neural spatiotemporal encoder for modeling dynamic scenes via a 4D feature field condensation. Experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate efficacy of LGS in terms of a compression rate exceeding 9 times while maintaining the pleasing visual quality and real-time rendering efficiency. LGS confirms a substantial step towards its application in robotic surgical services.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3D-GS）技术及其动态场景建模变体4D-GS的出现，为动态手术场景的实时渲染带来了前景。然而，大量高斯单元建模动态场景的前提——高维高斯属性和高分辨率变形场——都导致了资源有限的外科设备中实时渲染的存储问题。为克服这些限制，我们引入了轻量级四维高斯喷涂框架（LGS），能够释放动态内镜重建中渲染和存储的效率瓶颈。具体来说，为了最小化高斯量的冗余，我们提出了通过衡量每个高斯对变形影响来实现变形感知剪枝的做法。同时，为了减少高斯属性的冗余，我们通过修剪高斯属性的维度，简化了非关键区域纹理和光照的表示。我们还进一步解决了四维神经时空编码器高分辨率对动态场景建模（通过四维特征场凝聚）所造成的特征场冗余。公开基准测试显示，LGS在保持令人愉悦的画质和实时渲染效率的同时，压缩率超过9倍。LGS确认其在机器人手术服务应用方面迈出了重要一步。  

### [6] Sampling 3D Gaussian Scenes in Seconds with Latent Diffusion Models  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-18  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Paul Henderson, Melonie de Almeida, Daniela Ivanova, Titas Anciukevičius  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13099v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present a latent diffusion model over 3D scenes, that can be trained using only 2D image data. To achieve this, we first design an autoencoder that maps multi-view images to 3D Gaussian splats, and simultaneously builds a compressed latent representation of these splats. Then, we train a multi-view diffusion model over the latent space to learn an efficient generative model. This pipeline does not require object masks nor depths, and is suitable for complex scenes with arbitrary camera positions. We conduct careful experiments on two large-scale datasets of complex real-world scenes -- MVImgNet and RealEstate10K. We show that our approach enables generating 3D scenes in as little as 0.2 seconds, either from scratch, from a single input view, or from sparse input views. It produces diverse and high-quality results while running an order of magnitude faster than non-latent diffusion models and earlier NeRF-based generative models  
- **📝翻译**: 我们展示了一个基于三维场景的潜在扩散模型，仅可通过二维图像数据进行训练。为此，我们首先设计了一个自动编码器，将多视图图像映射到3D高斯碎片，同时构建这些碎片的压缩潜在表示。然后，我们在潜空间训练多视角扩散模型，以学习高效的生成模型。该流水线不需要物体遮罩或深度，适用于具有任意摄像机位置的复杂场景。我们在两个大型复杂现实场景数据集上进行了细致实验——MVImgNet和RealEstate10K。我们展示了我们的方法能够在短短0.2秒内生成3D场景，无论是从零开始、从单一输入视图，还是从稀疏输入视图生成。它产生多样且高质量的结果，运行速度比非潜在扩散模型和早期基于NeRF的生成模型快一个数量级  

### [7] A Hierarchical 3D Gaussian Representation for Real-Time Rendering of Very Large Datasets  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Bernhard Kerbl, Andréas Meuleman, Georgios Kopanas et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project Page: https://repo-sam.inria.fr/fungraph/hierarchical-3d-gaussians/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12080v1)   · [Project](https://repo-sam.inria.fr/fungraph/hierarchical-3d-gaussians/)  
- **📝摘要**：Novel view synthesis has seen major advances in recent years, with 3D Gaussian splatting offering an excellent level of visual quality, fast training and real-time rendering. However, the resources needed for training and rendering inevitably limit the size of the captured scenes that can be represented with good visual quality. We introduce a hierarchy of 3D Gaussians that preserves visual quality for very large scenes, while offering an efficient Level-of-Detail (LOD) solution for efficient rendering of distant content with effective level selection and smooth transitions between levels.We introduce a divide-and-conquer approach that allows us to train very large scenes in independent chunks. We consolidate the chunks into a hierarchy that can be optimized to further improve visual quality of Gaussians merged into intermediate nodes. Very large captures typically have sparse coverage of the scene, presenting many challenges to the original 3D Gaussian splatting training method; we adapt and regularize training to account for these issues. We present a complete solution, that enables real-time rendering of very large scenes and can adapt to available resources thanks to our LOD method. We show results for captured scenes with up to tens of thousands of images with a simple and affordable rig, covering trajectories of up to several kilometers and lasting up to one hour. Project Page: https://repo-sam.inria.fr/fungraph/hierarchical-3d-gaussians/  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，新颖视角合成取得了重大进展，3D高斯喷涂提供了卓越的视觉质量、快速的训练和实时渲染。然而，训练和渲染所需的资源不可避免地限制了能够以良好视觉质量表现的捕捉场景大小。我们引入了3D高斯层级结构，既能在超大场景中保持视觉质量，又提供高效的细节层级（LOD）解决方案，以高效渲染远处内容，实现关卡选择和关卡间平滑过渡。我们引入了分而治之的方法，使我们能够将非常大的场景训练成独立的块。我们将这些块整合成一个层级结构，可以优化以进一步提升合并到中间节点的高斯图的视觉质量。非常大的捕获通常场景覆盖稀疏，这对原始3D高斯喷射训练方法提出了许多挑战;我们调整并规范培训以应对这些问题。我们提出了一个完整的解决方案，能够实时渲染超大型场景，并能根据现有资源进行调整，这得益于我们的LOD方法。我们展示了使用简单且经济的设备拍摄多达数万张图像的场景，覆盖数公里的轨迹，持续时间可达一小时。项目页面：https://repo-sam.inria.fr/fungraph/hierarchical-3d-gaussians/  

### [8] 3DGS.zip: A survey on 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression Methods  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-17（更新：2025-03-05）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Milena T. Bagdasarian, Paul Knoll, Yi-Hsin Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：3D Gaussian Splatting compression survey; 3DGS compression; updated discussion; new approaches added; new illustrations  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09510v5)   · [Project](https://w-m.github.io/3dgs-compression-survey/)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a cutting-edge technique for real-time radiance field rendering, offering state-of-the-art performance in terms of both quality and speed. 3DGS models a scene as a collection of three-dimensional Gaussians, with additional attributes optimized to conform to the scene's geometric and visual properties. Despite its advantages in rendering speed and image fidelity, 3DGS is limited by its significant storage and memory demands. These high demands make 3DGS impractical for mobile devices or headsets, reducing its applicability in important areas of computer graphics. To address these challenges and advance the practicality of 3DGS, this survey provides a comprehensive and detailed examination of compression and compaction techniques developed to make 3DGS more efficient. We classify existing methods into two categories: compression, which focuses on reducing file size, and compaction, which aims to minimize the number of Gaussians. Both methods aim to maintain or improve quality, each by minimizing its respective attribute: file size for compression and Gaussian count for compaction. We introduce the basic mathematical concepts underlying the analyzed methods, as well as key implementation details and design choices. Our report thoroughly discusses similarities and differences among the methods, as well as their respective advantages and disadvantages. We establish a consistent framework for comparing the surveyed methods based on key performance metrics and datasets. Specifically, since these methods have been developed in parallel and over a short period of time, currently, no comprehensive comparison exists. This survey, for the first time, presents a unified framework to evaluate 3DGS compression techniques. We maintain a website that will be regularly updated with emerging methods: https://w-m.github.io/3dgs-compression-survey/ .  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射技术（3DGS）已成为实时辐射场渲染的前沿技术，在质量和速度方面都具备最先进的性能。3DGS将场景建模为一组三维高斯分布，并附加属性以适应场景的几何和视觉属性。尽管在渲染速度和图像保真度上有优势，3DGS 仍受限于其较大的存储和内存需求。这些高需求使得3DGS在移动设备或耳机中变得不切实际，降低了其在计算机图形学重要领域的适用性。为应对这些挑战并提升3DGS的实用性，本综述对为提升3DGS效率而开发的压缩和压实技术进行了全面且详细的分析。我们将现有方法分为两类：压缩，侧重于减少文件大小，以及压缩，旨在最小化高斯数。这两种方法都旨在保持或提升质量，分别通过最小化其各自的属性：压缩时使用文件大小，压缩时使用高斯计数。我们介绍了所分析方法背后的基本数学概念，以及关键的实现细节和设计选择。我们的报告详细讨论了两种方法的异同，以及它们各自的优缺点。我们建立了基于关键绩效指标和数据集比较调查方法的统一框架。具体来说，由于这些方法是在并行且短时间内开发的，目前尚无全面的比较。本次调查首次提出了一个统一的框架来评估3DGS压缩技术。我们维护一个网站，将定期更新新兴方法：https://w-m.github.io/3dgs-compression-survey/。  

### [9] PUP 3D-GS: Principled Uncertainty Pruning for 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-14（更新：2025-03-24）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Alex Hanson, Allen Tu, Vasu Singla et al.  
- **📝说明**：CVPR 2025, Project Page: https://pup3dgs.github.io/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10219v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent advances in novel view synthesis have enabled real-time rendering speeds with high reconstruction accuracy. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS), a foundational point-based parametric 3D scene representation, models scenes as large sets of 3D Gaussians. However, complex scenes can consist of millions of Gaussians, resulting in high storage and memory requirements that limit the viability of 3D-GS on devices with limited resources. Current techniques for compressing these pretrained models by pruning Gaussians rely on combining heuristics to determine which Gaussians to remove. At high compression ratios, these pruned scenes suffer from heavy degradation of visual fidelity and loss of foreground details. In this paper, we propose a principled sensitivity pruning score that preserves visual fidelity and foreground details at significantly higher compression ratios than existing approaches. It is computed as a second-order approximation of the reconstruction error on the training views with respect to the spatial parameters of each Gaussian. Additionally, we propose a multi-round prune-refine pipeline that can be applied to any pretrained 3D-GS model without changing its training pipeline. After pruning 90% of Gaussians, a substantially higher percentage than previous methods, our PUP 3D-GS pipeline increases average rendering speed by 3.56$\times$ while retaining more salient foreground information and achieving higher image quality metrics than existing techniques on scenes from the Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks & Temples, and Deep Blending datasets.  
- **📝翻译**: 新颖视图合成技术的最新进展使得实时渲染速度和高重建精度成为可能。3D高斯喷溅法（3D-GS）是一种基础性的基于点的参数化3D场景表示法，将场景建模为大量3D高斯分布。然而，复杂场景可能包含数百万高斯，导致存储和内存需求高，限制了3D-GS在资源有限设备上的可行性。当前通过剪枝高斯算法压缩这些预训练模型的技术依赖于结合启发式方法来确定应去除哪些高斯算法。在高压缩比下，这些修剪过的场景会严重降低视觉真实度，前景细节也丢失。本文提出了一种原则性的灵敏度剪枝评分，能够在显著高于现有方法的压缩比下保持视觉真实性和前景细节。它是作为训练视图相对于每个高斯空间参数的重建误差的二阶近似计算的。此外，我们还提出了一种多轮修剪-精细流水线，可以应用于任何预训练的3D-GS模型，而无需更改其训练流水线。在修剪了90%的高斯分布后，这一比例远高于以往方法，我们的PUP 3D-GS流程将平均渲染速度提高了3.56美元/时间，同时保留了更多显著的前景信息，并在Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks & Temples和Deep Blending数据集的场景中实现了比现有技术更高的图像质量指标。  

### [10] GaussianForest: Hierarchical-Hybrid 3D Gaussian Splatting for Compressed Scene Modeling  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-13（更新：2024-08-08）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Fengyi Zhang, Yadan Luo, Tianjun Zhang, Lin Zhang, Zi Huang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08759v2)    
- **📝摘要**：The field of novel-view synthesis has recently witnessed the emergence of 3D Gaussian Splatting, which represents scenes in a point-based manner and renders through rasterization. This methodology, in contrast to Radiance Fields that rely on ray tracing, demonstrates superior rendering quality and speed. However, the explicit and unstructured nature of 3D Gaussians poses a significant storage challenge, impeding its broader application. To address this challenge, we introduce the Gaussian-Forest modeling framework, which hierarchically represents a scene as a forest of hybrid 3D Gaussians. Each hybrid Gaussian retains its unique explicit attributes while sharing implicit ones with its sibling Gaussians, thus optimizing parameterization with significantly fewer variables. Moreover, adaptive growth and pruning strategies are designed, ensuring detailed representation in complex regions and a notable reduction in the number of required Gaussians. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Gaussian-Forest not only maintains comparable speed and quality but also achieves a compression rate surpassing 10 times, marking a significant advancement in efficient scene modeling. Codes will be available at https://github.com/Xian-Bei/GaussianForest.  
- **📝翻译**: 新颖视角综合领域最近出现了三维高斯喷涂技术，它以基于点的方式表示场景，并通过光栅化渲染。与依赖光线追踪的辐射场相比，这种方法展现了更优越的渲染质量和速度。然而，3D高斯的显式且非结构化特性带来了重大存储挑战，阻碍了其更广泛的应用。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了高斯-森林建模框架，该框架以层级方式表示场景为一片混合三维高斯森林。每个混合高斯分布表表都保留了其独特的显式属性，同时与其兄弟高斯分布共享隐式属性，从而以显著减少的变量优化参数化。此外，设计了适应性生长和修剪策略，确保复杂区域的详细表示，显著减少所需高斯量。大量实验表明，高斯-森林不仅保持了相当的速度和质量，压缩率还超过10倍，标志着高效场景建模的重大进展。兑换码将于 https://github.com/Xian-Bei/GaussianForest 年提供。  

### [11] Generative Gaussian Splatting for Unbounded 3D City Generation  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-10（更新：2025-02-27）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haozhe Xie, Zhaoxi Chen, Fangzhou Hong, Ziwei Liu  
- **📝说明**：CVPR 2025. Project Page: https://haozhexie.com/project/gaussian-city  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06526v3)    
- **📝摘要**：3D city generation with NeRF-based methods shows promising generation results but is computationally inefficient. Recently 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) has emerged as a highly efficient alternative for object-level 3D generation. However, adapting 3D-GS from finite-scale 3D objects and humans to infinite-scale 3D cities is non-trivial. Unbounded 3D city generation entails significant storage overhead (out-of-memory issues), arising from the need to expand points to billions, often demanding hundreds of Gigabytes of VRAM for a city scene spanning 10km^2. In this paper, we propose GaussianCity, a generative Gaussian Splatting framework dedicated to efficiently synthesizing unbounded 3D cities with a single feed-forward pass. Our key insights are two-fold: 1) Compact 3D Scene Representation: We introduce BEV-Point as a highly compact intermediate representation, ensuring that the growth in VRAM usage for unbounded scenes remains constant, thus enabling unbounded city generation. 2) Spatial-aware Gaussian Attribute Decoder: We present spatial-aware BEV-Point decoder to produce 3D Gaussian attributes, which leverages Point Serializer to integrate the structural and contextual characteristics of BEV points. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GaussianCity achieves state-of-the-art results in both drone-view and street-view 3D city generation. Notably, compared to CityDreamer, GaussianCity exhibits superior performance with a speedup of 60 times (10.72 FPS v.s. 0.18 FPS).  
- **📝翻译**: 基于NeRF的方法进行三维城市生成显示出有前景的生成效果，但计算效率较低。近年来，3D高斯喷溅（3D-GS）作为对象级三维生成的高效替代方案出现。然而，将3D-GS从有限尺度的3D物体和人类应用到无限尺度的3D城市并非易事。无界3D城市生成带来了显著的存储开销（内存不足问题），这源于需要将点扩展到数十亿个点，通常需要数百GB的显存来覆盖10km^2的城市场景。本文提出了高斯城（GaussianCity），一种生成式高斯喷溅框架，致力于通过单次前馈传递高效合成无界三维城市。我们的核心见解有两个方面：1）紧凑的3D场景表示：我们将BEV-Point介绍为高度紧凑的中间表示，确保无界场景的显存使用增长保持稳定，从而实现无界城市生成。2）空间感知高斯属性解码器：我们提供空间感知BEV点解码器，生成3D高斯属性，利用点串行器整合BEV点的结构和上下文特性。大量实验表明，GaussianCity 在无人机视角和街景 3D 城市生成方面都取得了最先进的性能。值得注意的是，与《CityDreamer》相比，GaussianCity的性能更优，速度提升了60倍（10.72帧对0.18帧）。  

### [12] Improving Gaussian Splatting with Localized Points Management  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-06（更新：2025-04-19）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haosen Yang, Chenhao Zhang, Wenqing Wang et al.  
- **📝说明**：CVPR 2025 (Highlight). Github: https://happy-hsy.github.io/projects/LPM/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04251v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Point management is critical for optimizing 3D Gaussian Splatting models, as point initiation (e.g., via structure from motion) is often distributionally inappropriate. Typically, Adaptive Density Control (ADC) algorithm is adopted, leveraging view-averaged gradient magnitude thresholding for point densification, opacity thresholding for pruning, and regular all-points opacity reset. We reveal that this strategy is limited in tackling intricate/special image regions (e.g., transparent) due to inability of identifying all 3D zones requiring point densification, and lacking an appropriate mechanism to handle ill-conditioned points with negative impacts (e.g., occlusion due to false high opacity). To address these limitations, we propose a Localized Point Management (LPM) strategy, capable of identifying those error-contributing zones in greatest need for both point addition and geometry calibration. Zone identification is achieved by leveraging the underlying multiview geometry constraints, subject to image rendering errors. We apply point densification in the identified zones and then reset the opacity of the points in front of these regions, creating a new opportunity to correct poorly conditioned points. Serving as a versatile plugin, LPM can be seamlessly integrated into existing static 3D and dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting models with minimal additional cost. Experimental evaluations validate the efficacy of our LPM in boosting a variety of existing 3D/4D models both quantitatively and qualitatively. Notably, LPM improves both static 3DGS and dynamic SpaceTimeGS to achieve state-of-the-art rendering quality while retaining real-time speeds, excelling on challenging datasets such as Tanks & Temples and the Neural 3D Video dataset.  
- **📝翻译**: 点管理对于优化三维高斯散落模型至关重要，因为点起始（例如通过运动结构）通常分布上不合适。通常采用自适应密度控制（ADC）算法，利用视均梯度幅度阈值进行点密度化，不透明度阈值进行剪枝，以及常规的全点不透明度重置。我们发现，由于无法识别所有需要点密度化的三维区域，且缺乏处理条件不良点（如因虚高不透明度导致遮挡）的机制，该策略在处理复杂/特殊图像区域（如透明）方面存在限制。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种局部点管理（LPM）策略，能够识别那些最需要点加法和几何校准的误差来源区域。区域识别通过利用底层的多视图几何约束实现，但存在图像渲染错误。我们在已识别的区域进行点密度化，然后重置这些区域前方点的不透明度，创造了修正条件不佳点的新机会。作为多功能插件，LPM可以无缝集成到现有的静态3D和动态4D高斯喷涂模型中，额外成本极低。实验评估验证了我们LPM在定量和定性上提升多种现有3D/4D模型的有效性。值得注意的是，LPM改进了静态3DGS和动态时空GS，实现了最先进的渲染质量，同时保持实时速度，在Tanks & Temples和神经3D视频数据集等具有挑战性的数据集上表现出色。  

### [13] Dynamic 3D Gaussian Fields for Urban Areas  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-05（更新：2024-11-23）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Tobias Fischer, Jonas Kulhanek, Samuel Rota Bulò et al.  
- **📝说明**：NeurIPS'24 spotlight. Project page is available at https://tobiasfshr.github.io/pub/4dgf/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03175v2)    
- **📝摘要**：We present an efficient neural 3D scene representation for novel-view synthesis (NVS) in large-scale, dynamic urban areas. Existing works are not well suited for applications like mixed-reality or closed-loop simulation due to their limited visual quality and non-interactive rendering speeds. Recently, rasterization-based approaches have achieved high-quality NVS at impressive speeds. However, these methods are limited to small-scale, homogeneous data, i.e. they cannot handle severe appearance and geometry variations due to weather, season, and lighting and do not scale to larger, dynamic areas with thousands of images. We propose 4DGF, a neural scene representation that scales to large-scale dynamic urban areas, handles heterogeneous input data, and substantially improves rendering speeds. We use 3D Gaussians as an efficient geometry scaffold while relying on neural fields as a compact and flexible appearance model. We integrate scene dynamics via a scene graph at global scale while modeling articulated motions on a local level via deformations. This decomposed approach enables flexible scene composition suitable for real-world applications. In experiments, we surpass the state-of-the-art by over 3 dB in PSNR and more than 200 times in rendering speed.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了一种高效的神经三维场景表示，用于大规模动态城市区域的新视角综合（NVS）。现有作品由于视觉质量有限且渲染速度非交互，不适合混合现实或闭环模拟等应用。近年来，基于光栅化的方法以令人印象深刻的速度实现了高质量的NVS。然而，这些方法仅限于小尺度、均匀的数据，即无法处理因天气、季节和光照引起的严重外观和几何变化，也无法扩展到拥有数千张图像的更大动态区域。我们提出了4DGF，一种神经场景表示，能够扩展到大规模动态城市区域，处理异构输入数据，并大幅提升渲染速度。我们使用三维高斯算作为高效的几何支架，同时依赖神经场作为紧凑且灵活的外观模型。我们通过全局尺度的场景图整合场景动力学，同时通过变形在局部层面建模关节运动。这种分解方法使场景构图变得灵活，适用于现实应用。在实验中，我们在PSNR上超越了最先进的3 dB以上，渲染速度提升了200多倍。  


## May 2024

### [1] ContextGS: Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting with Anchor Level Context Model  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-31  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yufei Wang, Zhihao Li, Lanqing Guo et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20721v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a promising framework for novel view synthesis, offering fast rendering speeds and high fidelity. However, the large number of Gaussians and their associated attributes require effective compression techniques. Existing methods primarily compress neural Gaussians individually and independently, i.e., coding all the neural Gaussians at the same time, with little design for their interactions and spatial dependence. Inspired by the effectiveness of the context model in image compression, we propose the first autoregressive model at the anchor level for 3DGS compression in this work. We divide anchors into different levels and the anchors that are not coded yet can be predicted based on the already coded ones in all the coarser levels, leading to more accurate modeling and higher coding efficiency. To further improve the efficiency of entropy coding, e.g., to code the coarsest level with no already coded anchors, we propose to introduce a low-dimensional quantized feature as the hyperprior for each anchor, which can be effectively compressed. Our work pioneers the context model in the anchor level for 3DGS representation, yielding an impressive size reduction of over 100 times compared to vanilla 3DGS and 15 times compared to the most recent state-of-the-art work Scaffold-GS, while achieving comparable or even higher rendering quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）已成为新颖视图合成的有前景框架，提供快速渲染速度和高保真度。然而，大量高斯及其相关属性需要有效的压缩技术。现有方法主要单独且独立地压缩神经高斯，即同时编码所有神经高斯，几乎没有设计它们的相互作用和空间依赖性。受上下文模型在图像压缩中的有效性启发，我们提出了3DGS压缩锚点层面的首个自回归模型。我们将锚点划分为不同层级，尚未编码的锚点可以根据所有粗层中已编码的锚点进行预测，从而实现更准确的建模和更高的编码效率。为了进一步提高熵编码的效率，例如编码最粗的层级且没有已编码锚点，我们提出引入一个低维量子化特征作为每个锚点的超先验，该特征可以有效压缩。我们的工作开创了3DGS表示的锚点层上下文模型，与原版3DGS相比，尺寸减少了100多倍，与最新最先进的Scaffold-GS相比减少了15倍，同时实现了相当甚至更高的渲染质量。  

### [2] GaussianRoom: Improving 3D Gaussian Splatting with SDF Guidance and Monocular Cues for Indoor Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-30（更新：2025-04-01）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haodong Xiang, Xinghui Li, Kai Cheng et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19671v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Embodied intelligence requires precise reconstruction and rendering to simulate large-scale real-world data. Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently demonstrated high-quality results with real-time performance, it still faces challenges in indoor scenes with large, textureless regions, resulting in incomplete and noisy reconstructions due to poor point cloud initialization and underconstrained optimization. Inspired by the continuity of signed distance field (SDF), which naturally has advantages in modeling surfaces, we propose a unified optimization framework that integrates neural signed distance fields (SDFs) with 3DGS for accurate geometry reconstruction and real-time rendering. This framework incorporates a neural SDF field to guide the densification and pruning of Gaussians, enabling Gaussians to model scenes accurately even with poor initialized point clouds. Simultaneously, the geometry represented by Gaussians improves the efficiency of the SDF field by piloting its point sampling. Additionally, we introduce two regularization terms based on normal and edge priors to resolve geometric ambiguities in textureless areas and enhance detail accuracy. Extensive experiments in ScanNet and ScanNet++ show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both surface reconstruction and novel view synthesis.  
- **📝翻译**: 具身智能需要精确的重建和渲染，以模拟大规模的真实世界数据。尽管3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）最近展示了高质量的实时性能，但在室内场景中具有大面积且无纹理区域时仍面临挑战，导致由于点云初始化不佳和优化受限不足，重建过程不完整且噪声较大。受带符号距离场（SDF）连续性（SDF）在曲面建模中自然具有优势的启发，我们提出了一个统一的优化框架，将神经带符号距离场（SDF）与3DGS集成，实现精确的几何重建和实时渲染。该框架包含神经SDF场，指导高斯分布的密度化和修剪，使高斯分布即使在初始化点云较差的情况下也能准确建模场景。同时，高斯分布表示的几何通过试点采样提高了SDF场的效率。此外，我们引入了基于法线和边缘先验的两个正则化项，以解决无纹理区域的几何模糊性并提升细节精度。ScanNet和ScanNet++中的大量实验表明，我们的方法在曲面重建和新颖视图综合方面均达到了最先进的性能。  

### [3] LP-3DGS: Learning to Prune 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-29  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhaoliang Zhang, Tianchen Song, Yongjae Lee et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18784v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become one of the mainstream methodologies for novel view synthesis (NVS) due to its high quality and fast rendering speed. However, as a point-based scene representation, 3DGS potentially generates a large number of Gaussians to fit the scene, leading to high memory usage. Improvements that have been proposed require either an empirical and preset pruning ratio or importance score threshold to prune the point cloud. Such hyperparamter requires multiple rounds of training to optimize and achieve the maximum pruning ratio, while maintaining the rendering quality for each scene. In this work, we propose learning-to-prune 3DGS (LP-3DGS), where a trainable binary mask is applied to the importance score that can find optimal pruning ratio automatically. Instead of using the traditional straight-through estimator (STE) method to approximate the binary mask gradient, we redesign the masking function to leverage the Gumbel-Sigmoid method, making it differentiable and compatible with the existing training process of 3DGS. Extensive experiments have shown that LP-3DGS consistently produces a good balance that is both efficient and high quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，3D高斯拼拍（3DGS）因其高质量和快速渲染速度，已成为新颖视图合成（NVS）的主流方法之一。然而，作为基于点的场景表示，3DGS可能会生成大量高斯以适应场景，导致内存使用较高。已提出的改进方案需要一个经验且预设的剪枝比率或重要性评分阈值来修剪点云。这种超参数需要多次训练，以优化并实现最大剪枝比，同时保持每个场景的渲染质量。在本研究中，我们提出了学习修剪3DGS（LP-3DGS）方法，即对重要性评分应用可训练的二元遮罩，从而自动找到最优修剪比率。我们不再使用传统的直通估计器（STE）方法来近似二元遮罩梯度，而是重新设计了遮罩函数，利用Gumbel-Sigmoid方法，使其可微且兼容3DGS现有的训练过程。大量实验表明，LP-3DGS始终能够实现高效且高质量的良好平衡。  

### [4] A Refined 3D Gaussian Representation for High-Quality Dynamic Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-28  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Bin Zhang, Bi Zeng, Zexin Peng  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17891v1)    
- **📝摘要**：In recent years, Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) has revolutionized three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction with its implicit representation. Building upon NeRF, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) has departed from the implicit representation of neural networks and instead directly represents scenes as point clouds with Gaussian-shaped distributions. While this shift has notably elevated the rendering quality and speed of radiance fields but inevitably led to a significant increase in memory usage. Additionally, effectively rendering dynamic scenes in 3D-GS has emerged as a pressing challenge. To address these concerns, this paper purposes a refined 3D Gaussian representation for high-quality dynamic scene reconstruction. Firstly, we use a deformable multi-layer perceptron (MLP) network to capture the dynamic offset of Gaussian points and express the color features of points through hash encoding and a tiny MLP to reduce storage requirements. Subsequently, we introduce a learnable denoising mask coupled with denoising loss to eliminate noise points from the scene, thereby further compressing 3D Gaussian model. Finally, motion noise of points is mitigated through static constraints and motion consistency constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that our method surpasses existing approaches in rendering quality and speed, while significantly reducing the memory usage associated with 3D-GS, making it highly suitable for various tasks such as novel view synthesis, and dynamic mapping.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，神经辐射场（NeRF）以其隐含的表示方式彻底革新了三维（3D）重建。基于NeRF，3D高斯喷溅（3D-GS）已摒弃了神经网络的隐式表示，直接将场景表示为带有高斯分布的点云。虽然这一变化显著提升了辐射场的渲染质量和速度，但不可避免地导致内存使用显著增加。此外，在3D-GS中有效渲染动态场景也成为了一个紧迫的挑战。为解决这些问题，本文采用了精细的三维高斯表示法，以实现高质量的动态场景重建。首先，我们使用可变形多层感知器（MLP）网络捕捉高斯点的动态偏移，并通过哈希编码和极小的MLP表达点的颜色特征，以减少存储需求。随后，我们引入可学习的去噪掩模与去噪损耗结合，以消除场景中的噪点，从而进一步压缩三维高斯模型。最后，点的运动噪声通过静态约束和运动一致性约束得到缓解。实验结果表明，我们的方法在渲染质量和速度方面超越了现有方法，同时显著降低了3D-GS相关的内存使用，使其非常适合新颖视图综合和动态映射等多种任务。  

### [5] SafeguardGS: 3D Gaussian Primitive Pruning While Avoiding Catastrophic Scene Destruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-28（更新：2024-11-22）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yongjae Lee, Zhaoliang Zhang, Deliang Fan  
- **📝说明**：19 pages, 20 figures, 7 tables  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17793v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has made significant strides in novel view synthesis. However, its suboptimal densification process results in the excessively large number of Gaussian primitives, which impacts frame-per-second and increases memory usage, making it unsuitable for low-end devices. To address this issue, many follow-up studies have proposed various pruning techniques with score functions designed to identify and remove less important primitives. Nonetheless, a comprehensive discussion of their effectiveness and implications across all techniques is missing. In this paper, we are the first to categorize 3DGS pruning techniques into two types: Scene-level pruning and Pixel-level pruning, distinguished by their scope for ranking primitives. Our subsequent experiments reveal that, while scene-level pruning leads to disastrous quality drops under extreme decimation of Gaussian primitives, pixel-level pruning not only sustains relatively high rendering quality with minuscule performance degradation but also provides an inherent boundary of pruning, i.e., a safeguard of Gaussian pruning. Building on this observation, we further propose multiple variations of score functions based on the factors of rendering equations and discover that assessing based on color similarity with blending weight is the most effective method for discriminating insignificant primitives. In our experiments, our SafeguardGS with the optimal score function shows the highest PSNR-per-primitive performance under an extreme pruning setting, retaining only about 10% of the primitives from the original 3DGS scene (i.e., 10x compression ratio). We believe our research provides valuable insights for optimizing 3DGS for future works.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）在小说视图合成方面取得了显著进展。然而，其密度化过程不理想，导致高斯原语数量过多，影响帧率并增加内存使用，使其不适合低端设备。为解决这一问题，许多后续研究提出了多种剪枝技术，并结合评分函数，旨在识别和去除次要的原语。然而，缺乏对其在所有技术中有效性和影响的全面讨论。本文首次将3DGS剪枝技术分为两类：场景级剪枝和像素级剪枝，区别于其对原语排序的范围。我们后续的实验显示，场景层级剪枝在高斯原语极端毁灭下会导致严重的质量下降，而像素级剪枝不仅能以极小的性能下降维持相对较高的渲染质量，还为剪枝提供了固有的边界，即高斯剪枝的保障。基于这一观察，我们进一步提出了基于渲染方程因子的多种评分函数变体，并发现基于颜色相似度与混合权重进行评估是区分无显著基元最有效的方法。在我们的实验中，带有最优评分函数的SafeguardGS在极端修剪设置下显示出最高的PSNR每个基元性能，仅保留了原始3DGS场景约10%的原件（即10倍压缩比）。我们相信我们的研究为优化3DGS未来工作提供了宝贵见解。  

### [6] GOI: Find 3D Gaussians of Interest with an Optimizable Open-vocabulary Semantic-space Hyperplane  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-27（更新：2024-07-27）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yansong Qu, Shaohui Dai, Xinyang Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：Our project page is available at https://quyans.github.io/GOI-Hyperplane/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17596v2)   · [Project](https://quyans.github.io/GOI-Hyperplane/)  
- **📝摘要**：3D open-vocabulary scene understanding, crucial for advancing augmented reality and robotic applications, involves interpreting and locating specific regions within a 3D space as directed by natural language instructions. To this end, we introduce GOI, a framework that integrates semantic features from 2D vision-language foundation models into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and identifies 3D Gaussians of Interest using an Optimizable Semantic-space Hyperplane. Our approach includes an efficient compression method that utilizes scene priors to condense noisy high-dimensional semantic features into compact low-dimensional vectors, which are subsequently embedded in 3DGS. During the open-vocabulary querying process, we adopt a distinct approach compared to existing methods, which depend on a manually set fixed empirical threshold to select regions based on their semantic feature distance to the query text embedding. This traditional approach often lacks universal accuracy, leading to challenges in precisely identifying specific target areas. Instead, our method treats the feature selection process as a hyperplane division within the feature space, retaining only those features that are highly relevant to the query. We leverage off-the-shelf 2D Referring Expression Segmentation (RES) models to fine-tune the semantic-space hyperplane, enabling a more precise distinction between target regions and others. This fine-tuning substantially improves the accuracy of open-vocabulary queries, ensuring the precise localization of pertinent 3D Gaussians. Extensive experiments demonstrate GOI's superiority over previous state-of-the-art methods. Our project page is available at https://quyans.github.io/GOI-Hyperplane/ .  
- **📝翻译**: 三维开放词汇场景理解对于推进增强现实和机器人应用至关重要，涉及根据自然语言指令解释和定位三维空间中的特定区域。为此，我们引入了GOI，这是一个将二维视觉语言基础模型的语义特征整合到三维高斯喷射（3DGS）中的框架，并通过可优化语义空间超平面识别感兴趣的三维高斯图。我们的方法包括一种高效的压缩方法，利用场景先验将噪声高维语义特征浓缩为紧凑的低维向量，这些向量随后嵌入到3DGS中。在开放词汇查询过程中，我们采用了与现有方法不同的方法，现有方法依赖手动设定固定的经验阈值，根据其语义特征距离与查询文本嵌入选择区域。这种传统方法常缺乏普遍准确性，导致精准识别特定目标区域存在挑战。相反，我们的方法将特征选择过程视为特征空间中的超平面划分，只保留与查询高度相关的特征。我们利用现成的二维引用表达切割（RES）模型微调语义空间超平面，从而实现目标区域与其他区域的更精确区分。这种微调大大提高了开放词汇查询的准确性，确保相关三维高斯的精确定位。大量实验证明了印度政府优于以往最先进方法的优势。我们的项目页面可在 https://quyans.github.io/GOI-Hyperplane/ 访问。  

### [7] F-3DGS: Factorized Coordinates and Representations for 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-27（更新：2024-05-28）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xiangyu Sun, Joo Chan Lee, Daniel Rho et al.  
- **📝说明**：Our project page including code is available at https://xiangyu1sun.github.io/Factorize-3DGS/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17083v2)    
- **📝摘要**：The neural radiance field (NeRF) has made significant strides in representing 3D scenes and synthesizing novel views. Despite its advancements, the high computational costs of NeRF have posed challenges for its deployment in resource-constrained environments and real-time applications. As an alternative to NeRF-like neural rendering methods, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers rapid rendering speeds while maintaining excellent image quality. However, as it represents objects and scenes using a myriad of Gaussians, it requires substantial storage to achieve high-quality representation. To mitigate the storage overhead, we propose Factorized 3D Gaussian Splatting (F-3DGS), a novel approach that drastically reduces storage requirements while preserving image quality. Inspired by classical matrix and tensor factorization techniques, our method represents and approximates dense clusters of Gaussians with significantly fewer Gaussians through efficient factorization. We aim to efficiently represent dense 3D Gaussians by approximating them with a limited amount of information for each axis and their combinations. This method allows us to encode a substantially large number of Gaussians along with their essential attributes -- such as color, scale, and rotation -- necessary for rendering using a relatively small number of elements. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that F-3DGS achieves a significant reduction in storage costs while maintaining comparable quality in rendered images.  
- **📝翻译**: 神经辐射场（NeRF）在三维场景的表现和新颖视角的综合方面取得了显著进展。尽管有进步，NeRF的高计算成本在资源受限环境和实时应用中仍面临挑战。作为类似NeRF神经渲染方法的替代方案，3D高斯喷射（3DGS）提供了快速渲染速度，同时保持优异的图像质量。然而，由于它使用大量高斯量表示对象和场景，因此需要大量存储才能实现高质量的表示。为减轻存储开销，我们提出了分解三维高斯喷涂（F-3DGS）新颖方法，大幅降低存储需求同时保持图像质量。受经典矩阵和张量分解技术的启发，我们的方法通过高效分解表示并近似高斯密集簇，高斯分布显著减少。我们旨在通过对每个轴及其组合提供有限信息，高效地表示稠密的三维高斯分布。这种方法使我们能够编码大量高斯分布及其基本属性——如颜色、比例和旋转——这些属性对于使用相对较少的元素进行渲染至关重要。大量实验结果表明，F-3DGS在保持渲染图像质量相当的同时，显著降低了存储成本。  

### [8] PyGS: Large-scale Scene Representation with Pyramidal 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-27（更新：2024-05-29）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zipeng Wang, Dan Xu  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16829v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in synthesizing photorealistic images of large-scale scenes. However, they are often plagued by a loss of fine details and long rendering durations. 3D Gaussian Splatting has recently been introduced as a potent alternative, achieving both high-fidelity visual results and accelerated rendering performance. Nonetheless, scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting is fraught with challenges. Specifically, large-scale scenes grapples with the integration of objects across multiple scales and disparate viewpoints, which often leads to compromised efficacy as the Gaussians need to balance between detail levels. Furthermore, the generation of initialization points via COLMAP from large-scale dataset is both computationally demanding and prone to incomplete reconstructions. To address these challenges, we present Pyramidal 3D Gaussian Splatting (PyGS) with NeRF Initialization. Our approach represent the scene with a hierarchical assembly of Gaussians arranged in a pyramidal fashion. The top level of the pyramid is composed of a few large Gaussians, while each subsequent layer accommodates a denser collection of smaller Gaussians. We effectively initialize these pyramidal Gaussians through sampling a rapidly trained grid-based NeRF at various frequencies. We group these pyramidal Gaussians into clusters and use a compact weighting network to dynamically determine the influence of each pyramid level of each cluster considering camera viewpoint during rendering. Our method achieves a significant performance leap across multiple large-scale datasets and attains a rendering time that is over 400 times faster than current state-of-the-art approaches.  
- **📝翻译**: 神经辐射场（NeRFs）在合成大比例场景的写实图像方面表现出了卓越的熟练度。然而，它们常常因细节丢失和渲染时间过长而困扰。3D高斯喷射技术最近被引入，作为一种强有力的替代方案，既实现了高保真视觉效果，又提升了渲染性能。尽管如此，3D高斯喷溅的缩放依然充满挑战。具体来说，大尺度场景需要在多个尺度和不同视角中整合物体，这常常导致效果受损，因为高斯分布器需要在细节层面之间取得平衡。此外，通过COLMAP从大规模数据集生成初始化点既计算量大，且重建不完整。为应对这些挑战，我们介绍了带有NeRF初始化的金字塔三维高斯喷溅（PyGS）。我们的方法通过高斯分布层叠加的结构，呈金字塔形排列。金字塔的顶层由几个大型高斯分布组成，而每一层则容纳更密集的较小高斯分布。我们通过在不同频率下快速训练的基于网格的NeRF采样，有效地初始化这些金字塔型高斯分布。我们将这些金字塔型高斯分布子分组为簇，并使用紧凑的加权网络动态确定每个簇金字塔层级在渲染过程中从摄像机视角计算的影响。我们的方法在多个大规模数据集中实现了显著的性能飞跃，渲染时间比当前最先进方法快了400多倍。  

### [9] Splat-SLAM: Globally Optimized RGB-only SLAM with 3D Gaussians  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-26  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Erik Sandström, Keisuke Tateno, Michael Oechsle et al.  
- **📝说明**：21 pages  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16544v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a powerful representation of geometry and appearance for RGB-only dense Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), as it provides a compact dense map representation while enabling efficient and high-quality map rendering. However, existing methods show significantly worse reconstruction quality than competing methods using other 3D representations, e.g. neural points clouds, since they either do not employ global map and pose optimization or make use of monocular depth. In response, we propose the first RGB-only SLAM system with a dense 3D Gaussian map representation that utilizes all benefits of globally optimized tracking by adapting dynamically to keyframe pose and depth updates by actively deforming the 3D Gaussian map. Moreover, we find that refining the depth updates in inaccurate areas with a monocular depth estimator further improves the accuracy of the 3D reconstruction. Our experiments on the Replica, TUM-RGBD, and ScanNet datasets indicate the effectiveness of globally optimized 3D Gaussians, as the approach achieves superior or on par performance with existing RGB-only SLAM methods methods in tracking, mapping and rendering accuracy while yielding small map sizes and fast runtimes. The source code is available at https://github.com/eriksandstroem/Splat-SLAM.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷涂已成为仅限RGB密集的同时定位与映射（SLAM）中几何和外观的强大表示方式，因为它提供了紧凑且密集的地图表示，同时实现高效且高质量的地图渲染。然而，现有方法的重建质量明显逊于使用其他三维表示的方法，例如神经点云，因为它们要么不采用全局映射和姿态优化，要么使用单眼深度。为此，我们提出了首个仅支持RGB的SLAM系统，采用密集的3D高斯映射表示，充分利用全局优化跟踪的所有优势，通过动态适应关键帧姿态和深度更新，主动变形3D高斯映射。此外，我们发现用单眼深度估计器细化不准确区域的深度更新，进一步提升了三维重建的准确性。我们在Replica、TUM-RGBD和ScanNet数据集上的实验显示，全球优化的3D高斯方法具有显著效果，该方法在跟踪、映射和渲染精度方面表现优于或与现有仅限RGB的SLAM方法相当，同时实现了小地图尺寸和快速运行时间。源代码可在 https://github.com/eriksandstroem/Splat-SLAM 获取。  

### [10] Don't Splat your Gaussians: Volumetric Ray-Traced Primitives for Modeling and Rendering Scattering and Emissive Media  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-24（更新：2025-09-08）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jorge Condor, Sebastien Speierer, Lukas Bode et al.  
- **📝说明**：17 pages, 17 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15425v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Efficient scene representations are essential for many computer graphics applications. A general unified representation that can handle both surfaces and volumes simultaneously, remains a research challenge. Inspired by recent methods for scene reconstruction that leverage mixtures of 3D Gaussians to model radiance fields, we formalize and generalize the modeling of scattering and emissive media using mixtures of simple kernel-based volumetric primitives. We introduce closed-form solutions for transmittance and free-flight distance sampling for different kernels, and propose several optimizations to use our method efficiently within any off-the-shelf volumetric path tracer. We demonstrate our method as a compact and efficient alternative to other forms of volume modeling for forward and inverse rendering of scattering media. Furthermore, we adapt and showcase our method in radiance field optimization and rendering, providing additional flexibility compared to current state of the art given its ray-tracing formulation. We also introduce the Epanechnikov kernel and demonstrate its potential as an efficient alternative to the traditionally-used Gaussian kernel in scene reconstruction tasks. The versatility and physically-based nature of our approach allows us to go beyond radiance fields and bring to kernel-based modeling and rendering any path-tracing enabled functionality such as scattering, relighting and complex camera models.  
- **📝翻译**: 高效的场景表示对于许多计算机图形学应用至关重要。能够同时处理曲面和体积的通用统一表示仍是一个研究挑战。受近期场景重建方法启发，利用三维高斯分布混合来建模辐射场，我们形式化并推广了利用简单基于核的体积原件混合的散射和发射介质建模。我们为不同核引入了透射和自由飞行距离采样的闭式解，并提出了若干优化方案，以高效地在任何现成体积路径追踪器中应用。我们展示了该方法作为前向和反向散射介质渲染的体积建模替代方案，是一种紧凑高效的替代方案。此外，我们改进并展示了我们的辐射场优化与渲染方法，相比当前的射线追踪体系，提供了额外的灵活性。我们还介绍了Epanechnikov核，并展示了其作为传统高斯核在场景重建任务中高效替代方案的潜力。我们方法的多样性和基于物理的特性使我们能够超越辐射场，将基于核的建模和渲染任何支持路径追踪的功能，如散射、重新照明和复杂的摄像机模型。  

### [11] GS-ROR$^2$: Bidirectional-guided 3DGS and SDF for Reflective Object Relighting and Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-22（更新：2025-04-28）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zuo-Liang Zhu, Beibei Wang, Jian Yang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18544v3)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown a powerful capability for novel view synthesis due to its detailed expressive ability and highly efficient rendering speed. Unfortunately, creating relightable 3D assets and reconstructing faithful geometry with 3DGS is still problematic, particularly for reflective objects, as its discontinuous representation raises difficulties in constraining geometries. Volumetric signed distance field (SDF) methods provide robust geometry reconstruction, while the expensive ray marching hinders its real-time application and slows the training. Besides, these methods struggle to capture sharp geometric details. To this end, we propose to guide 3DGS and SDF bidirectionally in a complementary manner, including an SDF-aided Gaussian splatting for efficient optimization of the relighting model and a GS-guided SDF enhancement for high-quality geometry reconstruction. At the core of our SDF-aided Gaussian splatting is the mutual supervision of the depth and normal between blended Gaussians and SDF, which avoids the expensive volume rendering of SDF. Thanks to this mutual supervision, the learned blended Gaussians are well-constrained with a minimal time cost. As the Gaussians are rendered in a deferred shading mode, the alpha-blended Gaussians are smooth, while individual Gaussians may still be outliers, yielding floater artifacts. Therefore, we introduce an SDF-aware pruning strategy to remove Gaussian outliers located distant from the surface defined by SDF, avoiding floater issue. This way, our GS framework provides reasonable normal and achieves realistic relighting, while the mesh from depth is still problematic. Therefore, we design a GS-guided SDF refinement, which utilizes the blended normal from Gaussians to finetune SDF. With this enhancement, our method can further provide high-quality meshes for reflective objects at the cost of 17% extra training time.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）因其细致的表现力和高效的渲染速度，展现出强大的新颖视图合成能力。遗憾的是，创建可重照的3D素材和用3DGS重建忠实几何体仍然存在问题，尤其是反光物体，因为其不连续的表现方式增加了约束几何体的困难。体积有符号距离场（SDF）方法提供了稳健的几何重建，而昂贵的光线行进阻碍了其实时应用并减缓训练速度。此外，这些方法难以捕捉清晰的几何细节。为此，我们提议以互补方式双向引导3DGS和SDF，包括SDF辅助的高斯喷溅以高效优化重照模型，以及GS引导SDF增强以实现高质量几何重建。我们SDF辅助高斯喷涂的核心是对混合高斯与SDF之间深度和法线的相互监督，避免了SDF昂贵的体积渲染。得益于这种相互监督，学习过的混合高斯分布器在时间成本下受到良好约束。由于高斯分布以延迟着色模式渲染，α混合的高斯分布是光滑的，而单个高斯分布仍可能为异常值，导致浮动伪影。因此，我们引入了一种基于SDF的剪枝策略，去除距离SDF定义的表面较远的高斯离群值，避免浮游现象。这样，我们的GS框架能提供合理的法线并实现真实的重新光照，而深度网格仍然存在问题。因此，我们设计了一个GS引导的SDF细化，利用高斯分布的混合法线对SDF进行微调。通过这一改进，我们的方法还能以额外17%的训练时间代价，为反射物体提供高质量网格。  

### [12] CoR-GS: Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting via Co-Regularization  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-20（更新：2024-07-11）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiawei Zhang, Jiahe Li, Xiaohan Yu et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted at ECCV 2024. Project page: https://jiaw-z.github.io/CoR-GS/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12110v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) creates a radiance field consisting of 3D Gaussians to represent a scene. With sparse training views, 3DGS easily suffers from overfitting, negatively impacting rendering. This paper introduces a new co-regularization perspective for improving sparse-view 3DGS. When training two 3D Gaussian radiance fields, we observe that the two radiance fields exhibit point disagreement and rendering disagreement that can unsupervisedly predict reconstruction quality, stemming from the randomness of densification implementation. We further quantify the two disagreements and demonstrate the negative correlation between them and accurate reconstruction, which allows us to identify inaccurate reconstruction without accessing ground-truth information. Based on the study, we propose CoR-GS, which identifies and suppresses inaccurate reconstruction based on the two disagreements: (1) Co-pruning considers Gaussians that exhibit high point disagreement in inaccurate positions and prunes them. (2) Pseudo-view co-regularization considers pixels that exhibit high rendering disagreement are inaccurate and suppress the disagreement. Results on LLFF, Mip-NeRF360, DTU, and Blender demonstrate that CoR-GS effectively regularizes the scene geometry, reconstructs the compact representations, and achieves state-of-the-art novel view synthesis quality under sparse training views.
