- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）生成由三维高斯分布组成的辐射场以表示场景。由于训练视野稀疏，3DGS容易出现过拟合问题，从而对渲染产生负面影响。本文引入了一种新的协正则化视角，用于改进稀疏视图3DGS。在训练两个三维高斯辐射场时，我们观察到两个辐射场存在点不一致和生成不一致，这些现象可以无监督地预测重建质量，这源于密度化实现的随机性。我们进一步量化了这两种分歧，并展示了它们与准确重建之间的负相关性，从而在不获取真实信息的情况下识别不准确的重建。基于研究，我们提出了CoR-GS方法，基于两种分歧识别并抑制不准确的重建：（1）共剪处理考虑在不准确位置中表现出高点不一致的高斯分布，并对其进行修剪。（2）伪视角共正则化认为表现出高度渲染不一致的像素不准确，并抑制这种不一致。LLFF、Mip-NeRF360、DTU和Blender的结果表明，CoR-GS能够有效正则化场景几何，重建紧凑的表示，并在稀疏训练视图下实现最先进的新颖视图综合质量。  

### [13] MotionGS : Compact Gaussian Splatting SLAM by Motion Filter  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-18（更新：2024-05-31）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xinli Guo, Weidong Zhang, Ruonan Liu, Peng Han, Hongtian Chen  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11129v2)    
- **📝摘要**：With their high-fidelity scene representation capability, the attention of SLAM field is deeply attracted by the Neural Radiation Field (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Recently, there has been a surge in NeRF-based SLAM, while 3DGS-based SLAM is sparse. A novel 3DGS-based SLAM approach with a fusion of deep visual feature, dual keyframe selection and 3DGS is presented in this paper. Compared with the existing methods, the proposed tracking is achieved by feature extraction and motion filter on each frame. The joint optimization of poses and 3D Gaussians runs through the entire mapping process. Additionally, the coarse-to-fine pose estimation and compact Gaussian scene representation are implemented by dual keyframe selection and novel loss functions. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm not only outperforms the existing methods in tracking and mapping, but also has less memory usage.  
- **📝翻译**: 凭借其高保真场景表现能力，SLAM场被神经辐射场（NeRF）和三维高斯喷射（3DGS）深深吸引。最近，基于NeRF的SLAM激增，而基于3DGS的SLAM则较为稀疏。本文提出了一种基于3DGS的新型SLAM方法，融合了深度视觉特征、双关键帧选择和3DGS。与现有方法相比，所提跟踪是通过特征提取和每帧的运动滤波实现的。姿态和三维高斯的联合优化贯穿整个映射过程。此外，粗到细的姿态估计和紧凑的高斯场景表示通过对偶关键帧选择和新颖的损失函数实现。实验结果表明，所提出的算法不仅在跟踪和映射方面优于现有方法，而且内存占用更少。  

### [14] From NeRFs to Gaussian Splats, and Back  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-15（更新：2024-08-13）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Siming He, Zach Osman, Pratik Chaudhari  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.09717v3)    
- **📝摘要**：For robotics applications where there is a limited number of (typically ego-centric) views, parametric representations such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) generalize better than non-parametric ones such as Gaussian splatting (GS) to views that are very different from those in the training data; GS however can render much faster than NeRFs. We develop a procedure to convert back and forth between the two. Our approach achieves the best of both NeRFs (superior PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS on dissimilar views, and a compact representation) and GS (real-time rendering and ability for easily modifying the representation); the computational cost of these conversions is minor compared to training the two from scratch.  
- **📝翻译**: 对于机器人应用中，视角数量有限（通常是以自我为中心的）方式，参数化表示如神经辐射场（NeRFs）比非参数表示（如高斯溅射（GS））更能推广到与训练数据截然不同的视图;不过GS渲染速度远快于NeRF。我们开发了一套在两者之间转换的程序。我们的方法既实现了NeRFs（在不同视图上更优越的PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS，且表示紧凑）和GS（实时渲染及易于修改表示的能力）的最佳优势;这些转换的计算成本相比从零开始训练两者要小得多。  

### [15] I3DGS: Improve 3D Gaussian Splatting from Multiple Dimensions  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-10  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jinwei Lin  
- **📝说明**：16 pages  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.06408v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting is a novel method for 3D view synthesis, which can gain an implicit neural learning rendering result than the traditional neural rendering technology but keep the more high-definition fast rendering speed. But it is still difficult to achieve a fast enough efficiency on 3D Gaussian Splatting for the practical applications. To Address this issue, we propose the I3DS, a synthetic model performance improvement evaluation solution and experiments test. From multiple and important levels or dimensions of the original 3D Gaussian Splatting, we made more than two thousand various kinds of experiments to test how the selected different items and components can make an impact on the training efficiency of the 3D Gaussian Splatting model. In this paper, we will share abundant and meaningful experiences and methods about how to improve the training, performance and the impacts caused by different items of the model. A special but normal Integer compression in base 95 and a floating-point compression in base 94 with ASCII encoding and decoding mechanism is presented. Many real and effective experiments and test results or phenomena will be recorded. After a series of reasonable fine-tuning, I3DS can gain excellent performance improvements than the previous one. The project code is available as open source.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅是一种新颖的3D视图合成方法，它可以获得比传统神经渲染技术隐式的神经学习渲染结果，同时保持更高分辨率的快速渲染速度。但在实际应用中，要实现三维高斯喷溅的足够快速效率仍然困难。为解决这一问题，我们提出了I3DS，一种合成模型性能改进评估解决方案和实验测试。从原始3D高斯喷溅的多个重要层次或维度出发，我们进行了两千多个不同类型的实验，以测试所选不同项目和组件如何影响3D高斯喷溅模型的训练效率。本文将分享丰富且有意义的经验和方法，探讨如何改进培训、绩效及模型中不同项目带来的影响。采用了一种特殊但正常的95进制整数压缩和94进制浮点压缩，采用ASCII编码和解码机制。许多真实有效的实验、测试结果或现象将被记录下来。经过一系列合理的微调，I3DS可以获得比前作更显著的性能提升。该项目代码以开源形式提供。  

### [16] GDGS: Gradient Domain Gaussian Splatting for Sparse Representation of Radiance Fields  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-08  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuanhao Gong  
- **📝说明**：arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2404.09105  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05446v1)    
- **📝摘要**：The 3D Gaussian splatting methods are getting popular. However, they work directly on the signal, leading to a dense representation of the signal. Even with some techniques such as pruning or distillation, the results are still dense. In this paper, we propose to model the gradient of the original signal. The gradients are much sparser than the original signal. Therefore, the gradients use much less Gaussian splats, leading to the more efficient storage and thus higher computational performance during both training and rendering. Thanks to the sparsity, during the view synthesis, only a small mount of pixels are needed, leading to much higher computational performance ($100\sim 1000\times$ faster). And the 2D image can be recovered from the gradients via solving a Poisson equation with linear computation complexity. Several experiments are performed to confirm the sparseness of the gradients and the computation performance of the proposed method. The method can be applied various applications, such as human body modeling and indoor environment modeling.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅方法正在流行。然而，它们直接作用于信号，导致信号的密集表示。即使采用修剪或蒸馏等技术，结果依然较为浓密。本文拟建模原始信号的梯度。梯度比原始信号稀疏得多。因此，梯度使用的高斯散块大幅减少，从而实现更高效的存储，从而在训练和渲染过程中获得更高的计算性能。由于稀疏性，在视图合成过程中只需少量像素挂载，从而实现更高的计算性能（100美元\模拟1000美元\更快时间美元）。通过解一个具有线性计算复杂度的泊松方程，可以从梯度中恢复二维图像。进行了多项实验以确认梯度的稀疏性及所提方法的计算性能。该方法可应用于人体建模和室内环境建模等多种应用。  


## April 2024

### [1] RTG-SLAM: Real-time 3D Reconstruction at Scale using Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-30（更新：2024-05-09）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhexi Peng, Tianjia Shao, Yong Liu et al.  
- **📝说明**：To be published in ACM SIGGRAPH 2024  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19706v3)    
- **📝摘要**：We present Real-time Gaussian SLAM (RTG-SLAM), a real-time 3D reconstruction system with an RGBD camera for large-scale environments using Gaussian splatting. The system features a compact Gaussian representation and a highly efficient on-the-fly Gaussian optimization scheme. We force each Gaussian to be either opaque or nearly transparent, with the opaque ones fitting the surface and dominant colors, and transparent ones fitting residual colors. By rendering depth in a different way from color rendering, we let a single opaque Gaussian well fit a local surface region without the need of multiple overlapping Gaussians, hence largely reducing the memory and computation cost. For on-the-fly Gaussian optimization, we explicitly add Gaussians for three types of pixels per frame: newly observed, with large color errors, and with large depth errors. We also categorize all Gaussians into stable and unstable ones, where the stable Gaussians are expected to well fit previously observed RGBD images and otherwise unstable. We only optimize the unstable Gaussians and only render the pixels occupied by unstable Gaussians. In this way, both the number of Gaussians to be optimized and pixels to be rendered are largely reduced, and the optimization can be done in real time. We show real-time reconstructions of a variety of large scenes. Compared with the state-of-the-art NeRF-based RGBD SLAM, our system achieves comparable high-quality reconstruction but with around twice the speed and half the memory cost, and shows superior performance in the realism of novel view synthesis and camera tracking accuracy.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍实时高斯SLAM（RTG-SLAM），这是一种基于RGBD摄像机的实时3D重建系统，适用于使用高斯喷涂的大型环境。该系统具有紧凑的高斯表示和高效的即时高斯优化方案。我们强制每个高斯图要么是不透明的，要么是近乎透明的，不透明的高斯图适配表面和主色，透明高斯图则是残余色。通过以不同于色彩渲染的方式渲染深度，我们使单个不透明高斯图能够很好地拟合局部表面区域，而无需多个重叠的高斯，从而大大降低了内存和计算成本。对于即时高斯优化，我们为每帧的三种像素类型显式添加高斯分布：新观测像素、大色彩误差和大深度误差。我们还将所有高斯分布量分为稳定高斯和不稳定，其中稳定高斯分布图预期能很好地拟合先前观测到的RGBD图像，否则则是不稳定的高斯分布。我们只优化不稳定的高斯，只渲染被不稳定高斯占据的像素。这样，需要优化的高斯分布量和渲染的像素数量大幅减少，优化可以实时完成。我们展示了各种大型场景的实时重建。与最先进的基于NeRF的RGBD SLAM相比，我们的系统实现了相当的高质量重建，但速度约是其两倍，内存成本仅为一半，并且在新颖的视角合成和摄像机跟踪精度的真实性方面表现出优越性能。  

### [2] SAGS: Structure-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-29  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Evangelos Ververas, Rolandos Alexandros Potamias, Jifei Song, Jiankang Deng, Stefanos Zafeiriou  
- **📝说明**：15 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19149v1)   · [Project](https://eververas.github.io/SAGS/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Following the advent of NeRFs, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) has paved the way to real-time neural rendering overcoming the computational burden of volumetric methods. Following the pioneering work of 3D-GS, several methods have attempted to achieve compressible and high-fidelity performance alternatives. However, by employing a geometry-agnostic optimization scheme, these methods neglect the inherent 3D structure of the scene, thereby restricting the expressivity and the quality of the representation, resulting in various floating points and artifacts. In this work, we propose a structure-aware Gaussian Splatting method (SAGS) that implicitly encodes the geometry of the scene, which reflects to state-of-the-art rendering performance and reduced storage requirements on benchmark novel-view synthesis datasets. SAGS is founded on a local-global graph representation that facilitates the learning of complex scenes and enforces meaningful point displacements that preserve the scene's geometry. Additionally, we introduce a lightweight version of SAGS, using a simple yet effective mid-point interpolation scheme, which showcases a compact representation of the scene with up to 24$\times$ size reduction without the reliance on any compression strategies. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate the superiority of SAGS compared to state-of-the-art 3D-GS methods under both rendering quality and model size. Besides, we demonstrate that our structure-aware method can effectively mitigate floating artifacts and irregular distortions of previous methods while obtaining precise depth maps. Project page https://eververas.github.io/SAGS/.  
- **📝翻译**: 随着NeRF的出现，三维高斯喷射（3D-GS）为实时神经渲染开辟了道路，克服了体积方法的计算负担。继3D-GS的开创性工作之后，多种方法尝试实现可压缩和高保真的性能替代方案。然而，通过采用几何无关的优化方案，这些方法忽视了场景固有的三维结构，从而限制了表现力和表现质量，导致各种浮点和伪影。本研究提出一种结构感知高斯喷溅法（SAGS），隐式编码场景几何体，反映出最先进的渲染性能和基准新视角综合数据集的存储需求。SAGS基于局部-全局图表示，便于学习复杂场景，并强制执行有意义的点位移，从而保持场景的几何形状。此外，我们还引入了轻量级SAGS版本，采用简单但有效的中点插值方案，展示了场景的紧凑表现，最大可减少24美元\倍数的尺寸，无需依赖任何压缩策略。跨多个基准数据集的广泛实验证明，SAGS在渲染质量和模型尺寸方面优于最先进的3D-GS方法。此外，我们证明了结构感知方法能够有效减少漂浮伪影和先前方法的不规则畸变，同时获得精确的深度图。项目页面 https://eververas.github.io/SAGS/。  

### [3] GSTalker: Real-time Audio-Driven Talking Face Generation via Deformable Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-29  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Bo Chen, Shoukang Hu, Qi Chen et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19040v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present GStalker, a 3D audio-driven talking face generation model with Gaussian Splatting for both fast training (40 minutes) and real-time rendering (125 FPS) with a 3$\sim$5 minute video for training material, in comparison with previous 2D and 3D NeRF-based modeling frameworks which require hours of training and seconds of rendering per frame. Specifically, GSTalker learns an audio-driven Gaussian deformation field to translate and transform 3D Gaussians to synchronize with audio information, in which multi-resolution hashing grid-based tri-plane and temporal smooth module are incorporated to learn accurate deformation for fine-grained facial details. In addition, a pose-conditioned deformation field is designed to model the stabilized torso. To enable efficient optimization of the condition Gaussian deformation field, we initialize 3D Gaussians by learning a coarse static Gaussian representation. Extensive experiments in person-specific videos with audio tracks validate that GSTalker can generate high-fidelity and audio-lips synchronized results with fast training and real-time rendering speed.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍GStalker，一个基于高斯Splatting的高斯语音面部生成模型，支持快速训练（40分钟）和实时渲染（125帧每秒），并配有3美元\模拟币5分钟的训练视频，与之前需要数小时训练和每帧数秒渲染的2D和3D基于NeRF的建模框架形成鲜明对比。具体来说，GSTalker 学习音频驱动的高斯变形场，将三维高斯变形映射与音频信息同步，其中集成了多分辨率哈希基于网格的三平面和时间光滑模块，以学习细粒度面部细节的精确变形。此外，设计了一个姿态条件变形场以模拟稳定躯干。为了高效优化高斯变形场条件，我们通过学习粗静态高斯表示来初始化三维高斯分布。对针对特定人物的音频轨视频的广泛实验验证了GSTalker能够以快速训练和实时渲染速度生成高保真和音频同步的结果。  

### [4] CLIP-GS: CLIP-Informed Gaussian Splatting for View-Consistent 3D Indoor Semantic Understanding  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-22（更新：2025-06-23）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Guibiao Liao, Jiankun Li, Zhenyu Bao et al.  
- **📝说明**：ACM TOMM 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14249v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Exploiting 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with Contrastive Language-Image Pre-Training (CLIP) models for open-vocabulary 3D semantic understanding of indoor scenes has emerged as an attractive research focus. Existing methods typically attach high-dimensional CLIP semantic embeddings to 3D Gaussians and leverage view-inconsistent 2D CLIP semantics as Gaussian supervision, resulting in efficiency bottlenecks and deficient 3D semantic consistency. To address these challenges, we present CLIP-GS, efficiently achieving a coherent semantic understanding of 3D indoor scenes via the proposed Semantic Attribute Compactness (SAC) and 3D Coherent Regularization (3DCR). SAC approach exploits the naturally unified semantics within objects to learn compact, yet effective, semantic Gaussian representations, enabling highly efficient rendering (>100 FPS). 3DCR enforces semantic consistency in 2D and 3D domains: In 2D, 3DCR utilizes refined view-consistent semantic outcomes derived from 3DGS to establish cross-view coherence constraints; in 3D, 3DCR encourages features similar among 3D Gaussian primitives associated with the same object, leading to more precise and coherent segmentation results. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our method remarkably suppresses existing state-of-the-art approaches, achieving mIoU improvements of 21.20% and 13.05% on ScanNet and Replica datasets, respectively, while maintaining real-time rendering speed. Furthermore, our approach exhibits superior performance even with sparse input data, substantiating its robustness.  
- **📝翻译**: 利用3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）结合对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）模型，实现室内场景的开放词汇3D语义理解，已成为一个有吸引力的研究方向。现有方法通常将高维CLIP语义嵌入附加到三维高斯图上，并利用视图不一致的二维CLIP语义作为高斯监督，导致效率瓶颈和三维语义一致性不足。为应对这些挑战，我们介绍了CLIP-GS，通过提出的语义属性紧凑性（SAC）和三维相干正则化（3DCR）高效实现三维室内场景的连贯语义理解。SAC方法利用对象内自然统一的语义，学习紧凑但有效的语义高斯表示，实现高效渲染（>100帧每秒）。3DCR在二维和三维领域强制执行语义一致性：在二维领域，3DCR利用从3DGS导出的精炼视图一致语义结果，建立交叉视野一致性约束;在三维中，3DCR鼓励与同一对象相关的三维高斯原语表现出相似特征，从而获得更精确和连贯的分割结果。大量实验结果表明，我们的方法显著抑制了现有最先进方法，在ScanNet和Replica数据集上分别实现了21.20%和13.05%的mIoU提升，同时保持了实时渲染速度。此外，我们的方法即使在输入数据稀少的情况下也表现出优异性能，证明了其鲁棒性。  

### [5] EfficientGS: Streamlining Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale High-Resolution Scene Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-19  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Wenkai Liu, Tao Guan, Bin Zhu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12777v1)    
- **📝摘要**：In the domain of 3D scene representation, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a pivotal technology. However, its application to large-scale, high-resolution scenes (exceeding 4k$\times$4k pixels) is hindered by the excessive computational requirements for managing a large number of Gaussians. Addressing this, we introduce 'EfficientGS', an advanced approach that optimizes 3DGS for high-resolution, large-scale scenes. We analyze the densification process in 3DGS and identify areas of Gaussian over-proliferation. We propose a selective strategy, limiting Gaussian increase to key primitives, thereby enhancing the representational efficiency. Additionally, we develop a pruning mechanism to remove redundant Gaussians, those that are merely auxiliary to adjacent ones. For further enhancement, we integrate a sparse order increment for Spherical Harmonics (SH), designed to alleviate storage constraints and reduce training overhead. Our empirical evaluations, conducted on a range of datasets including extensive 4K+ aerial images, demonstrate that 'EfficientGS' not only expedites training and rendering times but also achieves this with a model size approximately tenfold smaller than conventional 3DGS while maintaining high rendering fidelity.  
- **📝翻译**: 在三维场景表示领域，三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一项关键技术。然而，其在大尺度高分辨率场景（超过4k$\times$4k像素）中的应用受到管理大量高斯高斯图计算需求的限制。针对此，我们介绍了“EfficientGS”，一种优化3DGS以适应高分辨率、大尺度场景的方法。我们分析了3DGS中的致密过程，并识别出高斯过度扩散的区域。我们提出了一种选择性策略，将高斯增加限制在关键原节点，从而提升表示效率。此外，我们开发了一种修剪机制，用以去除冗余的高斯分布，即仅为相邻高斯的辅助。为了进一步增强，我们集成了球谐（SH）的稀疏阶增量，旨在缓解存储限制并减少训练开销。我们对包括大量4K+航拍图像在内的多种数据集进行的实证评估表明，“EfficientGS”不仅加快了训练和渲染时间，还能以约十倍规模的模型规模实现这一目标，同时保持高渲染精度。  

### [6] Application of 3D Gaussian Splatting for Cinematic Anatomy on Consumer Class Devices  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-17（更新：2024-06-25）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Simon Niedermayr, Christoph Neuhauser, Kaloian Petkov, Klaus Engel, Rüdiger Westermann  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11285v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Interactive photorealistic rendering of 3D anatomy is used in medical education to explain the structure of the human body. It is currently restricted to frontal teaching scenarios, where even with a powerful GPU and high-speed access to a large storage device where the data set is hosted, interactive demonstrations can hardly be achieved. We present the use of novel view synthesis via compressed 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to overcome this restriction, and to even enable students to perform cinematic anatomy on lightweight and mobile devices. Our proposed pipeline first finds a set of camera poses that captures all potentially seen structures in the data. High-quality images are then generated with path tracing and converted into a compact 3DGS representation, consuming < 70 MB even for data sets of multiple GBs. This allows for real-time photorealistic novel view synthesis that recovers structures up to the voxel resolution and is almost indistinguishable from the path-traced images  
- **📝翻译**: 三维解剖的互动式写实渲染被用于医学教育中解释人体结构。目前它仅限于正面教学场景，即使配备了强大的GPU和高速访问存放数据集的大型存储设备，互动演示也很难实现。我们展示了通过压缩三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）进行新颖视角合成，以克服这一限制，甚至使学生能够在轻量级和移动设备上进行电影解剖。我们提出的流水线首先找到一组摄像机姿态，捕捉数据中所有可能可见的结构。随后通过路径追踪生成高质量图像，并转换为紧凑的3DGS表示，即使是多GB数据集，也占用<70 MB。这允许实时写实的新视角合成，恢复结构至体素分辨率，且几乎与路径追踪图像无异  

### [7] 3D Gaussian Splatting as Markov Chain Monte Carlo  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-15（更新：2025-02-12）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shakiba Kheradmand, Daniel Rebain, Gopal Sharma et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09591v3)    
- **📝摘要**：While 3D Gaussian Splatting has recently become popular for neural rendering, current methods rely on carefully engineered cloning and splitting strategies for placing Gaussians, which can lead to poor-quality renderings, and reliance on a good initialization. In this work, we rethink the set of 3D Gaussians as a random sample drawn from an underlying probability distribution describing the physical representation of the scene-in other words, Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) samples. Under this view, we show that the 3D Gaussian updates can be converted as Stochastic Gradient Langevin Dynamics (SGLD) updates by simply introducing noise. We then rewrite the densification and pruning strategies in 3D Gaussian Splatting as simply a deterministic state transition of MCMC samples, removing these heuristics from the framework. To do so, we revise the 'cloning' of Gaussians into a relocalization scheme that approximately preserves sample probability. To encourage efficient use of Gaussians, we introduce a regularizer that promotes the removal of unused Gaussians. On various standard evaluation scenes, we show that our method provides improved rendering quality, easy control over the number of Gaussians, and robustness to initialization.  
- **📝翻译**: 虽然3D高斯喷涂最近在神经渲染中变得流行，但当前方法依赖于精心设计的高斯克隆和分割策略，这可能导致渲染质量较差，且依赖良好的初始化。在本研究中，我们重新思考三维高斯集合作为从描述场景物理表示的底层概率分布中随机抽取的样本——换句话说，就是马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）样本。根据这种观点，我们证明了三维高斯更新可以通过引入噪声转换为随机梯度朗之文动力学（SGLD）更新。随后，我们将三维高斯喷溅中的密度化和剪枝策略重写为MCMC样本的确定性状态转变，去除这些启发式。为此，我们将高斯分布的“克隆”修正为一个近似保持样本概率的重定位方案。为了鼓励高效使用高斯分布，我们引入了一种正则化器，促进去除未使用的高斯分布。在多个标准评估场景中，我们证明了该方法提升了渲染质量，易于控制高斯数量，并且具有初始化的鲁棒性。  

### [8] CompGS: Efficient 3D Scene Representation via Compressed Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-15  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xiangrui Liu, Xinju Wu, Pingping Zhang et al.  
- **📝说明**：Submitted to a conference  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09458v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Gaussian splatting, renowned for its exceptional rendering quality and efficiency, has emerged as a prominent technique in 3D scene representation. However, the substantial data volume of Gaussian splatting impedes its practical utility in real-world applications. Herein, we propose an efficient 3D scene representation, named Compressed Gaussian Splatting (CompGS), which harnesses compact Gaussian primitives for faithful 3D scene modeling with a remarkably reduced data size. To ensure the compactness of Gaussian primitives, we devise a hybrid primitive structure that captures predictive relationships between each other. Then, we exploit a small set of anchor primitives for prediction, allowing the majority of primitives to be encapsulated into highly compact residual forms. Moreover, we develop a rate-constrained optimization scheme to eliminate redundancies within such hybrid primitives, steering our CompGS towards an optimal trade-off between bitrate consumption and representation efficacy. Experimental results show that the proposed CompGS significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving superior compactness in 3D scene representation without compromising model accuracy and rendering quality. Our code will be released on GitHub for further research.  
- **📝翻译**: 高斯喷溅以其卓越的渲染质量和效率著称，已成为3D场景表现中的一种重要技术。然而，高斯喷溅的庞大数据量限制了其在实际应用中的实用性。本文提出一种高效的三维场景表示，称为压缩高斯喷溅（Compressed Gaussian Splatting，简称CompGS），它利用紧致的高斯原语实现忠实的三维场景建模，同时数据量显著减少。为确保高斯原的紧致性，我们设计了一种混合原始结构，捕捉彼此间的预测关系。然后，我们利用一小部分锚点原语进行预测，使大多数原语能够封装为高度紧致的残差形式。此外，我们开发了一种速率约束优化方案，以消除此类混合原语中的冗余，引导我们的CompGS在比特率消耗与表示效能之间实现最佳权衡。实验结果显示，所提出的CompGS显著优于现有方法，在三维场景表示中实现了卓越的紧凑性，同时不影响模型的准确性和渲染质量。我们的代码将发布在GitHub上，供进一步研究。  

### [9] End-to-End Rate-Distortion Optimized 3D Gaussian Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-09（更新：2024-10-21）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Henan Wang, Hanxin Zhu, Tianyu He et al.  
- **📝说明**：ECCV 2024  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01597v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become an emerging technique with remarkable potential in 3D representation and image rendering. However, the substantial storage overhead of 3DGS significantly impedes its practical applications. In this work, we formulate the compact 3D Gaussian learning as an end-to-end Rate-Distortion Optimization (RDO) problem and propose RDO-Gaussian that can achieve flexible and continuous rate control. RDO-Gaussian addresses two main issues that exist in current schemes: 1) Different from prior endeavors that minimize the rate under the fixed distortion, we introduce dynamic pruning and entropy-constrained vector quantization (ECVQ) that optimize the rate and distortion at the same time. 2) Previous works treat the colors of each Gaussian equally, while we model the colors of different regions and materials with learnable numbers of parameters. We verify our method on both real and synthetic scenes, showcasing that RDO-Gaussian greatly reduces the size of 3D Gaussian over 40x, and surpasses existing methods in rate-distortion performance.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种新兴技术，在三维表示和图像渲染中具有显著潜力。然而，3DGS庞大的存储开销严重阻碍了其实际应用。在本研究中，我们将紧凑的三维高斯学习表述为端到端速率失真优化（RDO）问题，并提出了能够实现灵活连续速率控制的RDO-高斯方案。RDO-高斯分布解决了当前方案中存在的两个主要问题：1）与以往在固定畸变条件下最小化速率的尝试不同，我们引入了动态剪枝和熵约束向量量化（ECVQ），以同时优化速率和失真。2）以往的工作对每种高斯分布的颜色一视同仁，而我们则用可学习的参数数量来建模不同区域和材料的颜色。我们在真实和合成场景中验证了我们的方法，展示了RDO-高斯在3D高斯尺寸上大幅缩小了40倍以上，并且在速率失真性能上超越了现有方法。  

### [10] Revising Densification in Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-09  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Samuel Rota Bulò, Lorenzo Porzi, Peter Kontschieder  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06109v1)    
- **📝摘要**：In this paper, we address the limitations of Adaptive Density Control (ADC) in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), a scene representation method achieving high-quality, photorealistic results for novel view synthesis. ADC has been introduced for automatic 3D point primitive management, controlling densification and pruning, however, with certain limitations in the densification logic. Our main contribution is a more principled, pixel-error driven formulation for density control in 3DGS, leveraging an auxiliary, per-pixel error function as the criterion for densification. We further introduce a mechanism to control the total number of primitives generated per scene and correct a bias in the current opacity handling strategy of ADC during cloning operations. Our approach leads to consistent quality improvements across a variety of benchmark scenes, without sacrificing the method's efficiency.  
- **📝翻译**: 本文探讨了自适应密度控制（ADC）在3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）中存在的局限性，这是一种场景表示方法，能够实现高质量、逼真的新视角合成结果。ADC已被引入用于自动三维点基元管理，控制密度化和修剪，但密度化逻辑存在一定限制。我们的主要贡献是采用更为原则性的像素误差驱动3DGS密度控制公式，利用辅助的每像素误差函数作为密度化的标准。我们还进一步引入了一种机制，用于控制每个场景生成的图元总数，并纠正ADC在克隆作中当前不透明度处理策略中的偏见。我们的方法在多个基准场景中实现了持续的质量提升，同时不牺牲方法的效率。
